# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Sotho translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

Molaotheo Oa Zimbabwe Phetolo (No. 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

MOLAO O tlosang empa o kenyang Molaotheo oa Zimbabwe. O ENTSOENG le Palamente ke Mookameli ea Zimbabwe. 1 5

Sehlooho se sekhutsoane

Molao ona o ka ntse oa bitsoa hore ke Molaotheo oa Zimbabwe Phetolo (No. 20) Molao 2013. 2

Hlaloso Molaong oo– “Molaotheo o le teng” o ra hore Molaotheo oa Zimbabwe oa pele o qalileng ho sebetsa ka li 18 Mesa 1980, o fetotsoeng kamoraho ha mong; “Molaotheo o mocha”, ho ra hore Molaotheo oa Zimbabwe o beiloeng ho lenaneo le.


Ho tlosoa ha Molaotheo o le teng ke Molaotheo o mocha

(1) Ho lateloa karoloana (2), Molaotheo o le teng o tlosoa ebile o nkeloa sebaka ke Molaotheo o mocha.

(2) Hore ho seke ha ba le pelaelo, ho ile ha boleloa hore—

(a)

Molaotheo o mocha o beiloe “ka letsatsi la phatlalatso” joale ka ha ho hlalositsoe ho lenaneo la bo tsilela la Molaotheo oo, mo ho ra hore, ka Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

letsatsi leo molao oo o ngoliloeng hoGazetteho khaolo 51 (5) ea Molaotheo o le teng; le

(b) ho tloha letsatsing la ho beoa ea ha Molaotheo o mocha joale ka ha ho hlalositsoe ho temana (a), Molaotheo o le teng o ntse o sebetsa joale ka tse li neiloeng ho Lenaneo la tsilela la Molaotheo o mocha.

## Section 33

Maikemisetso a etellang lihlopha tsa sechaba le Hurumente. Mmuso o lokileng. Bongoe ba sechaba, khotso le boiketlo. Ho hlokomela litokelo le litokoloho. Leano la ho sebelisana le mangoe mafatse. Tsoelopele ea sechaba. Matlafatso le ho tsoeletsa mesebetsi. Ho fumaneha ha lijo. Setso. Tekano ea bong. Kemelo e lekanetseng ea setereke. Bana. Ba bacha. Ba tsofetseng. Batho ba nang le bokooa/ba holahetsing. Bahlabane ba Ntoa ea Tokoloho. Mesebetsi lekamano mesebetsing. Tsireletso ea malapa. Lenyalo. Thuto. Bolulo. Litsebeletso tsa Bophelo. Boiketlo ba sechaba. Thuso ho e leng molaong. Meaho ea Lipapali le boithabiso. Ho boloka tsebo ea setso.

Karolo

## Section 34

Ho sebelisa melao ea mafatse. KHAOLO 3 Boahi

## Section 43

Boahi ba Zimbabwe. Boahi ka tsoalo. Boahi ka leloko. Boahi ka ho ngolisa. Ho nkeloa boahi. Ho boloka boahi lemo o le lenyalong kapa lenyalo le felile. Boto ea boahi le ho etelana. Matla a Palamente ka tsa boahi. Ho tsoelapele le ho tsoseletsa boahi bo e leng ba bateng. KHAOLO 4 Hlaloso ea Litokelo KAROLO 1 Tsebeliso Le Hlaloso ea Khaolo 4

## Section 47

Molato oa ho hlompha litokelo le tokoloho tsa batho tsa bohlokoa. Tsebeliso ea Khaolo 4. Hlaloso ea Khaolo 4. Khaolo 4 ha e thibele ho ba le litokelo tse lingoe. KAROLO 2 Litokelo Tsa Batho Le Litokoloho Tsa Bohlokoa

## Section 68

Tokelo ea bophelo. Tokelo ea tokoloho ea motho. Litokelo tsa batho ba ba tsoeroeng le ba bo toronkong. Litokelo ho seriti sa botho. Tokelo ea tsireletso ea batho. Tokoloho tsoaro e bohloko kapa ea sehloho, ho nyatsoa le kotlo. Tokoloho bokhobeng le ba makhoba. Bolokolohi ba ho qobelloa kapa bolokolohi ba ho tsotsometsoa. Tekano le ho se khetholle. Tokoloho ea ho ba le lekunutu. Bolokolohi ba kopano le botsoalle. Bolokolohi ba ho bontsa le ho nea lipelaelo. Bolokolohi ba letsoalo. Bolokolohi ba ho bua le tokoloho ea phatlalatso ea litaba. Ho fumana boitsebiso. Puo le setso. Tokoloho ea botsebi, khoebo kapa mosebetsi. Litokelo tsa mosebetsi. Tokoloho ea ho tsamaea le ho lula. Litokelo tsa lipolotiki. Tokelo ea horeroa ha melato.

Karolo

## Section 78

Tokelo ea ho ba le tsekiso e tsoanetseng. Litokelo tsa batho ba roesoang melato. Tokelo ea thepa. Litokelo tsa mobu oa temo. Tokelo ea tikoloho. Bolokolohi ba ho lelekoa bolulong ka sekhahla. Tokelo ea Thuto. Tokelo ea ho fumana bophelo bo bontle. Tokelo ea ho fumana lijo le metsi. Litokelo tsa lenyalo. KAROLO 3 Hlaloso ea Litokelo Le Tse Lingoe

## Section 85

Ho hlahisa litokelo le litokoloho tsa batho tsa bohlokoa. KAROLO 5 Ho Thibela Litokelo Le Litokoloho Tsa Batho Tsa Bohlokoa

## Section 87

Ho thibela litokelo le tokoloho. Ho thibeloa ka nako ea tsohanyetso ea sechaba. KHAOLO 5 Baeteleli Ba Mmuso KAROLO 1 Matla a Baeteleli Ba Mmuso

Matla a baeteleli ba Mmuso. KAROLO 2 Mookameli Le Batlatsi Ba Mookameli

## Section 94

Mookameli. Mesebetsi ea Mookameli. Litsoanelo tsa ho khetho ea ha Mookameli le Motlatsi oa Mookameli. Lekhetho la Mookameli le Batlatsi ba Mookameli. Ngangisano ea khetho ea Mookameli. Ho qala mosebetsi ha Mookameli le Batlatsi ba Mookameli.

Karolo

Nako e beiloeng ea ho sebetsa ha Mookameli.

Ho se finyelelehi ha Mookameli ho litsekiso.

## Section 99

Mesebetsi ea Batlatsi ba Mookameli.

## Section 101 — Ho siea setulo ka lebaka la lefu, ho e tokolla mosebetsing kapa ho se khone ha

Mookameli kapa Motlatsi oa Mookameli.

## Section 102

Moputso oa Mookameli le Motlatsi oa Mookameli.

## Section 103

Mookameli le Motlatsi oa Mookameli le ba ba litulong tsena kileng ba etsa mesebetsi e tsoanang ha ba na tsoanelo ea ho etsa e mingoe mesebetsi kapa ho hiroa. KAROLO 3 Ma Minista, Batlasi Ba Ma Minista Le Kabinete

## Section 104

## Section 108

## Section 109

Ho khetho ea ha maMinista le Batlatsi maMinista. Kabinete. Boitsoaro batlatsi ba Mookameli, maMinista le Batlatsi ba maMinista. Boikarabelo ba Batlatsi ba Mookameli, maMinista le Batlatsi ba maMinista. Nako e beiloeng ea ho ba bolulong ba boMinista le Batlatsi ba maMinista. Khetho ea ho se tsepi Mmuso oa Hurumente. KAROLO 4 Misebetsi ea Baeteleli

## Section 110

## Section 111

## Section 112

## Section 113

Mesebetsi e lebaneng le Mookameli le Kabinete. Ntoa le khotso. Matla a mohau oa ho tsoarela. Maemo a tsohanyetso ea sechaba. KAROLO 5 Mookameli oa Molao

## Section 114

## Section 115

Mookameli oa Molao. Ho tlosoa setulong ha Mookameli oa Molao. KHAOLO 6 Molao KAROLO 1 Matla a Ba Molao

## Section 116

Molao. Boemo le tekanyo ea matla a ba Molao.

KAROLO 2 Palamente Karolo

## Section 118

Khotla ea Palamente. Mosebetsi oa Khotla ea Palamente. KAROLO 3 Seneta

## Section 120

## Section 121

## Section 123

Sebopeho sa Seneta. Litsoanelo tsa ho khethoa le ho se tsoanele ho khethoa ho ba Seneta. Mookameli oa Seneta. Motlatsi oa Mookameli oa Seneta. KAROLO 4 Lekhotla la Sechaba

## Section 124

## Section 125

## Section 126

## Section 127

Maloko a bopang lekhotla la Sechaba. Litsoanelo tsa ho khetheloa le ho sa khetheloa ho ba Lekhotleng ea Sechaba. Sebui ho Lekhotla la Sechaba. Motlatsi oa Sebui Lekhotleng la Sechaba. KAROLO 5 Nako e Beiloeng Ea Ho Lula Setulong Ha Ba Batlatsi Ba Palamente

## Section 128

## Section 129

Boikano ba Maloko a Palamente. Nako e beiloeng ea ho lula setulong ha Leloka la Palamente. KAROLO 6 Litaba Tsa Molao Le Matla a Mangoe

## Section 130

## Section 131

## Section 132

## Section 133

## Section 134

Matla le mesebetsi ea Seneta le Kopano ea Sechaba. Melao ea Palamente le tsebeliso ea oona. Ho qala ha ho sebelisoa Melao ea Palamente. Ho ngoloa ha Melao ea Palamente. Molao o tsehetsang. KAROLO 7 Tsamaiso ea Palamente

## Section 135

## Section 136

## Section 137

## Section 138

## Section 139

## Section 140

## Section 141

## Section 142

Hlooho ea Khotla la Palamente. Batho ba e telelang mosebetsi ho khotla ea Palamente. Palo ea maloko a Palamente a tsoanetseng ho tsamaisa mosebetsi ho khotla ea Palamente. Ho khetha le tokelo ea ho mameloa ke Palamente. Taolo etse Emisitsoeng. Lipuo tsa Mookameli ho khotla ea Palamente. Boikano ba sechaba ho tsamaiso ea Palamente. Tiiso ea ho tsamaisa tsa Palamente.

Karolo

## Section 143

## Section 145

## Section 146

## Section 147 — KAROLO 8

Sebaka, Ho Phatlalatsoa Le Mekopano Ea Palamente Nako e beiloeng ea ho sebetsa le ho qhaqhoa ha ea Phalamente. Likhetho tse akaretsang sechaba sohle ho bakeloa ke ho qhaqhoa ha Palamente. Ho qala ho sebetsa Palamente moraho ha khetho e akaretsang sechaba sohle. Nako ea ho sebetsa le ea ho phomola. Ho emisoa ha tsa melao, lipono le tse lingoe tse li tsoanetsing ho etsoa ka nako ea ho qhaqhoa ha Palamente. KAROLO 9 Tse Lingoe Tse Tsamaelanang Le Tsa Khotla ea Palamente

## Section 148

## Section 150

## Section 151

## Section 152

## Section 153

## Section 154

Litokelo le ho sa fihleleha ho tsa tsekiso. Tokelo ea ho kupa maloka a Palamente. Mo ho etsoang mesebetsi ea khotla ea Palamente. Komiti ea Mela le etse Emisitsoeng. Komiti ea Molao ea khotla ea Palamente. Moputso oa Mookameli oa Seneta, Sebui le Maloko a Palamente. Mongoli oa Palamente le bangoe basebeletsi. KHOALO 7 Khetho KAROLO 1 Melao Ea Tsamaiso Ea Khetho

## Section 155

## Section 156

## Section 157

Melao ea tsammaiso ea khetho. Melao ea khetho le ea ho batla lipono tsa sechaba. Molao oa Khetho. KAROLO 2 Nako Ea Khetho

## Section 158

## Section 159

Nako ea khetho. Ho tlatsoa ha likheo litulong tsa lekhetho. KAROLO 3 Ho Khaoloa Ha Mekhano Ea Khetho

## Section 160

Palo ea li khonstichuoensi le li-oati. Ho khaoloa ha mekhano ea khetho. KHAOLO 8 Boahloli Le Makhotla KAROLO 1

Bolaoli ba boahloli. Boahloli. Boipuso ba boahloli.

Matla a khotla ea Molaotheo, Lekhotla la Holimo-limo, Lekhotla la Holimo. KAROLO 2 Ho Hiroa Ha Baahloli le Nako e Beiloeng ea Hore ba be Mosebetsing Karolo

## Section 177

## Section 188

Litsoanelo tsa mosebetsi oa Moahloli oa Lekhotla la Molaotheo. Tse hlokahalang hore motho a be moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo-limo. Tse hlokahalang hore motho a be moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo, Lekhotla la Mesebetsi, Lekhotla la tse etsang ka Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsi. Ho hiroa ha baahloli. Ho hiroa ha Batlatsi ba baahloli. Ho Hiroa ha ma mastrata le mangoe maloko a lisebeletsi tsa molao. Ba sebetsang ho tsamaiso ea Molao a ba tsoanele ho khetheloa ho sebetsa ho khotla e fetang e ngoe. Ho hiroa ha basebeletsi ba Molao ho tsoanetsi ho bontsa boemo ba sechaba. Boikano. Nako e neiloeng ea ho sebetsa ha baahloli. Ho tlosoa ha baahloli bolulong. Likemiso tsa mosebetsi le boemo ba mosebetsi oa maloko a baahloli. KAROLO 3 Komeshene Ea Balaoli

## Section 189

## Section 190

## Section 191

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Balaoli ba Molao. Mesebetsi oa Komeshene ea Balaoli. Ho se khethi le ho hlaka ha mekhoa ea Tsebetso ea Komiti ea Boahloli. KAROLO 4 Tse Akaretsang Tsohle

## Section 192

## Section 193

Molao o tla sebelisoa. Mokhano oa Matla a makhotla ho tseko ea melato. KHAOLO 9 Maitseho a Tsamaiso Le Ketello ea Sechaba

## Section 194

## Section 195

## Section 196

Melao ea motheo le maitseho e laolang tsamaiso ea sechaba. Mekhatlo ea khoebo e laoloang ke Mmuso. Boikarabelo ba basebeletsi ba sechaba le maitseho a boetapele.

Karolo

## Section 197

Boemo ba lihlooho tsa lihlopha tse laoloang ke Mmuso.

## Section 198

Taelo ea ho tiisa Khaolo ea 9. KHAOLO 10 Tsebeletso Ea Sechaba

## Section 199

## Section 202

## Section 203

## Section 204

## Section 205

Tsebeletso ea Sechaba. Boitsoaro ba Basebeletsi ba Sechaba. MoMinista a ikarabelang bakeng sa Tsebeletso ea Sechaba. Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Sechaba. Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Sechaba. Baemeli ba Zimbabwe mafatseng a mangoe. Bangoli ba Baholo. KHAOLO 11 Litsebeletso Tsa Tsireletso KAROLO 1 Likamano Tse Tloalehileng

## Section 206

## Section 207

## Section 208

## Section 209

## Section 210

Polokeho ea Sechaba. Litsebeletso tsa tsireletso. Boitsoaro ba lisebeletsi tsa tsireletso. Lekhotla la Tsireletso la Sechaba. Mekhoa e ikemetseng ea litletlebo. KAROLO 2 Masole a Tsireletso

## Section 211

## Section 212

## Section 213

## Section 214

## Section 215

## Section 216

## Section 217

## Section 218

Masole a Tsireletso. Mosebetsi oa Masole a tsireletso. Tsela ea ho sebetsa ha Masole a tsireletso. Boikarabelo ba lipolitiki ho romeloeng ha Masole. MoMinista a ikarabelang pakeng sa Masole. Taolo ea Masole a Tsireletso. Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea Tsebeletso ea Tsireletso. Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea Tsebeletso ea Masole a tsireletso. KAROLO 3 Tsebeletso Ea Mapolesa

## Section 219

## Section 220

## Section 221

## Section 222

## Section 223

Tebeletso ea Mapolesa le mesebetsi ea eona. Minista a ikarabelang pakeng sa Mapolesa. Komeshene ea Molao oa Mapolesa. Kemisole pupo ea Komeshene ea Mapolesa. Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea Mapolesa.

KAROLO 4 Tsebeletso Ea Ba Bohlale/hlaselo Karolo

## Section 224

## Section 225

## Section 226

Kemiso ea Tsamaiso ea Bohloli. Minista a laolang tsebeliso ea bohlale le hlaselo. Taolo ea tsamaiso ea bohloli ba sechaba. KAROLO 5 Litsebeletso Tsa Litoronko Le Tokisiso

## Section 227

## Section 228

## Section 229

## Section 230

## Section 231

Tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokisiso. Minista a laolang tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokisiso. MoKomeshene a akaretsang oa tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokiso. Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokisiso. Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokisiso. KHAOLO 12 Litsamaiso Tse Li Ikemetseng Li Tsehetsa Temokrasi KAROLO 1 Tse Li Akaretsang

## Section 232

## Section 233

## Section 234

## Section 235

## Section 236

## Section 237

Likomeshene tse ikemetsing. Lipheo tsa Likomeshene tse ikemetseng. Basebeletsi ba Likomeshene tse ikemetseng. Boipuso ba Likomeshene. Maloka a Komeshene tse ikemetseng e seng tsa lipolitiki. Ho khetho ea le ho tlosoa mosebetseng ha maloka a Likomeshene tse e kemetseng. KAROLO Komeshene Ea Likhetho ea Zimbabwe

## Section 238

## Section 239

## Section 240

## Section 241

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea Likhetho ea Zimbabwe. Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea Likhetho ea Zimbabwe. Ho tlosoa setulong sa Komeshene ea Likhetho tsa Zimbabwe. Komeshene ea Likhetho tsa Zimbabwe e tla tsebisa ka tsa likhetho le lireferendamu. KAROLO 3 Komeshene Ea Litokelo Tsa Batho Ea Zimbabwe

## Section 242

## Section 243

## Section 244

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea litokelo tsa batho ea Zimbabwe. Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea Litokelo Tsa Batho ea Zimbabwe. Tlaleho ho Komeshene le tlaleho e tsoang ho Komeshene ea Litokelo Tsa Batho ea Zimbabwe.

KAROLO 4 Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea Tekano Karolo

## Section 245

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea Tekano ea Zimbabwe.

## Section 246

Mesebetsi oa Komeshene ea Tekano ea Zimbabwe.

## Section 247

Litlaleho tsa Komeshene ea Tekano ea Zimbabwe. KAROLO 5 Komeshene Ea Phatlalatso Ea Litaba Ea Zimbabwe

## Section 248

## Section 249

## Section 250

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea ho phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe. Mesebetsi ea Komeshene ea ho phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe. Litlaleho tsa Komeshene ea ho phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe. KAROLO 6 Komeshene Ea Tsa Khotso Le Ho Tsoarelana Ea Zimbabwe

## Section 251

## Section 252

## Section 253

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana. Mesebetsi ea Komeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana. Litlaleho tsa Komeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana. KHAOLO 13 Mekhatlo e Loang Le Bobolu Le Tlolo Ea Molao KAROLO 1 Komeshene e Loang Le Bobolu Le Tlolo Ea Molao Ea Zimbabwe

## Section 254

## Section 255

## Section 256

## Section 257

Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene e lebeletseng ka tsa bobolu ea Zimbabwe. Mesebetsi ea Komeshene e loanang le bobolu ea Zimbabwe. Tsebeliso ea tse ling tsa Khaolo 12 ho Komeshene e loang le bobolu ea Zimbabwe. Litlaleho tsa Komeshene e loang le bobolu ea Zimbabwe. KAROLO 2 Moahloli o Moholo Oa Sechaba

## Section 258

## Section 259

## Section 260

## Section 261

## Section 262

## Section 263

Kemiso le mesebetsi ea Moahloli o moholo oa sechaba. Moahloli o moholo oa sechaba le ba bang. Boekemeli ba Moahloli o moholo. Boitsoaro ba sebeletsi ea Mmuso ena le matla a ho ahlolela. Moahloli o moholo a akaretsang o tlaleha Palamente selemo le selemo. Matla a mang a ho ahlola.

KHAOLO 14 Hurumente Ea Liprofinse Le Malapa Tsimoloho KAROLO 1 Karolo

## Section 264

## Section 265

## Section 266 — Pele

Ho arohana matla ha Hurumente le maikarabelo. Melao e akaretsang ea hurumente ea liProfinse le malapa. Boitsoaro ba basebeletsi ba mebuso ea liProfinse le malapa. KAROLO 2 Likomiti Tsa Liprofinse Le Liprofinse Le Litoropo

## Section 267

## Section 268

## Section 269

## Section 270

## Section 271

## Section 272

## Section 273

Liprofinse le litereke tsa Zimbabwe. Likomiti tsa li Profinse. Likomiti tsa litoropo. Mesebetsi ea likomiti tsa liprofinse le litoropo. Likomiti tsa makhotla a liprofinse. Moeteleli oa likomiti tsa liprofinse. Litokisetso tse amanang le likomiti tsa profinse le tsa litoropo. KAROLO 3 Mmuso Oa Malapa

## Section 274

## Section 275

## Section 276

## Section 277

## Section 278

## Section 279

Ba na le matla litoropong le malapeng. Ba na le matla sepakeng sa mahae. Mesebetsi ea ba etelelang tsa malapa. Likhetho tsa baetapele ho tsa malapa. Ho ba setulong ha baetapele ba tsa malapeng. Tsamaiso ea tsa baetapele ba malapa. KHAOLO 15 Baetapele Ba Setso

## Section 287

Boetapele ba setso. Maitseho a tsoanetsing ho hlokomeloa ke baetapele ba setso. Mesebetsi ea baetapele ba setso. Ho khetho ea le ho tlosoa ha baetapele ba setso. Meputso le ho pateloa ha baetapele ba setso. Lekhotla la Sechaba le likopano tsa likhosi tsa Marena. Mesebetsi ea Lekhotla la Sechaba le likopano tsa likhosi tsa Marena. Komiti ea Botsepehi le Boitsoaro bo bolokileng. KHAOLO16 Mobu Oa Temo

## Section 289

## Section 290

## Section 291

Tsoelopele ea litokelo tsa baahi ho mobu oa temo.

Tsireletso ea mobu oa temo.

## Section 293

Ho nkeloa mobu oa temo ke Mmuso.

## Section 294

## Section 295

Ho pateloa pakeng sa ho fumana mobu oa temo .

Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea tsa Naha ea Zimbabwe. KHAOLO 17 Tsa Lichelete KAROLO 1 Ho Bolokoa Ha Chelete

## Section 301

Melao ea ho boloka lichelete tsa sechaba. Mosebetsi oa Palamente ho tsa lichelete tsa Mmuso tse li tsenang le tse li sebelisoang. Ho thibela likoloto tsa sechaba le tiiso ka tsa lefatse. Kabelo ea lichelete pakeng tsa likarolo tsa Mmuso, tsa liprofinse le tsa malapa. KAROLO 2 Chelete e Bolokiloeng Ke Sechaba

## Section 302

## Section 303

## Section 304

Chelete e bolokiloeng ke sechaba. Ho nka chelete e bolokiloeng ke sechaba. Leotlo la ho sebelisa chelete e bolokiloeng ke sechaba. KAROLO 3 Tumelloano Ea Ho Sebelisa Chelete E Bolokiloeng Ke Sechaba

## Section 305

## Section 306

## Section 307

Ho abelana chelete e bolokiloeng ke sechaba. Ho neoa matla a ho berekisa chelete pele ha ho abelana. Ho berekisa chelete ho sena tumelloano. KAROLO 4 Ho Bolokoa Ha Chelete Le Thepa Ea Sechaba

## Section 308

Mesebetsi ea ba tsireletsang chelete le thepa ea sechaba. KAROLO 5 Oditha-ea Akaretsang

## Section 317 — Tse Li Akaretsang

Tsa ho reka le likontraka tsa Hurumente. Tsamaiso ea mekhatlo ea molao. Banka ea Lefatse la Zimbabwe. KHAOLO 18 Melao Le Melao e Akaretsang KHAOLO 1

## Section 323 — Emingoe Melao e Akaretsang Likomeshene

Ho sebelisoa ha Karolo 1. Likomeshene li tla ba likala tsa molao. Ho ba leloko la Komeshene le tsela ea ho sebetsa. Mesebetsi le tsamaiso ea Likomeshene. Lithuso tsa chelete ho Likomeshene. Likomeshene li tla tlaleha ho Palamente selemo le selemo.

## Section 329 — KAROLO 2

Tse Li Akaretsang Tsebetso e tsitsitseng ea boikarabelo ba molaotheo. Lithuso tsa chelete ho likala le lihlopha tsa Mmuso. Molao oa setso o akaretsang tsa mafatse a mangoe. Likopano, mananeo le tumelloano le mafatse a mangoe. Phetoho ea Molaotheo. Ho qala ha Molaotheo, litokisetso le melao e sa sebetseng. KAROLO 3 Hlaloso

## Section 345

Ho sebetsa ha Karolo 3. Melao e akaretsang ea ho hlalosa Molaotheo. Hlaloso. Mo molao o buoang ka likhaolo, likarolo le tse lingoe. Mantsoe a mabeli a kenyelletsang bongata, le ka tsela e tsoanang. Hlahiso le lihlooho tsa litaba. Karolo e buoang ka nako. Karolo ea Melao ea ba nang le mesebetsi. Lihlaloso tsa Palamente. Keletso le literisano. Ho beoa setulong. Ho e tokolla kapa ho tlohela ha bonolo mosebetseng. Ho etsa mesebetse, le tse lingoe. Ha motho a bonala joale ka eo a senang letho la ho etsa ho Hurumente. Batho ba tsolang boemo ho likomiti tsa sechaba. Ho se lumellane pakeng tsa litemana le likarolo tsa Molaotheo.

Hlaloso Ea Pele: Fuleki ea Sechaba, Lipina tsa Sechaba, Liaparo tsa Sechaba le Lipontso tsa Sechaba. Hlaloso Ea Bobeli: ho thibela Litokelo nakong ea lipotlako tsa Sechaba. Hlaloso Ea Boraro: Maikano le Matlafatso ea litokelo. Hlaloso Ea Bonne: Lipatlo tsa ba khethang. Hlaloso Ea Bohlano: Litokiso tsa melao tsa Palamente. Molao Oa Bo Tselela: Ho qala ha Molaotheo ona, Litlhoko tsa Phetoho le Polokeho ea tsona.

TSIMOLOHO ______________________ Rona batho ba Zimbabwe, Re kopane ka litsela tse sieaneng ka takatso ea rona e tloaelehileng pakeng sa tokoloho, toka le ho lekanngoa, le ho hanyetsa ha rona ka bohale ho bokolone, khethollo ea merabe le mefuta eohle ea khatello, Ho phahamisa le ho rorisa banna le basali ba sebete ba ileng ba tela bophelo ba bona ka nako ea Mokhatlo oa ntoa ea tokoloho ea sechaba, Ho hlompha bo-rakhoebo ba rona le basebeletsi ba rona ba sebetsang ka thata ho tsa tsoelopele ea naha ea rona, Ho lemoha hlokahalo ea ho kenya tsebetsong ea temokrasi, tsamaiso e ntle, e hlakileng le e ikarabelang le molao, Re tiisa boikemisetso ba rona ba ho tšehetsa le ho tsireletsa litokelo tsa botho le litokoloho, Ho amohela maruo a rona a hlaho, Ho amohela ho tiea ha mekhoa le setso sarona, Ho keteka ho sisinyeha ha meetlo le meetlo ea rona, re ikemiselitse ho hlõla mathata le litšitiso tse sitisang tsoelo-pele ea rona, Ho rata tokoloho, tekano, khotso, toka, mamello, katleho le bochaba ea meeli e mecha tlase ha morero o tloaelehileng, Ho amohela bophahamo ba Molimo oa Matla Ohle, mo bokamuso ba rona bo bong teng re rarolle ka litaba tsa Molaotheo ho ikemisetsa ho aha sechaba se tsoaraneng, se nang le toka le se atlehileng, se thehiloeng litekanyetso tsa ho pepeneneng, ho lekana, tokoloho, ho hloka toka, botsepehi le seriti sa mosebetsi o boima, tataiso le ea Molimo ea Matla Ohle, ka hona re etsa Molao ona ebile re tsehetso

KHAOLO 1 Likemiso Tse Bohlokoa 1

Repabuleke Zimbabwe ke lefatse la tsoarano, tokoloho le puso ea sechaba e ikemetseng.


Matla a Molaotheo

(1) Molaotheo keona molao o moholo oa lefatse ebile molao ofe le ofe, mokhoa kapa boitsoaro bo sa tsamaesaneng leona ha ona matla.

(2) Molaotheo ona o tlama mang le mang ho akaretsa Hurumente le boeteleli ba eona, lihlopha tsa boikemelo ba Mmuso, tsamaiso ea pupo ea molao, mafapha a kemiso ea melao lemafapha a mekhatlo eohle ea Mmuso. 3

Melao le likemiso tsa bohlokoa

(1) Zimbabwe e ahetsoe holimo ha hlompho ea melao e bohlokoa ea tse latelang—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

(i)

boholo ba Molaotheo; hlompho ea molao; litokelo le litokoloho tsa botho tsa bohlokoa; setso le mekhoa, botho le ho sieana ha tsa borapeli ba sechaba; hlompho ea botho hlokomelo ea tekano ea motho oohle; tekano ea bong; puso entle; le hlokomelo le hlompho ea ntoa ea tokoloho.

(2) Melao ea puso entle, etlamang lefatse le likala tsa Mmuso ho mabaka ohle a Hurumente, ho akaretsa—

(a)

tsamaiso ea boipuso ho tsa politiki e lumelang mekhatlo e fapaneng;

(b) tsamaiso ea tsa khetho e ahetsoeng ho—

(i) ho nea motho ohle o moholo matla a ho khetha le tekano ho tsamaiso ea ho khetha;

(ii) hore hobe le likhetho tse li etsoang ka tokolo ka nako le nako e beiloeng; le

(iii) hore hobe le boemeli bo lekanang ba lichaba tse likhethang.

(c)

tsela e tsoanetseng ea ho nea mmuso moraho ha likhetho;

(d) hlompho ea litokelo tsa mekhatlo eohle ea tsa lipolitiki;

(e)

ho lemoha tsela tsa ho abelana matla a Mmuso;

(f)

hlompha batho ba Zimbabwe, mo ho tsoang matla a puso;

(g) tsela e bonahalang, toka le boikarabelo;

(h) ho hlokomelo ea tsoarano ea sechaba, khotso le tulisano, le ho hlokomeloa ha moseano oa lipuo, mekhoa le mietlo;

(i)

hlokomelo ea litokelo tsa—

(i) lihlopha tsa batho ba seaneng ka merafe, setso, lipuo le borapeli;

(ii) batho ba nang le bohole;

(iii) basali, ba holileng, ba bacha le bana;

(iv) baloeli ba ntoa ea tokoloho;

(j)

ho abelana khumo ea sechaba ka tekano, ho akaretsa mobu;

(k) hlompho ea litokelo tsa batho ka tsoanelo; le

(l) 4

ho abelana ha matla a Mmuso ho mafapha a seaneng le ho phatlalatsa ha li ofisi a etsang mosebetsi oa Hurumente.

Fuleki, Pina Ea Sechaba, Setempe le Letsao la Hurumente

Zimbabwe e na le Fuleki ea Sechaba, Pina ea Sechaba, Setempe le Letsoao la Hurumente, tse hlalosetsoeng ho Lenaneo la Pele. 5

Likala tsa Hurumente Likemiso tsa hurumente ea Zimbabwe li akaretsa—

(a)

hurumente ea sechaba;

(b) likhansile tsa liprofinse le litoropo; le

(c)


likhansile tsa malapa, horea hore-—

(i) likhansile tsa litoropo, li ka bitsoa kapa ka lefe lebitso, ho emela le ho laola litaba tsa batho ba litoropo tseo; le

(ii) likhansile tsa mahae li ka bitsoang kapa ka lefe lebitso, ho emela le ho laola litaba tsa batho ba mahae a leng hare ha litereke tse li bopang li Profensi.

Lipuo

(1) Lipuo tse akaretsang, Chewa, ChiBarwe, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda, le Xhosa, ke lipuo tse hlokomeloang lefatseng la Zimbabwe.

(2) Molao oa Palamente o ka laola tsebeliso ea lingoe lipuo ho likarolo tse li beilong ke Hurumente.

(3) Mmuso le likala tsohle tsa Hurumente litsoanetse ho—

(a)

netefatsa hore lipuo tsohle tse hlokometsoeng ke molao li tsoanetse ho neoa matla a lekanang; le

(b) ho tiisa le ho hlokomela puo e sebelisoang haholo ke batho ba ngongorehang ka tsamaiso ea Hurumente kapa ho tsoarelana ka lipuo tse ba li sebelisang.

(4) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho holisa le ho tsoeletsa lipuo tse li sebelisoang Zimbabwe, ho akaretsa puo ea liSaene le ho leka ka litsela tsohle ho tsoeletsa lipuo tse. 7

Ho tsebisa le ho hlalosetsa sechaba ka Molaotheo Mmuso o tsoanetse ho tsebisa le ho hlalosetsa sechaba ka Molaotheo, haholo ka—

(a) ho fetolela le ho phatlalatsa Molaotheo ho lipuo tsohle tse hlokomeloang ho le molaong;

(b) ho tiisa hore Molaotheo o ea rutisoa likolong; le

(c)

ho khuthatsa batho bohle le mekhatlo tse seaneng ho akaretsa tse li e kemetseng ho akaretsa tse li sebetsang le sechaba, hore liphatlalatse tsebo ka Molaotheo ho sechaba ka bophara.

KHAOLO 2 Maikemisetso a Sechaba 8

Maikemisetso a etellang lihlopha tsa sechaba le Hurumente

(1) Tse li lebeleletsoeng ho khaolo ee, li etella sechaba le likala tsohle tsa Mmuso mapakeng ohle, ho pupo le ho tsamaiso ea molao. Tse li tla nolofatsa ho hloma, ho tsehetsa leho holisa tiiso ea nnete le tokoloho ea sechaba hore batho ba fumane bophelo bo feletseng.

(2) Ha ho hlalosoa Melaotheo oo le melao eohle ea Sechaba, o tsoanetsi ho laoloa ke tse lebeletsoeng tsa Khaolo e. 9

Mmuso o Lokileng

(1) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho sebelisa le ho hlahisa mananeo le melao ka ho tsoeletsa pele bokhone, khuthatso, botsepehi, nnete, hlompho le ho boloka chelete ha lihlopha tsohle tsa Hurumente, likarolong tsohle, haholo—

(a) setulo sa ofisi ea sechaba se tsoanetse motho a na le bokhone ba mosebetsi;

(b) mehato ea ho phatlalatsa, ho loana le ho tlosa bobolu ho sebelisa matla sa lokang ha ba sebetsang li ofising tsa lipolitiki le tsa sechaba.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetsi ho netefatsa hore lihlopha tsohle le likala tsa hurumente, haholo Likomeshene, li tsoanetsing ho tsoeletsa mesebetsi ea bona hantle ka nete ka tsela e tsoanang le ka bokhone. 10

Bongoe baSechaba, khotso le boiketlo

Mmuso le motho e mong le e mong, ho akaretsa batho ba melao, likala tsa hurumente li tsoanetse ho tsoeletsa pele tsoarano, khotso le boiketlo ba lefatse. 11

Ho hlokomela litokelo le litokoloho

Mmuso o tsoanetse ho leka ka ho tsireletsa litokelo le litokoloho tse supiloeng ho Khaolo 4 hore li tlatsoe ebile li hlokomeloe. 12

Leano la ho sebelisana le mangoe mafatse

(1) Leano la ho sebelisana le mangoe mafatse le tsoanetsi ho aheloa ho mananeo a latelang—

(a) ho tsoeletsa leho tsireletsa tsa bohlokoa bophelong ba batho ba Zimbabwe;

(b) ho hlompha molao oa le fatse ka bophara;

(c)

ho lulisana ka khotso le mangoe mafatse; le ho

(d) ho lamola lintoa hare ha mafatse a mangoe ka khotso.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho kenya pele tsoarano ea lefatse la Afrika ka bophara ho tsa setso le lipolitiki, khumo le ho tsoarelana le mafatse a mangoe a emelang taba tsa khotso, ho lula hantle le tsoelapele ea Afrika le botho. 13

Tsoelopele ea sechaba

(1) Sechaba le likala tsa hurumente le mafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho hlahisa tsoelopele ea tekano le potlako, haholo ka mabapi le—

(a)

2013

Ho khuthatsa tsoeletso ea tsa baikemetseng le ho ikemela;

(b) ho tsoeletsa tsebo ea lilemo, tsa likhoebo, liindasitiri, tsa thekinoloji le tsoelopele ea tsa li saensi;

(c)

ho tsoeletsa tsebo ea li-indasitiri le tsa ho rekisa tsa ho matlafatsa ba ahi ba Zimbabwe; le

(d) ho tlisa tsoelopele e lekanang ho mafapha ohle a Zimbabwe, haholo ho lekana ha tsoelopele hohle mahae le litoropong.

(2) Tsohle tse li tsoang karolong ee li tsoanetsi ho akaretsa batho bohle hore ba bope maano a tsoelopele a tse li ba amang.

(3) Tsohle tse litsoang karolong ee ke matsapa a batlang ho tsireletsa le ho tsoeletsa tekano le litokelo tsa batho, haholo basali.

(4) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho hlokomela leho tiisa hore batho ba sechaba se seng le se seng sefumane tsohle tse li tsoang ho khumo ea naha ea bone. 14

Matlafatso le ho tsoeletsa mesebetsi

(1) Mmuso, likala le lihlopha tsa hurumente, karolong tse li fapaneg li tsoanetse ho lakatsa hlahiso le maikarabelo a ho matlafatsa ka mehato e bonahalang e tsoanetsi, haholo ho batho, lihlopha le lichaba tse setsing moraho ho tsa tsoelapele.

(2) Ka nako tsohle lefatse le likala tsohle tsa Mmuso le mafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho bona hore tsela tse atlameng li ea lateloa ho ngatafatsa mesebetse haholo holo ho basali le ba bacha. 15

Ho fumaneha ha lijo Lefatse le tsoanetse ho—

(a) khuthatsa sechaba ka tsa ho lema leho boloka lijo tse lekaneng;

(b) aha poleke tse likholo tsa ho boloka lijo; le

(c)


ho khuthatsa le ho ruta batho ka tsa lijo tse li hloekileng le ka tsela tse sieaneng.

Setso

(1) Lefatse le likala tsohle tsa hurumente lemafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho holisa leho tsireletsa mekhoa le liketso, tsa setso, tsa maemo, botho le tekano ea batho baZimbabwe.

(2) Lefatse le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona le bohle baahi ba Zimbabwe ba tsoanetse ho e neela ho boloka le ho tsireletsa lefa lefatse la Zimbabwe.

(3) Lefatse le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho nea hlompho e kholo ho tsohle likala tsa setso. 17

Tekano ea bong

(1) Lefatse la Zimbabwe le tsoanetse ho hlahisa tekano ea bong, haholo—

(a)

ho akaretsa basali mesebetseng e tsoanang le ea banna ka ho lekana;

(b) sechaba se tsoanetse, ho hlokomela hore—

(i) bong bohle bo tsoanetse ho emeloa ke likala le lihlopha tsa hurumente li karolong tsohle; le

(ii) basali ba bopa hafu ea maloko a Likomeshene tsohle li khethong le mafapeng ohle a hurumente tlase ha molaotheo kapa tlase ha pupo ea Molao oa Palamente;

(c)

lefatse le tsohle li kala tsa hurumente lemafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho hatela pele hore basali bafumane lefa la bona ho akaretsa khumo le Mobu ho tsoana le banna.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho hatela pele ho qeta ho khethana hare ha basali le banna ho lateloa liketso tse li fetileng le mananeo. 18

Kemelo e lekanetsing ea setereke

(1) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho bona hore mekhatlo eohle ea Zimbabwe e emeloa ka tsela e tsoanang ho likala le likarolo tsohle tsa hurumente.

(2) Mmuso le likala tsohle tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona litsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle hore lichaba tsohle tsa lefatse li fumane tokelo e lekanang ho tsohle tsa hlaho hore li tsoeletsoe ka tsona. 19

Bana

(1) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho tsoeletsa le ho bopa melao e hlokomelang bana le tse libong bohlokoa ho bona.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho khomarela melao e reng leha tsa khumo ea naha e le teng li hlokomele hore bana ba fumane takatso ea pele, hore—(a) ba fumane hlokomelo ea phole kapa batsoali kapa ba fumane bolulo bo bontle leha ba sena batsoali;

(b) ba fumane bolulo le lijo tse tsoanetseng, hlokomelo ea tsa bophelo le tsohle tsa ho phela;

(c)

ba tsireletsehe ho ho hlokofatso, ho nyatso kapa ho hateletso;

(d) ba fumane thuto e tsoanetseng le bokhone ba tsa bophelo ba bona.

(3) Mmuso o tsoanetsi ho laoloa ke melao le mehato e fapaneng mabapi le—

(a)

ho tsireletsa bana ho mesebetse e hlokofatsang;

(b) ho hlokomela hore bana ba se ke ba lumelloa ho etsa mesebetse—

(i) e sa lekaneng mengoaha ea matsoalo a bona;

(ii) e ka ba utloisang bohloko mele le likhopolo, lithutong, bophelong ba bona kapa tsela ea ho jelana le bangoe bana. 20

Ba bacha

(1) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa Hurumente le mafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho etsa ka litsela tsohle le ka tumelloano hore ba bacha ba mengoaha etsoang ho ee leshome le metso e mehlano ho fihla ho mashome a mararo le metso e mehlano—

(a) ba fumane thuto le bokhone bo tsoanetseng;

(b) ba fumane sebaka saho kopanelana leho emeloa hotsa politiki le tsa khumo, tsa malapa le lingoe tse ngata tsa bophelo;

(c)

ba fumane sebaka sa mesebetse le ho tsoeletsa khumo ea naha;

(d) ba fumane sebaka sa ho e tebatsa le ho etela tulo tse tsoeletseng tsa ho ethabisa; le

(e)

ho tsireletsoa mekhoeng ea setso e ka ba etsang hore batsoe taolong leho hateleloa.

(2) Molao oa Palamente o ka nea hore hobe le lenaneho le le ngoekapa mananeho a bacha lefatseng lohle.

(3) Mabaka le mananeho a le ho likaroloana 1 le 2atsoanetse ho akaretsaba bacha ba Mmuso ohle, ho saema le sehlopha sa politiki. 21

Batho ba ba tsofetseng

(1) Lefatse letsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle ho bopa melao e neang hlompho, tsutsumetso le tsireletso ea batho ba holileng hore ba khone ho sebetsa sechabeng sa bona.

(2) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho—

(a)

khuthatsa ba ba holo ho sebetsa ka matla ho tsohle ho tsoeletso ea sechaba;

(b) nea li tsoanelo lijo, le tsohle tse thusang ba ba holo ba lihlokang;

(c)

tsoeletsa mananeho a neang ba holo sebaka sa ho phetha mesebetse ea tsoelopele tse tsamaelanang le limpho le litakatso tsa bona; le

(d) ho khuthatsa makhotla a lekang ho thusa ho tsoeletso ea bophelo batho ba ba holo. 22

Batho ba nang le bokooa/ba holahetsing

(1) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho leka ka hohle ho hlokomela batho banang le bohole menahanong kapa meleng ha holo tokelo ea bona ea ho neoa hlompho.

(2) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho leka ka hohle ho tse lifumanehang ho thusa batho banang le bohole ho oketsa tsolofelo ea bona le ho fokotsa tse li ba tsoenyang.

(3) Ha holo Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho—

(a)

bopa mananeho a hlokomelang batho banang le bohole menahanong le meleng ho oketsa tsolofelo ea bona le hofokotsa tse li batsoenyang;

(b) nea litsoanelo, lijo, le tseo tse li thusang ba holahetseng ba lihlokang ho mananeho a tsoelopele ea Mmuso;

(c)

khuthatsa le ho tsoeletsa tsela tsa puo tse li tsoanetseng batho ba holehileng; le

(d) ho thaletsa makhotla a takatso a lebeletsoeng ho holisa tsela ea bophelo a bo ba holehileng.

(4) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho bona hore ho na le mananeo a lokileng hore batho ba holahehileng ba ea finyella ho fihla mo ho bafihlang mang le mang. 23

Bahlabane ba Ntoa ea Tokoloho

(1) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho nea hlompho, khuliso, le hlokomelo ho bahlabane ba ntoa ea tokoloho, ho rea hore—

(a)

bohle baloeli ba ntoa ea tokoloho;

(b) ba thusitseng ba loeli ba ntoa ea lefatse ho ntoa ea tokoloho; le—

(c)

ba ba e leng ba hlahleloa litoronkong ka nako ea ntoa ea tokoloho.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho bopa melao le makhotla a bonang ka bophelo ba baloeli ba tokoloho le ho ba nea matla ho khumo ea lefatse le bophelo ba bona. 24

Mesebetsi le kamano mesebetsing

(1) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho khumarela molao le mabaka hare ha tsohle tsa hlaho tse li fumanehang ho nea motho mang kapa mang sebaka ba ho sebetsa ka tokoloho mo a khethileng hore ba tle bafumane bolulo bo lokileng ba bona le liphole tsa bona.

(2) Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho netefatsa hore—

(a) batho ba fumana mesebetse ka bongata ba bona;

(b) melao e thibelang batho ho sebetsa mesebetse e ea tlosoa hore ba tle ba etse mesebetsi e kenyang khumo;

(c)

thuto ea batho le tsoeletso ea lithuto le tokisetso ea mesebetse e akaretsa le ea batho ba na le bohole; le

(d) tsoeletso ea melao joale ka hlokomela ea phole e etsang hore basali ba sebetse. 25

Tsireletso ea malapa

Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapha a tsona li tsoanetse ho tsireletsa leho tsoeletsa likala tsa ba le lelapa haholo ho tsoeletsa khumo e fumanehang mabapi le ho nka molao oa—

(a) ho nea thuto le thuso batsoaling ba hlokomelang bana; le

(b) ho thibela lintoa tsa mefuta oa ho hlokofatsoa malapeng. 26

Lenyalo Mmuso o tsoanetsi ho nka mananeo a tsoanetseng hore—

(a)

ho seke ha ba le lenyalo hare ha batho ba babeli ho sena tumelloano ea bao ba nyalanang;

(b) bana a ba tsoanela ho nyalana ka lebaka la litakatso tsa batsoali ba bona;

(c)

hona le tekano ea litokelo ho batho ba nyalaneng le ho phatlalatsoeng ha lona;

(d) ho phatlalatso ea lenyalo e kaba lefu kapa ho sa utloana, molao o tsireletsa monna, mosali le bana. 27

Thuto

(1) Mmuso o tsoanetsi ho nka mananeo a tsoanetseng ho tsoeletsa—

(a)

thuto e sa pateloeng ho bana bohle ka khopeletso; le

(b) lithuto tsa mabaka ea holimo.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa e tsoanetseng hore banyana ba fumane thuto e tsoanang le ea basemana. 28

Bolulo

Mmuso le tsohle likala tsa hurumente lemafapa a tsona li tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle ho nka melao le matsapa ho netefatsa hore motho mang le mang a fumana tsireletso ea le lapa le bolulo.

2013

Litsebeletso tsa Bophelo

(1) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle hore batho ba Zimbabwe bafumane likala tse likanang ka nako tsohle lefatseng ka bophara.

(2) Mmuso o tsoanetse ho nka qetho ho netefatsa hore a hona motho a haneloang kalafo ea potlako lipetleleng tsohle.

(3) Mmuso o tsoanetsi ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle le tsohle tse li fumanehang ho akaretsa thutiso le tsebiso sechabeng ea potlako ho tsa maloetse. 30

Boiketlo ba sechaba

Mmuso o tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle, hare ha tsohle tse fumanehang ho nea tsireletso ho batho bohle lefatseng le bohle ba hlokang thuso. 31

Thuso e leng molaong

Mmuso o tsoanetse hore hare ha tsohle tse fumanehang,e neoe kemelo hare ha makhotla a lebeletseng melato e menyenyane le e meholo bathong ba e batlang empa bas a finyelle ho fumana bao bae khethetseng bona. 32

Meaho ea Lipapali le boithabiso

Mmuso o tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle ho khuthatsa meraloko ea ho e tibatsa ho lipoleke tse li ahiloeng ke Mmuso. 33

Ho boloka tsebo ea setso

Mmuso o tsoanetse ho etsa ka mekhoa eohle ho tiisa tsireletso eohle le ho tsoeletso ea tsa hlaho tse akaretsang a lihlare le mekhoa ea liphoofolo le lifate tse li fumanehang sechabeng se ba lula ho sona. 34

Ho sebelisa melao ea mafatse

Mmuso o tsoanetse ho hlokomela hore tsohle litumelloano tsa tsa ho ahesana lemangoe mafatse li ea ngoloa hare ha molao oa lefatse. KHAOLO 3 Boahi 35

Boahi ba Zimbabwe

(1) Batho ba Zimbabwe ba e tsupa bo bona ka matsoalo, ka ho etela le ho ngoloa libukeng tsa molao.

(2) Baahi bohle ba Zimbabwe ba na le tokelo, likutloelo le litsoanelo ka ma tsoalo a bona hape ba na le tsoanelo ea ho etsa mesebetse e sieaneng lefatseng ho ea ka matsoalo a bona.

(3) Batho bohle ba Zimbabwe bana le litokelo le litsoanelo tse latelanglitsoanele le tse lingoe tsa bohlokoa tse ba li neoa ke molao—

(a)

ho tsireletsa lefatse la bona ka mekhoa eohle;

(b) hofumana li pasipoti lemangoe mangolo a neang tokelo ea ho etela tulo tse sieaneng; le

(c)

ho fumana mangolo a matsoalo a bona le a mangoe a supang hore bona ke bo mang.

(4) Batho bohle ba tsoaloang Zimbabwe ba na le mesebetse e latelang holimo ha e eo ba e neoang ke molao—

(a) ho tsepeha lefatseng;

(b) ho hlokomela molaotheo leho hlompha litsehetso le likarolo tsa ona;

(c)

ho hlompa fuleki ea sechaba le pina ea sechaba, le

(d) ka matla a bona ohle ho tsireletsa lefatse lena le bonatla ba lona. 36

Boahi ka tsoalo

(1) Batho ke baahi ba Zimbabwe ka matsoalo ha ba tsoaletsoe mona ebileha—

(a)

ebile kapa ntate oa bona a na le boahi ba Zimbabwe; kapa

(b) rakholo kapa kuku o na le boahi ba Zimbabwe ka matsoalo kapa ka leloko.

(2) Batho ba tsoaletsoeng ntleha Zimbabwe, ke baahi ba ba Zimbabwe ka matsoalo ha e le hore ka nako ba tsoaloa e mongoe oa batsoali ba bona a ne a le moahi oa Zimbabwe—

(a) ha a ne a lula Zimbabwe; kapa

(b) a sebetsa ntleha lefatse la Zimbabwe a sebeletsa Mmuso kapa kampani efumanehang mafatseng a mangata.

(3) Ngoana a fumanehang Zimbabwe a na le mengoaha e tlase ha mengoaha e mashome le e mehlano a sena batsoali kapa a sa tsebe a motsoalang, ke moahi oa Zimbabwe ka matsoalo. 37

Boahi ka leloko

Ha re lebelela khaolong 36(2) batho ba tsoaletsoeng ntleha lefatse la Zimbabwe ke ba Zimbabwe ka leloko ha e baka nako ba tsoaloa—

(a) e mongoe oa batsoali ba hae kapa baholo holo ba hae ba ne ba le baluli ba Zimbabwe ka matsoalo kapa ka leloko;

(b) e ka ba e mongoe oa batsoali ba hae a ne a le moahi oa Zimbabwe ka ho ngolisa mangolo a lefatse, ho lateloa molao. 38

Boahi ka ho ngolisa

(1) Motho eo a nyetsoeng ke motho oa Zimbabwe ha sebaka se lekanang mengoaha e mehlano, e ka ba pele kapa kamoraho ha sebaka sebeiloeng a tsotisa tse hlalositsoe molaong oa Palamente, o na le tumello ea ho ngoloa bukeng ea baahi ba Zimbabwe.

(2) Motho e mong a lutseng Zimbabwe ka tumello ea molao ha nako e lekanang mongoaha e leshome, e ka ba pele kapa kamoraho ha sebaka sebeiloeng kapa pele ho nako e beiloeng, a tsotisa tse hlalositsoeng meloang ea Palamente, o na le tumelo ea ho ngoloa bukeng ea litsoaloa tsa Zimbabwe.

(3) Ngoana a sebong setsoala sa Zimbabwe, empa a bolokiloe ke motho oa Zimbabwe moraho kapa pele ho nako e beiloeng, o na letokelo ea ho ngoloa bukeng ea litsoaloa tsa Zimbabwe. 39

Ho nkeloa boahi

(1) Bo Zimbabwe ka ho ngolisa bo ka lahloa ha—

(a) motho a tsoereng pampiri tse se mo molaong, ka ho epata kapa ho fihla nnete;

(b) ka nako ea ntoa ea tokoloho, motho eo a fumanehile buisana kapa ho berekisana le sera kapa a ne a na le mesebetsi a tseba hore ke ea ho thusa sera ntoeng eo.

(2) Boahi baZimbabwe ka matsoalo bo ka lahloa ha—

(a)

motho a fumane mapampiri e sa lokang ea ho patella, ka ho epata;

(b) ka tsela ea motho a sopiloeng ho khaolo 36(3) botsoalo ba motho kapa batsoali ba hae ba tsebea, ho supa hore motho eo ke se tsoala sa lengoe lefatse.

(3) Motho ha haneloe ho ba moahi oa Zimbabwe ka tlase ha karolo e na ha ho sena e le hore o tla hloka Mmuso oa boahi. 40

Ho boloka boahi leha o le lenyalo kapa lenyalo le felile Botsoalo ba Zimbabwe a bo lahlehi ka lenyalo kapa ka ho fela ha le nyalo.


Boto ea boahi le ho etelana

Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho hlahisa kemiso ea Boto ea Boahi le tsa Boeteli e tla bang le Motsoare oa Setulo le mangoe maloka a mabeli atla khethoang ke Mookameli a tla lebellang ka tsa ho—

(a) nea le ho lahla boahi ka ho ngolisa;

(b) ho lumella batho, ho lula le ho sebetsa Zimbabwe, ba lokisa litsoanelo tse ba lumellang ba lule le ho sebetsa; le

(c) 42

ho phethahatsa tsohle litsoanelo tse li beiloeng ho Boto ke molao oa Palamente.

Matla a Palamente ka tsa boahi Molao oa Palamente o ka nea ka tsela e tsamaelanang le khaolo e na—

(a) litsela tse lateloang tsa ho fumana mangolo a matsoalo;

(b) ho lahla boahi ba Zimbabwe ka ho ithatela;

(c)

tsela tse lateloang tsa ho lahla boahi ba Zimbabwe ka ho ngolisoa;

(d) ho boloka botsoalo ba Zimbabwe;


(e)

ho hanela ho ba moahi oa mafatse amabeli ho ka ba boahi ka leloko kappa ka ho ngolisa ; le

(f)

ho nea matla a ho sebelisa khaolo ena ka botlalo.

Ho tsoelapele le ho tsoseletsa boahi bo eleng ba ba teng

(1) Motho oohle a ne ale teng ka letsatsi la mongolo oo, o tla etella pele ale moahi oa Zimbabwe kamoraho ha letsatsi leo.

(2) Motho oohle a tsoaletsoeng Zimbabwe peleha letsatsi la tsoeletso ea mongolo oo, ke se tsoaloa sa Zimbabwe ka matsoalo ha—

(a)

ha e mongoe kapa batsoali ba hae bohle tsoaletsoe lefatseng le le bileng leloko la SADC le bopiloeng Windhoek lefatseng la Republic ea Namibia ka le tsatsi la 17 Mpalane ka ngoaha oa 1992 le;

(b) eo a ne a lula Zimbabwe ka tsatse la tsoeletso ea mongolo oo.

KHAOLO 4 Hlaloso Ea Litokelo KAROLO 1 Sebeliso Le Hlaloso Ea Khaolo 4 44

Molato oa ho hlompha litokelo le litokoloho tsa batho tsa bohlokoa

Mmuso le batho bohle ho akaretsa batsoari ba molao le tulo tsa Mmuso mapakeng ohle le tsoanetse ho hlompha, ho tsireletsa, ho tsoeletsa le ho tlatsa malokelo le litokoloho tse neiloeng khaolong ena. 45

Tsebeliso ea Khaolo 4

(1) Khaolo ena e tlama Mmuso le likala tsohle tsa lekhotla le tulo tsa hurumente mapakeng ohle.

(2) Khaolo ena e tlama ba busi ba hlaho le batsoari ba litulo, e hlokomela hape le matla a neiloeng ba busi tulong le mesebetseng ba e etsang.

(3) Batho ba molao le batho bohle ba neoa matla le litokelo le litokoloho tse neiloeng khaolong e na hore litokelo le litokoloho li fetisetsoe ho bona. 46

Hlaloso ea Khaolo 4

(1) Ho hlalosa khaolo e na, khotla, karolo kapa sehlopha se—

(a) tsoanetse ho nea tokelo le litokoloho tse tletseng tse bong khaolong ena;

(b) tsoanetse ho tsoeletsa le ho tiisa melao e tsehetsang sechaba se lokolohileng ka ho ba phatlalatsa ho boitsoaro, hlompho, tekano le tokoloho ka botlalo ka tse li beiloeng ho karolo 3;

(c)

tsoanetse ho hlokomela melao ea mafatse ohle le litumelloano mo Zimbabwe e leng leloka;

(d)

tsoanetsi ho nea hlompho le hlokomelo tsohle tse beiloeng ke molaotheo, haholo kemiso ne litakatso kapa li tetetellotse neiloeng ho khaolo 2; le ho

(e)

ho nea hlompho ho melao ea mangoe mafatse;

ho akaretsoa le mabaka ohle a tsoanetseng ho hlokomeloa ha ho hlalosoa Molaotheo.

(2) Ha re hlalosa tse supiloeng le ho tsoeletsa molao oa setso, Lekhotla le likhoro li tsoanetse ho tsoeletsa lietelelloa ke moea omontle le li tetelello tsa khaolo ena. 47

Khaolo 4 ha e thibele ho ba le litokelo tse lingoe

Khaolo ena ha e hlalose ho bat eng ha lingoe litokelo le litokoloho tse ka sebelisoang ke molao tse ka etsang hore li tsamaelane le Molaotheo ona. KAROLO 2 Litokelo Tsa Batho Le Li Tokoloho Tsa Bohlokoa 48

Tokelo ea bophelo

(1) Motho ohle o na le tokelo ea bophelo.

(2) Molao o ka nea kahlolo ea lefu mothong eo aroesoang molato oa ho bolaea ka tsela e sehloho ebile—

(a) molao o ka nea khoro tsoetso ea ho a hlola kapa ho se ahlole;

(b) kahlolo e na e ka e tsoa fela ka moahloli a na le bokhone;

(c)

kahlolo ha tsoanela ho neoa ha—

(i) e le hore molato ona o etsahetse ka nako motho a na le mengoaha e mashome a mabeli kapa tlase;

(ii) a nale mengoaha e fetang e mashome a supileng;

(d) kahlolo e na e ke ke ea neoa motho oa mosali; le

(e)

hore motho a roesoang molato ona o na le tumello ea ho kupa tsoarelo kapa ho phimuleloa molato ke Mookameli.

(3) Molao oa lefatse o tsoanetse ho tsireletsa bana ba e so ba tsoaloe le hore ho thuba mpa ho etsoe ho lateloa molao. 49

Tokelo ea tokoloho ea motho

(1) Motho mong le mong o na le tokelo ea hae e akaretsang—

(a)

ho se hlahleloe ntleha tseko; le

(b) ho se nkeloe litokelo tsa hae phatlalatsane ntleha bohlatse.

(2) A ho motho ea tsoanetseng ho hlahleloa toronkong ka baka la ho se tlatse tumelloano. 50

Litokelo tsa batho ba tsoeroeng le ba bo toronkong

(1) Motho eo a tsoeroeng o—

(a)

tsoanetse ho tsebesoa ka molato oa haee ka nako a tsoaroa;

(b) tsoanetse ho lumelelloa kapela—

(i) ho patela Mmuso, ho tsebisa e mongoe ka eena, motsoalle, moemel i oa molao e mongoe a mokhethileng; le

(ii) hore a ikhethele moemeli kapa ngaka a ipatelela;

(c)

ho tsoaroa ka botho le hlompho e neoang batho bohle bophelong;

(d) ho lokolloa ho se na letho le mothibelang kapa ka mabaka a utloalang ba emetsi kahlolo, ntle ha hona le mabaka a etsang hore ba tsoelepele ba le toronkong;

(e)

o tsoanetse ho neoa tokelo ea ho hana ho tsoaroa ho se bo molaong peleha tseko ea molato.

(2) Motho ofe le ofe ea tsoaroeng kapa ea bo teronkong—

(a)

ka morero oa ho mo tlisa ka peleha khotla; kapa

(b) pakeng sa tlolo ea molao; empa a sa lokolloa o tsoanetsi ho tlisoa kapela lekhotleng ho eso ho fete lihora tse mashone a manne le tse robeli kamoraho ha ho tsoaroa ho se na taba hore ke Moqibelo, Sontaha kapa ke letsatsi la phomolo ea sechaba.

(3) Motho ofe le ofe ea sa tlisoang lekhotleng ka nako ea lihora tse mashome a manne le a ma robeli a boletsoeng ho serapeng (2) o tsoanetsi ho lokolla kapela ntle le hore ho kena ha bona teronkong ho laotsoe ke lekhotla le nang le bokhone.

(4) Motho ofe le ofe ea tsoaroang kapa ea kenyoa chankaneng ka lebaka la tlolo ea molao o na le tokeloea—

(a) ho khutsa;

(b) ho tsebisoa kapela—

(i) tokelo ea bona ea ho khutsa; le

(ii) tsa liphello tsa ho khutsa le ho se khutse;

(c)

a se ke a qobelloa hore a lumele kapa a amohele; le

(d) ho ponahalo ea pele lekhotleng kamoraho ho tsoaroa, ho qosoa kapa ho tsebisoa hore lebaka la hore ba tsoelapele ba le chankaneng kapa ho lokolloa.

(5) Motho ofe le ofe ea tsoeroeng, ho akaretsa leo a fumanehileng ana le molato, o na le tokelo—

(a) ho tsebisoa ka potlako lebaka la ho ba toronkong ha bona;

(b) ka litsenyehelo tsa bona, ho buisana le ba bang ka lekunutu le ngaka ba e khethileng, le ho tsebisoa ka tokelo ena kapele;

(c)

ho buisana le, le ho eteloa ke—

(i) emongoe ka eena;

(ii) motsalle;

(iii) moeletsi oa bona oa bolumeli eo a ikhethetseng;

(iv) moemeli ikhethetseng oa molao;

(v) ngaka ea bona a ikhethileng; le

(vi) ho ea ka mabaka aka thibelang a utloahalang a beiloeng pakeng sa tsamaiso e nepahetseng ea lichankana kapa libaka tsa botlamuoa, mang kapa mang eo a mokhethileng;

(d) maemo a litlamong a lumellanang le seriti sa batho, ho kenyelletsa le monyetla oa ho ikoetlisa meleng le ho nea ka litsenyehelo tsa muso, bolulo bo lekaneng, mehaho ea bolulo, bohloeki ba botho, phepo e nepahetseng, lisebelisoa tse nepahetseng tsa ho bala le kalafo; le

(e)

ho phephetsa molao oa ho kenyoa ha bona ka tholko ka pela lekhotla ebile, ha eba ho koalloa tironkong ho sa lumelloe ke molao, ho lokolloa kapela.

(6) Motho leha e le ofe ea koaletsoeng ho emetse nyeoe ka lebaka la molato e bileha ka a emisoa ka nako e tsoanetsing o tlameha ho lokolloa teronkong, ho sa hlokahale kapa ka maemo a utloahalang ho netefatsa hore kamoraho hore a lokolloe—

(a) o tla ea nyeoeng;

(b) o se ke oa kena-kenana le bopaki bo tla neoang nyeoeng; le

(c)

o se ke oa etsa phoso leha e le efe pele nyeoe e qala.

(7) Ha e ba ho na le mabaka a utloalang a ho lumela hore motho o koaletsoe ka molao kapa ha ho khonehe ho netefatsa hore na motho ea tsoeroeng o kae, motho leha e le ofe a ka atamela Lekhotla la holimo pakeng sa taelo—

(a) ea habeas corpus, ke hore taelo e hlokang hore motho ea koaletsoeng a lokolloe, kapa a tlisoe ka pela lekhotla pakeng sa molao oa ho kenyoa toronkong ho tsoanetsi kappa ho hloka hore motho ea tsoeroeng a hlahe hore o kae; kapa

(b) ho phatlalatsa hore batsoaruoa ha ba lumelloe ke molao ebile ba laela ho lokolloa ha motho ea koaletsoeng; ebile Lekhotla la holimo le ka etsa eng kapa eng e loketseng maemo.

(8) Ho tsoaroa kapa ho koalloa ho etsoang karolong ena, kapa mo lihlaloso tse boletsoeng karolong ena li sa finyelloang, ha li lumelloe ke molao.

(9) Motho leha e le ofe ea tsoeroeng ka molao kapa a tsoeroeng ntleha molao o na le tokelo ea ho fumana tefo ho motho ea nang le boikarabelo ba ho tsoaroa kapa ho koalloa teronkong, empa molao o ka thibela batho ba latelang boikarabelo tlase ha karolo ena—

(a) ofisi ea boahloli e sebetsang boemong ba molao ka mokhoa o utloahalang le ka tumelo e ntle;

(b) ofisi engoe le engoe ea sechaba e sebetsang ka mokhoa o utloahalang le ka botsepehi le ntle le ho hloka tsebo kapa ho se tsotelle. 51

Litokelo le seriti sa botho

Motho e mong le e mong o na le seriti sa boithati bophelong ba bona ba le nosti le ba sechaba, le tokelo ea ho ba le seriti se hlomphehang le se tsirelelitsoeng. 52

Tokelo ea tsireletso ea motho

Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho tsepahala meleng le kelellong, e kenyeletsang hantle—

(a) ho lokoloha mefuteng eohle ea pefo e tsoang mehloling ea sechaba kapa ea sechaba;

(b) ho latela mangolo a mang a Molaotheo ona, ho etsa liqeto malebana le ho ikatisa; le

(c) 53

hore ba se ke ba neoa liteko tsa bongaka kapa tsa saense, kapa ho tlosoa kapa ho sebelisoa ha maloka a bona tsa nama, ntle le tumello ea bona.

Tokoloho ho tsoaro e bohloko kapa e sehloho, ho nyatsoa le kotlo

Ha hona motho a tsoanetsi ho tsoaro e se nang botho kapa e nyonyehang. Ha ho motho ea ka hlokofatsoang ke mele kapa kelellong kapa kalafo e sehloho, e se nang botho kapa e nyonyehang kapa kotlo. 54

Tokoloho bokhobeng le bokhoba Ha ho motho ea ka bang le tokelo ea makhoba kapa bokhoba.


Bolokolohi ba ho qobelloa kapa ho tsotsometsoa mosebetsing Ha ho motho ea ka e tsoang ho etsa mosebetsi o qobelloang kapa o tsotsometsoang.


Tekano le le ho se khetholle

(1) Batho bohle ba lekana pele ha molao ebile ba na le tokelo ea ho tsireletso.

(2) Basali le banna ba na le tokelo ea ho phekoloa ka tsela e lekanang, ho kenyelletsa le tokelo ea menyetla e lekanang litabeng tsa lipolotiki, tsa khumo, tsa setso le tsa sechaba.

(3) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho se tsoaroe ka mokhoa o hlokang toka ka mabaka a kang boahi, bochaba, morabe, leloko, sebaka sa tsoalo, puo, sehlopha, tsa bolumeli, botsoalle ba lipolotiki, maikutlo, tloaelo, setso, thobalano, tekano, boemo ba lenyalo, lilemo, bokhachane, bokooa kapa boemo ba moruo kapa sechabeng, kapa hore na tsoaletsoe ntleha lenyalo.

(4) Motho o tsoaroa ka tsela e khethollang molemong oa karolo ea (3) ha eba—

(a)

a be oa ka kotloloho kapa ka mokhoa o sa tobang ho boemo, thibelo kapa bokooa bo batho ba bang ba sa o laelang; kapa

(b) batho ba bang ba neoa tlotla kapa monyetla oo ba o filoeng ka kotloloho kapa ka tsela e tobileng.

(5) Khethollo ka mabaka a hlalositsoeng ho serapeng sa (3) ha a na toka ntle le hore ho be ho hore khethollo e lokile, e na le mabaka a utloahalang ebile e nepahala mokhatlong oa temokrasi o thehiloeng ka bolokolohi, toka, seriti sa botho, tekano le tokoloho.

(6) Mmuso o tlameha ho nka mehato e lokileng ea molao le mehato e meng ho ntlafatsa katleho ea ho lekana le ho tsireletsa kapa ho etetsa pele batho kapa lihlopha tsa batho ba ileng ba sotloa ke khethollo e se nang toka, empa—

(a)

mehato e joalo e lokela ho nkoa ho lokisa maemo a hlokahalang;

(b) mehato e joalo ha e tsoanele ho lebelloa jaole ka e mebe ho merero ea

(3). 57

Tokoloho ea ho ba le lekunutu

Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho ba le sephiri, ho kenyelletsang tokelo ea ho se be le—

(a) ntlo ea bona, moaho kapa thepa e kenyoe ntle le tumello ea bona;

(b) motho oa bona, lehae, moaho kapa thepa e batlisisitsoe;

(c)

thepa ea bona e nkioe;

(d) boinotsing ba melaetsa ea bona ea puisano; kapa

(e) 58

boemo ba bona ba bophelo bo phatlalatsoe.

Bolokolohi ba kopano le botsoalle

(1) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea tokoloho ea ho kopana, le tokelo ea ho se kopane kapa ho kopanela le ba bang.

(2) Ha ho motho ea ka qobelloa ho ba setho sa mokhatlo kapa ho ea libokeng kapa ho bokella. 59

Bolokolohi ba ho bontsa le ho nea lipelaelo

Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho bontsa le ho hlahisa likupo, empa litokelo tsena li sebelisoa ka khotso. 60

Bolokolohi ba letsoalo

(1) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea bolokolohi ba letsoalo, e akaretsang—

(a) bolokolohi ba ho nahana, maikutlo, bolumeli kapa tumelo; le

(b) bolokolohi ba ho e tloaetsa le ho phatlalatsa le ho bontsa ka maikutlo ho menahano ea bona, maikutlo, bolumeli kapa tumelo, e bang phatlalatsa kappa sephiring ba le bang kapa ba na le ba bangoe.

(2) Ha ho motho ea ka qobelloang ho nka kano e khahlanong le bolumeli kapa tumelo ea hae kapa ho nka kano ka tsela e khahlanong le bolumeli kapa tumelo ea bona.

(3) Batsoali le bahlokomeli ba bana ba banyenyane ba na le tokelo ea ho ikhethela, ho ea ka litumelo tsa bona, ho holisa bana ba bona boitsoarong le bo lumeling, ha feela ba sa khetholle litokelo tse bana ba bona ba nang le tokelo ho tsona tlase ha

Molaotheo ona, ho akaretsa le litokelo tsa bona ho thuto, bophelo bo botle, polokeho le boiketlo.

(4) Mokhatlo ofe kapa ofe oa bolumeli o ka emisa mekhatlo mo ho ka etsoang tsa bo lumeli, leha eba ba sa amohela thuso kapa chelete e ngoe e tsoang ho Mmuso. 61

Bolokolohi ba ho bua le tokoloho ea phatlalatso ea litaba

(1) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea tokoloho ea ho bua, e akaretsang—

(a) bolokolohi ba ho batla, ho amohela le ho buisana ka maikutlo le boitsebiso bo bong;

(b) tokoloho ea polelo ea bonono le lipatlisiso tsa saense le tsebetso; le

(c)

tokoloho ea thuto.

(2) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea tokoloho ea phatlalatso, e leng tokoloho e kenyeletsang tsireletso ea lekunutu la baqolotsi ba litaba.

(3) Phatlalatso ea litaba le melaetsa e meng ea eleketronike ea puisano e na le tokoloho ea ho theho ea, ho ea ka mekhoa ea ho ngolisa litokomane tsa Mmuso feela—

(a) ho hlokahala hore ho laoloe tsa liretio le mefuta e meng ea tsamaiso ea lipontso; me

(b) ba ikemetse ka taolo ke Mmuso kapa ka lithahaselo tsa lipolotiki kapa tsa khoebo.

(4) Mecha eohle ea litaba ea ho buisana ea Mmuso e tlameha ho—

(a) ba le bolokolohi ho ikhethela boipiletso ba lihlooho tsa liphatlalatso tsa tsona tse ling;

(b) ho se be le lehlakure; le

(c)

ho fumana monyetla o motle oa ho bontsa maikutlo a sieaneng le maikutlo a sa lumellaneng.

(5) Tokoloho ea ho bua le tokoloho ea mecha ea phatlalatso ha e kenyelletsoe—

(a) ho khothalletsa pefo;

(b) bo buoi ba lehloeo kapa puo ea lehloeo;

(c)

kotsi ho botho kapa botumo ba motho; kapa

(d) hlokofatso ea mpe kapa e sa lokelang ea motho ea ho ba lekunutu. 62

Ho fumana litaba

(1) Moahi e mong le e mong oa Zimbabwe kapa moahi ea lulang ka nako e sa lekanyetsoang, ho kopanya le molaoli oa litaba le mecha ea litaba ea Zimbabwe, o na le tokelo ea ho fumana boitsebiso leha e le bofe bo etsoang ke mmusi ka mokhatlo leha e le ofe oa Mmuso kapa mokhatlo o mong le o mong, ho fihlela boitsebiso bo hlokahala lithahasello tsa boikarabelo ba sechaba.

(2) Motho e mong le e mong, ho kopanya le mecha ea phatlalatso ea Zimbabwe, o na le tokelo ea ho fumana boitsebiso leha e le bofe bo etsoang ke motho leha e le ofe, ho kenyeletsa le Naha, ho fihlela boitsebiso bo sa fumanehe pakeng sa ho ikoetlisa kapa ho tsireletsa tokelo.

(3) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho lokisa tlhahisoleseding, kapa ho tlosoa tlhahisoleseding e fosahetseng, e fosahetseng kapa e khelosang, e etsoang ke Mmuso kapa mokhatlo ofe kapa ofe oaMmuso ka boemong leha e le bofe, ebile o amana le motho eo.

(4) Melao e tlameha ho etsoa ho nea ka matla ho tokelo ena, empa e ka thibela ho fihlella hlahisoleseding ho ameha ho tsireletsa, tsireletso ea sechaba kapa boikarabelo ba litsebi, ho fihlela tekanyetso e le e loketseng, e utloahalang, e hlokahalang le e nepahetseng mokhatlong oa demokrasi ho itsetlehile ka bolokolohi, toka, seriti sa botho, ho lekana le tokoloho. 63

Puo le setso Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo—

(a) ho sebelisa puo eo a ikhethileng; le

(b) ho kenya letsoho bophelong ba setso sa khetho ea bona; empa ha ho motho ea sebelisang litokelo tsena a ka etsa joalo ka tsela e sa lumellaneng le Khaolo ena.


Tokoloho ea botsebi, khoebo kapa mosebetsi

Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho khetha le ho etsa mosebetsi leha e le ofe, khoebo kapa mosebetsi, empa mokhoa oa mosebetsi, khoebo kapa mosebetsi o ka laoloa ke molao. 65

Litokelo tsa mosebeletsi

(1) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho sebetsa ka mokhoa o nepahetseng le o bolokehileng le ho neoa moputso o tsoanetsing.

(2) Ntle ha basebeletsi ba tsa tsireletso, motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho theha le ho kopanela mekhatlo ea basebeletsi le mekhatlo ea basebeletsi kapa bahiri ba khethiloeng, le ho nka karolo mesebetsing ea molao ea mekhatlo.

(3) Ntle ha basebeletsi ba tsa litsebeletso tsa tsireletso, mosebeletsi e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho kenya letsoho mosebetsing o kopanetsoeng mosebetsing, ho kenyelletsa le tokelo ea ho otla, ho lula, ho tlosa mosebetsi oa bona le ho nka bohato bo bong bo tsoanang, empa molao o ka thibela mosebeletsi ea tokelo ena e le hore a boloke litsebeletso tsa bohlokoa.

(4) Mosebeletsi e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho sebetsa ka toka, maemo a lekana le a khotsofatsang a mosebetsi.

(5) Ntleha basebeletsi ba tsa litsebeletso tsa tsireletso, mosebeletsi e mong le e mong, mokhatlo oa mohiri kapa basebeletsi o na le tokelo ea ho—

(a)

ho kopanela moqoqong o kopanetsoeng;

(b) hlophisa; le

(c)

ho theha le ho kopanela mekhatlo ea mekhatlo le mekhatlo e joalo.

(6) Basali le banna ba na le tokelo ea ho fumana moputso o lekanang pakeng sa mosebetsi o tsoanang.

(7) Basebeletsi ba basali ba na le tokelo ea ho ba le phomolo e feletseng ea bokhachane ka nako e ka etsang likhoeli tse tharo. 66

Tokoloho ea ho tsamaea le ho lula

(1) Moahi e mong le e mong oa Zimbabwe o na le—

(a)

tokelo ea ho kena Zimbabwe;

(b) ho itsireletsa ho ho lelekoa Zimbabwe; le

(c)

2013

tokelo ea pasepoto kapa lengolo le leng la maeto.

(2) Moahi e mong le e mong oa Zimbabwe le motho e mong le e mong ea lumelloang ka molao Zimbabwe o na le tokelo ea ho—

(a) tsamaea ka tokoloho hare ha lefatse Zimbabwe;

(b) lula karolong efe kapa efe ea Zimbabwe; le

(c) 67

ho tloha Zimbabwe.

Litokelo tsa lipolotiki

(1) Moahi e mong le e mong oa Zimbabwe o na le tokelo—

(a) ho khetha le ho khetho ea ho likhetho tse sa lefelloeng, tse hlokang leeme pakeng sa ofisi leha e le efe ea sechaba e khethiloeng ho latela Molaotheo ona kapa molao o mong; le

(b) ho etsa khetho ea lipolotiki ka bolokolohi.

(2) Ho latela Molaotheo ona, moahi e mong le e mong oa Zimbabwe o na le tokelo—

(a) ea ho theha, ho kopanela le ho kenya letsoho ho tsa lipolotiki kapa mokhatlo ba ikhethileng;

(b) ho tsebisa batho ka bolokolohi le ka khotso pakeng sa mokhoa oa lipolotiki kapa ho baka sesosa;

(c) ho kopanela mosebetsing oa lipolotiki oa khotso; le

(d) ho nka karolo, ka bomong kapa ka kakaretso, mekopanong kapa lihlopha kapa ka tsela leha e le efe, ka mesebetsi e nang le khotso e tla susumetsa, ho phephetsa kapa ho tsehetsa maano a Mmuso kapa leha e le efe ea lipolotiki kapa lebaka leha e le lefe.

(3) Ho latela Molao-motheo ona, moahi e mong le e mong oa Zimbabwe ea nang le mengoaha tse ka holimo ho leshome le metso e robeli o na le tokelo—

(a) ho khetha likhethong tsohle le li-referendamo tseo Molaotheo ona kapa molao o mong o sebetsang ho tsona, le ho o etsa se, sephiring; le

(b) ho ema pakeng sa likhetho tsa ofisi ea sechaba ebile, ha o khethiloe, o tsoare ofisi e joalo.

(4) Ka morero oa ho tsehetsa temokrasi, Molao oa Palamente o lokela ho nea ka tsehetso ea mekhatlo ea lipolotiki. 68

Tokelo ea ho reroa ha melato

(1) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea tsamaiso ea molao e amohelehang, e potlakileng, e sebetsang hantle, e nang le kahlolo e molemo, e lekanang, e se nang leeme ebile e le ea bohlokoa ka tsela e lokileng.

(2) Motho leha e le ofe ea nang le tokelo ea hae, tokoloho, thahasello kapa tibello ea hae e nepahetseng a amehilehampe ka boitsoaro ba tsamaiso o na le tokelo ea ho neoa nako le ka ho ngola mabaka a boitsoaro.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o tlameha ho sebelisa litokelo tsena, ebile o tsoanetsi ho—

(a)

nea ka hlahlobo ea tsamaiso ea lekhotla kapa, ha ho loketse, ka lekhotla le ikemetseng le le se nang leeme;

(b) ho qobella Mmuso hore o sebetse ka litokelo litemaneng (1) le (2); le

(c)

ho ntshetsa pele tsamaiso e nepahetseng.

Tokelo ea tsekiso e tsoanetseng

(1) Motho e mong le e mong ea qosoa ka tlolo ea molao o na le tokelo ea ho qosoa ka toka le phatlalatsa ka nako e loketseng peleha lekhotla le ikemetseng le le se nang leeme.

(2) Ha ho etsoa boikarabelo ba litokelo le boikarabelo ba sechaba, motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ho kahlolo e nepahetseng, e potlakileng le ka nako e loketseng peleha lekhotla le e kemetseng le le se nang leeme, lekhotla le leng kapa seboka se thehiloeng ke molao.

(3) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho kena makhotleng, kapa lekhotla le leng le le leng kapa se se thehiloeng ke molao pakeng sa qeto ea phehisano leha e le efe.

(4) Motho e mong le e mong o na lse tokelo, ka litsenyehelo tsa hae, ho khetha le ho emisoa ke ngaka ea molao peleha lekhotla le le leng. 70

Litokelo tsa batho ba roesoang melato

(1) Motho leha e le ofe o ea qosoa ka phoso o na le litokelo tse latelang—

(a) ho nkoang a se na molato ho fihlela a pakoa;

(b) ho tsebisoa ka ho phakisa molatooa hae ka boitlalo hore ho be bonolo ho araba;

(c) ho neoa nako le lisebelisoa tse lekaneng ho lokisetsa tsireletso;

(d) ho khetha ngaka ea molao le ho emeloa ke ngaka eo;

(e) ho emeloa ke ngaka ea molao e neoang ke Mmuso le litsenyehelo tsa Mmuso ha e ba ho hloka toka ho ka bang teng;

(f) ho tsebisoa ka potlako litokelo tse neoang ke lirapa (d) le (e).

(g) ho ba teng ha a nyeoa;

(h) ho oketsa le ho hanetsa bopaki;

(i) ho khutsa le ho se pakele kapa ho qobelloa ho nea bopaki ba ho ikemela;

(j) ho etsa hore nyeoe ea nyeoe e fetoleloe ka puo eo ba e utloisisang;

(k) hore a se ke a ahloleloa ketso kapa ho se hlaseloe ho o e neng e se phoso ha e etsahala;

(l) hore a se ke a ahloloa ka ketso e itseng kapa e sa etseng phoso;

(m) hore ba se ke ba qosoa pakeng sa tlolo ea molao mabapi le ketso kapa ketso eo ba kileng ba tsoareloa ka eona kapa ba lokolotsoe kapa ba fumanoa ba le molato ka melemo;

(n) ho ahloleloa kotlo e fokolisitsoeng ha eba kotlo e beiloeng pakeng sa tlolo ea molao e fetohile pakeng tsa nako eo nyeoe e entsoeng le nako ea kahlolo.

(2) Moo karolo ena e hlokang hore boitsebiso bo neoe motho—

(a) boitsebiso bo tlameha ho etsoa ka puo eo motho a e utloisisang; le

(b) ha eba motho a sitoa ho bala kapa ho ngola, tokomane leha e le efe e nang le hlahiso-leseling e tlameha ho hlalosoa ka tsela eo motho a e utloisisang.

(3) Nyeoeng efe kapa efe ea tlolo ea molao, bopaki bo fumanoeng ka mokhoa o khahlanong le tokisetso leha e le efe ea Khaolo ena e tlameha ho kenyelloa hae ba ho kenyaoa ha bopaki ho ka etsa hore nyeoe e se na toka kapa e ka ba kotsi ho tsamaiso ea toka kapa sechaba thahaselo.

(4) Motho leha e le ofe ea nyeoiloeng pakeng sa tlolo ea molao o na le tokelo, ha a lefa tefo e lekaneng e latetsoeng ke molao, o noea lengolo la tlaleho ea nyeoe ka nako e loketseng kamoraho hore kahlolo e neoe nyeoeng.

(5) Motho leha e le ofe ea lekiloeng le ea fumanoeng a le molato oa tlolo ea molao o na le tokelo, ho latela lithibelo tse utloahalang tse ka beoang ke molao, ho—

(a) nyeoe e hlahlojoa ke lekhotla la holimo; kapa

(b) boipiletso lekhotleng le holimo ho khahlanong le kholiseho le polelo. 71

Tokelo ea thepa

(1) Karolong ena— “melemo ea penshene” e bolela penshene, moputso le tse lingoe tse tsoanang kapa moputso o tsoanang o lefeoang—

(a) ho motho leha e le ofe ea tsoang Sekotleng sa Likoloto sa Sechaba;

(b) mabapi le tsebeletso ea motho le mohiri;

(c) mabapi le ho kula kapa ho lematsa ha motho; kapa

(d) malebana le ho tlohela mosebetsi ka nako ea lilemo kapa ho lula kapa mabaka afe kapa afe;

ebile e kenyeletsa phetoho ea penchene le tse li joalo, annuity, gratuity kapa monehelo le puseletso ea menehelo e lefelloeng pensheneng, moputso le tse lingoe tse joalo moputso; “Thutu” e bolela thepa ea hlaloso leha e le efe le tokelo leha e le efe kapa phaello ho thepa.

(2) Tumelloanong le karolo ea 72, motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho fumana, ho tsoara, ho sebelisa, ho fetisetsa, ho fetisa, ho hira kapa ho lahla mefuta eohle ea thutu, ka bomong kapa ka ho kopana le ba bang.

(3) Ho latela karolo ena le ho karolo ea 72, ha ho motho ea ka qobelloang ho lahleheloa ke thepa ea hae ntle lehae ba maemo a latelang a khotsofatsoa—

(a) ho hlokoa ho latela molao oa kopo ea kakaretso;

(b) ho hlekefetsoa ha hlokahala pakeng sa mabaka a latelang—

(i) molemong oa tsireletso, polokeho ea sechaba, sechaba sa sechaba, boitsoaro ba sechaba, bophelo bo botle ba sechaba kapa moralo le moralo oa naha; kapa

(ii) e le ho ntlafatsa kapa ho sebelisa thepa kapa thepa leha e le efe ka morero oa bohlokoa ho sechaba;

(c)

molao o hloka bolaoli ba ho fumana—

(i) ho nea ka leseli le utloahalang ka morero oa ho fumana thepa ho bohle ba nang le thahaselo kapa tokelo ea thepa eo ba tla e fumana;

(ii) ho lefa matsiliso a lekaneng le a lekaneng pakeng sa ho fumanoa pele a fumana thepa kapa ka nako e loketseng kamoraho hore a fumane; le

(iii) ha eba thepa e fumanoa e hanyetsanoa, ho kopa lekhotla le nang le boiphihlelo pele e fumana thepa, kapa kamoraho ha matsatsi a mashome a mararo kamoraho hore ho fumanoe thepa, pakeng sa taelo e netefatsang ho fumanoa;

(d) molao o lumella motho leha e le ofe eo thepa ea hae e fumanoeng ho sebetsa lekhotleng le nang le bokhone pakeng sa ho khutla kapeleha thepa ha eba lekhotla le sa tiise hore le fumanoe; ebile

(e)

molao o lumella moemeli leha e le ofe hore a fumane matsiliso ho kopa lekhotla le nang le Batlatsi pakeng sa boikemisetso ba—

(i) boteng, hlaho le bohlokoa ea thakatso ea bona mohahong o amehang;

(ii) molao oa ho hlekefetsoa; le

(iii) palo ea matsiliso ao ba nang le tokelo ea bona;

le ho kenya kopo lekhotleng pakeng sa taelo e lebisang tsebetso ea kapele ea matsiliso.

(4) Moo motho a nang le tokelo e nang le tokelo ea ho leneoa ha phaello ea penshene, molao o neang ka ho felisoa kapa ho fokotseha ha tokelo eo o nkoa, ka morero oa karolo ea (3), e le molao o neang ka ho qobelloa ho fumana thepa. 72

Litokelo tsa mobu oa temo

(1) Karolong ena— “mobu oa temo” ho bolela mobu o sebelisitsoeng kapa o loketseng temo, ke hore ho omela lilemo (horticulture, viticulture,) meru ea meru kapa meru kapa ka morero ofe kapa ofe oa ho lema, ho akaretsa—

(a) ho boloka kapa ho ikatisa liphoofolo, liphoofolo, likhoho, liruo kapa linotsi; kapa

(b) ho fulisoa ha liruo kapa liphoofolo;

empa ha e kenye Mobu oa Sechaba kapa mobu ka hara ha meeli ea boholong motseng kapa toropong e thehiloeng tlase ha molao e amanang le moralo oa toropo le oa naha kapa o hlalositsoe molaong o amanang le patlisiso ea mobu; “Naha” e kenyelletsa ntho leha e le efe e kenyelletsoeng kapa e hulang mobung; “Karoloana ea mobu oa temo” e bolela karolo ea mobu oa temo o ngoliloeng e le karolo e fapaneng ea mobu Lefapheng la Tsebeletso.

(2) Moo mobu oa temo, kapa tokelo kapa thahasello ho mobu o joalo, e hlokehang pakeng sa morero oa sechaba, ho kenyelletsa—

(a) ho rarola lihlahisoa tsa temo kapa merero e meng;

(b) tlhophiso ea mobu, meru, hlokomelo ea tikoloho kapa tsebeliso ea bophelo ba leholimo kapa lisebelisoa tse ling tsa hlaho; kapa

(c)

ho lulisoa ha batho ba lahliloeng ka lebaka la tsebeliso ea mobu ka morero o boletsoeng serapeng (a) kapa (b); naha, se nepahetseng kapa thahasello e ka ‘na ea qobelloa ho e fumana ke Mmuso ka tsebiso e phatlalalitsoeng ho Gazette e tsebang hore na mobu ke ofe, ho nepahetse kapa thahasello, kahoo naha, litokelo tse nepahetseng kapa tsa thahaselo Naheng e nang le sehlooho se feletseng ho tloha letsatsing la ho hatisoa ha hlokomela.

(3) Mobu oa temo, kapa tokelo leha e le efe kapa thahasello ho mobu o joalo, o qobelloe ka morero o boletsoeng karolong ea (2)—

(a) ho ea ka karolo 295 (1) le (2), ha ho na moputso o leneoang mabapi le ea kopo ea eona, ntle le liphetoho tse etsoang ho eona pele e fumanoa;

(b) ha ho motho a ka ngolelang lekhotleng pakeng sa qeto ea potso leha e le efe e amanang le matsiliso, ha ese pakeng sa matsiliso pakeng sa ntlafatso e entsoeng mobung pele e ba teng, ebileha ho lekhotla le ka amohelang kopo e joalo; ebile

(c)

horeka ho ka se ke ha e-ba le phephetso ka hobane e ne e le khethollo e khahlanong le karolo ea 56.

(4) Mobu o mong le o mong oa temo oo—

(a) o hlalositsoeng ho Lenaneo7 ho Molaotheo oa pele; kapa

(b) pele letsatsi le atlehileng, le khetholloa ho latela karolo ea 16B(2)(a)(ii) kapa (iii) ea Molaotheo oa pele; e tsoela pele ho neoa Mmuso, ebileha ho na matsiliso a neoang mabapi le ho e fumana ntle le liphetoho tse etsoang pele ho eona.

(5) Ha ho khoneha kamoraho ha hore mobu oa temo o fumanoe ho latela tumelloano ea (2), ofisi e ikarabelang pakeng sa ho ngolisoa ha mobu ka mobu, e lokela ho etsoa ka tumello e hlokahalang holimo ha lengolo le leng le le leng la boitsebiso le litlaleho ho ngolisa leha e le efe ka sepheo sa ho tlosa mosebetsi oa tlotla ka molao le ho ngolisa sehlooho sa Mmuso holimo ha naha.

(6) Molao oa Palamente o ka etsa hore e be tlolo ea molao ho motho leha e le ofe, ntle le matla a lumelloang ke molao, ho ba le mobu oa temo o boletsoeng karolong ena kapa mobu o mong oa Mmuso.

(7) Mabapi le ho qobelloa ho fumana mobu oa temo pakeng sa ho tsosolosoa ha batho ho ea ka lenaneo la phetoho ea mobu, lintlha tse latelang li lokela ho nkoa e le tsa bohlokoa ka ho fetisisa le tse fetang—

(a) tlase ha puso ea bokolone batho ba Zimbabwe ba ne ba le teng ho leleka naha ea bona le mehloli e meng ntle ho tefo;

(b) batho ba ile ba nka libetsa e le hore ba tsosolose naha ea bona le puso ea ba lipolotiki, ebile sena se ile sa fela ka Boipuso ba Zimbabwe ka1980;

(c)

batho ba Zimbabwe ba tlameha ho neoa matla a ho tsehetsa litokelo tsa bona le ho ba le matla ka mobu oa bona; ebile ka lebaka leo—

(i) matla a pele a bokolone a na le boikarabelo ba ho lefa matsiliso pakeng sa mobu oa temo o qobelloang ho fumana phaliso, ho etsoa ka chelete e lekaneng pakeng sa morero; le

(ii) ha eba matla a pele a bokolone a hloleha ho lefa matsiliso ka letlole lena, Mmuso oa Zimbabwe ha oa tlameha ho lefa moputso oa mobu oa temo o qobelloang ho fumana phaliso.

(8) Karolo ena e sebetsa ntle le ho hlokomoloha boikarabelo ba matla a pele a bokolone ho lefa matsiliso pakeng sa mobu o boletsoeng karolong ena e fumanoeng pakeng sa phalliso. 73

Tokelo ea tiokoloho

(1) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea—

(a) tokolohong e sa kotsi ho bophelo bo botle kapa boiketlo ba bona; le

(b) ho tsireletsa tikoloho molemong oa meloko e teng hona joale le e tlang, ka mehato e utloahalang ea melao le mehato e meng e latelang—

(i) ho thibela tšilafalo le ho tsilafala ha tikoloho;

(ii) tsebelisoang mobu; le

(iii) ho boloka tsoelopele e tsitsitseng ea tokoloho le tsebeliso ea lisebelisoa tsa hlaho ha ho ntse ho khothalletsa ntlafatso ea leruo le sechaba.

(2) Mmuso o tlameha ho nka mehato e loketseng ea molao le mehato e meng, ho latela meeli ea lisebelisoa tse fumanehang ho oona, ho finyella ts’ebetso e tsoelang pele ea litokelo tse boletsoeng karolong ena.

Bolokolohi ba ho lelekoa bolulong ka sekhahla

Ha ho motho ea ka lelekoang malapeng a bona, kapa a senyeheloa ke ntlo ea hae, ntle le taelo ea lekhotla kamoraho ha ho hlahloba maemo ohle a amehang. 75

Tokelo ea thuto

(1) Moahi e mong le e mong ea lulang Zimbabwe o lula a na le tokelo ea—

(a) thuto ea mantlha ea lichelete ea Mmuso ho kenyeletsoa thuto ea batho ba baholo; le

(b) ho tsoelapele ka thuto, eo Mmuso, ka mehato e utloahalang ea molao le mehato e meng, e tlameha ho etsoa ka mofuthu le ho fumaneha ha bonolo.

(2) Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho theha le ho boloka, ka litsenyehelo tsa bona, mekhatlo ea boipheliso ea thuto e nang le litekanyetso tse loketseng, ha feela ba sa khetholle mobu leha e le ofe o hanetsoeng ke Molaotheo ona.

(3) Molao o ka nea ka ho ngolisoa ha mekhatlo ea thuto e boletsoeng ho serapeng sa (2) le ho koaloa ha mekhatlo e joalo e sa sebetsaneng le mabaka a mangata a ho ngolisoa.

(4) Mmuso o tlameha ho nka mehato e loketseng molao le mehato e meng, ho latela meeli ea thepa e fumanehang ho eona, ho finyella ts’ebetsong e ntseng e tsoela pele ea tokelo e boletsoeng karolong ea (1). 76

Tokelo ea ho fumana bophelo bontle

(1) Moahi e mong le e mong ea lulang Zimbabwe o lula a na le tokelo ea ho fumana litsebeletso tsa mantlha tsa bophelo bo botle, ho kenyelletsa le litsebeletso tsa hlokomelo ea bophelo bo botle.

(2) Motho e mong le e mong ea phelang le boloetse bo sa foleng o na le tokelo ea ho fumana litsebeletso tsa mantlha tsa bophelo bo botle pakeng sa boloetse.

(3) Ha ho motho ea ka hanang ho ha nyetsoa kalafo ea meriana ea hlokomelo ea bophelo.

(4) Mmuso o tlameha ho nka mehato e loketseng ea molao le mehato e meng, ho latela meeli ea lisebelisoa tse fumanehang ho oona, ho finyella tsebetso e tsoelang pele ea litokelo tse boletsoeng karolong ena. 77

Tokelo ea ho fumana lijo le metsi Motho e mong le e mong o na le tokelo ea ho—

(a) metsi a sireletsehileng, a hloekileng le a roalehang

(b) lijo tse lekaneng; empa Mmuso o tlameha ho nka mehato e utloahalang ea melao le mehato e meng, ho latela meeli ea thepa e fumanehang ho eona, ho finyella tsebetso e tsoelang pele ea tokelo ena.


Litokelo tsa lenyalo

(1) Motho e mong le e mong ea fihlisitseng lilemo tse leshome le metso e robeli o na le tokelo ea ho fumana lelapa.

(2) Ha ho motho ea ka qobelloa ho kena lenyalong khahlanong le thato ea bona.

(3) Batho ba bong bo tsoanang ba thibetsoe ho nyalana.

KAROLO 3 Hlaloso Ea Li Tokelo Tse Li Ngoe 79

Ho sebelisoa ha Karolo 3

(1) Karolo ena e hlakisa litokelo le tokoloho tse itseng ho netefatsa hore litokelo le bolokolohi li sebelisoa ho batho ba itseng.

(2) Karolo ena ha ea lokela ho nkoa e le ho fokotsa tokelo kapa tokoloho leha e le efe e hlalositsoeng Karolong ea 2. 80

Litokelo tsa basali

(1) Mosali e mong le e mong o na le seriti se feletseng le se lekanang sa motho oa monna empa sena se akaretsa menyetla e lekanang le lipolotiking, moruo le sechabeng le mesebetsi.

(2) Basali ba na le tokelo e tsoanang le ea banna mabapi le hlokomelo le hlokomelo ea bana, empa Molao oa Palamente o ka laola hore litokelo tseo li lokela ho sebelisoa joang.

(3) Melao eohle, meetlo, litloaelo le mekhoa ea meetlo e fapaneng le litokelo tsa basali ba neoang ke Molaotheo ona ha li na thuso ho isa tekanyong ea tlolo ea molao. 81

Litokelo tsa bana

(1) Ngoana e mong le e mong, ke hore mosemana le monyana e mong le e mong ea ka tlase ho lilemo tse robeli, o na le tokelo—

(a) ho tsoaroa ka tsela e lekanang peleha molao, ho kenyeletsa le tokelo ea ho utloahala;

(b) ho neoa lebitso le lebitso la lelapa;

(c) tabeng ea ngoana ea—

(i) hlahetseng Zimbabwe; kapa

(ii) tsoaletsoeng ka ntle ho Zimbabwe le moahi oa Zimbabwe ka moloko; tokisetsong e potlakileng ea lengolo la lengolo la tsoalo;

(d) ho hlokomela lelapa kapa hlokomelo ea batsoali, kapa hlokomelo e loketseng ha e tlosoa tokolohong ea lelapa;

(e) ho tsireletsoa licheleteng tsa moruo le tlhlekefetso ea thobalano, ho tloha mosebetsing oa bana, le ho tsoa tlokotsing, ho hlokomolohuoa kapa mofuta ofe kapa ofe oa tlhekefetso;

(f) thuto, litsebeletso tsa bophelo bo botle, phepo e nepahetseng le bolulo;

(g) hore a se ke a kenyoa sesoleng sa sesole kapa a kenella ntoeng kapa lintoeng;

(h) ho se qobellehe ho kenya letsoho mosebetsing ofe kapa ofe oa lipolotiki; le

(i) ho sa tsoaroe ha e se feela ka tekanyo ea ho qetela le, hae ba e koaletsoe—

(i) ho koalloa ka nako e khutsoanyane;

(ii) ho bolokoa ka thuko ho batho ba tsoeroeng ho feta lilemo tse leshome le metso e robeli; le

(iii) ho tsoaroa ka mokhoa o itseng, le ho boloka maemo, a nahanang ka lilemo tsa ngoana.

(2) Lithahasello/litakatso tsa ngoana li bohlokoa ka ho fetisisa tabeng engoe le engoe e amanang le ngoana.

(3) Bana ba na le tokelo ea ho tsireletsoa ka ho lekaneng ke makhotla, haholoholo ke Lekhotla la holimo e le mohlokomeli oa bona. 82

Litokelo tsa ba tsofetseng Batho ba fetang lilemo tse mashome a supileng ba na le tokelo—

(a) ho fumana hlokomelo le thuso e tsoang malapeng a bona le ho Mmuso;

(b) ho fumana hlokomelo ea bophelo le thuso ea bongaka ho tsoa ho Mmuso; le

(c)


ho fumana tsehetso ea lichelete ka tsela ea tsireletso le boiketlo ba sechaba; ebile Mmuso e tlameha ho nka melao ea molao le eohle mehato, ho latela meeli ea thepa e fumanehang ho eona, ho finyella tsebetso e tsoelang pele ea tokelo ena.

Litokelo tsa batho ba nang le bokooa
