(b) sechaba, ka mehato ea molao le mehato e meng, e tlameha ho thusa le ho tsireletsa likhotla ho netefatsa boipuso, ho se khethulle, seriti, le ho sebetsa ka tsoanelo le bonnete ba hore li latela likemiso tsa melao e leng ho karolo 165.

(3) Taelo kapa morero oa lekhotla o tlama Mmusole batho baona bohle le mekhatlo ea mebuso le mekhatlo e sebetsang ho eona, ebile e tlameha ho mamela lentsoe.

(4) Ha ho letho le le leng karolong ee le ka thibelang Molao oa Palamente ho nea mesebetsi e se leng e laoloang ke tsamaiso ea likhotla, ho ea ka hore mosebetsi oa teng ha ho senye bona boipuso ba sesebeletsi hore se etse mosebetsi oa sona o senye boikemelo ba boahloli ka kakaretso. 165 Melao e laolang balaoli ba melao

(1) Ha ho sebelisoa matla a boahloli, baahloli ba tsoanetsi ho laoloa ke melao e lateng—

(a)

toka e tsoanetsi ho etsoa ho bohle, ho se na eng le boemo ba bona;

(b) toka e tsoanetsi ho lateloa, ka mong maloko ohle a sebetsang ka tsa molao a tsoanetse ho phetha mesebetsi ea bona ea boahloli ka mokhoa o nepahetseng le ka potlako;

(c)

mesebetsi ea likhotla e bohlokoa ho tsireletsa litokelo le li tokoloho le puso ea molao.

(2) Maloko a balaoli ba molao, ka bo mong le ka kakaretso, a tsoanetsi ho hlompha le ho tlotla ofisi ea bona ea boahloli joale ka bokopano ba sechaba ebile a tsoanetsi ho e tlama ho phahamisa boikemelo hobane a tsepehe ka nako tsohle peleha sechaba mosebetsing oa bolaoli ba molao.

(3) Ha ha etsoa morero oa molao, leloko la baahloli le tsoanetsi ho sebetsa le lokolohile ho sena tsutsumetso e sa lokelang mosebetsi.

(4) Maloko a tsamaiso ea molao ha tsoanele ho—

(a)

kopanela mesebetsing ea lipolotiki;

(b) ba le bolulo ba ofisi kapa ho tsa mekhatlo ea lipolitiki;

(c)

2013

kopa kapa ho nea chelete ho mekhatlo ea lipolotiki; kapa

(d) ho ea mekopanong ea lipolitiki.

(5) Maloko a likhotla ha tsoanele ho kopa kapa ho amohela mpho leha e le efe, ho neoa, ho kolota kapa ho ba le mohau o ka susumetsang boitsoaro ba bona ba boahloli kapa ho bonahatse ho sebetsa ka tsela e sa lokang.

(6) Maloko a likhotla a tsoanetsi ho e tlama mosebetsing ho feta eohle, e bileha ba tsoanele ho ba le liketso tse tla senyang mosebetsi oa boahloli.

(7) Maloko a likhotla ba tsoanetsi ho etsa ntho tse li tsoanetseng hore ba boloke le ho supa tsebo e phahameng ka mosebetsi oa bona, e bile a tsoanetse ho ba le tsebo ka tse li etsahalang naheng ea bona le ho tsa mafatse a mangoe ka tsa molao ka nako tsohle. 166 Lekhotla la molaotheo

(1) Lekhotla la molaotheo ke lekhotla le leng holimo ha oohle le bolokang litaba le phahameng la tlaleho ebile le na le—

(a)

Moahloli e Moholo le Motlatsi oa Moahloli; le

(b) baahloli ba bang ba bahlano ba Lekhotla la Molaotheo;

(2) Ha e ba mesebetsi ea moahloli a sebetsa mong a seo a hlokahala ho Lekhotla la Molaotheo ha nako e etseng, Moahloli e Moholo a ka khetha moahloli kapa moahloli oa pele hore a be moahloli oa Lekhotla la molao ha sebaka se.

(3) Melato e sekoang ho Lekhotla la molaotheo—

(a)

mabapi le ho qosoa ha litokelo tsa bohlokoa le litokoloho ea motho kapa tokoloho e boletsoeng ho khaolong 4, kapa tse lebaneng le khetho ea Mookameli kapa Motlatsi oa Mookameli, e tsoanetseng hore baahloli bohle ba Lekhotla ba e utloe;

(b) ntleha litaba tse li leng ho tema (a), e tsoanetsi hore e sekoe peleha baahloli ba Lekhotla ba ka ba ba bararo kapa ho feta; empa Molao oa Palamente kapa melao ea likhotla e ka nea hore litabeng tse ho be le ho buisana le baahloli ba Lekhotla.

(4) Baahloli kapa baahloli ba pele ba khethetsoeng ho sebetsa ba latela ha karoloana (2) ba ka tsoelapele ho lula le baahloli ba Likhotla tsa Molaotheo kamoraho hore ba khethoe, ka morero oa ho sebetsana le nyeoe leha e le efe e qalileng ka peleha bona ha ba ntse ba etsa joalo. 167 Matla a Lekhotla la Molaotheo

(1) Lekhotla la molaotheo—

(a)

ke lekhotla la holimo ha tsohle ho tsamaiso ea litaba tsohle tsa molaotheo, le horera ka litaba tsa likhotla;

(b) e rera fela ka litaba tsa molao-motheo le litaba tse amanang le merero e tsamaelanang le litaba tsa oona, haholo-holo litsupo tse li leng ho karolo 131(8)(b) le tema 9(2) tse li leng ho Lenaneo la Bohlano;

(c)

ke eona e phethang litaba e kaba tsa molaotheo kapa morero ka litaba tsa molao.

(2) Ha ho ea ka tse neiloeng ke Molaotheo oo, ke eona fela Lekhotla e ka—

(a)

lemosang ka tsamaiso ea molaotheo ka melao e tsoanetsing ho lateloa, empa o ka etsa joalo fela ha ho lateloa tse aroloang ke Molaotheo oo;

(b) e laolang ka litaba tsa khetho ea ho khetha Mookameli;

(c)

e laolang ka litsekisano tsa hore motho a ka ba a tsoanetsi ho ba Motlatsi oa Mookameli; kapa

(d) ho laola hore Palamente kapa Mookameli a ka ba a hlolehile ho phetha boikarabelo ba molao.

(3) Lekhotla la Motheotheo le laola hore na Molao oa Palamente kapa boitsoaro ba Mookameli kapa Palamente bo ka ba bo tsamaelana le litaelo tsa molaotheo, e bile o tsoanetsi ho hlokomela hore tse sa tsamaelaneng le molao o neiloeng a li ka se sebetsi.

(4) Molao oa Palamente o ka neoa tsebeliso ea bolaoli ke Lekhotla la molaotheo ebile ka lebaka le o ka ba le matla a ho etsa melao ea lekhotla.

(5) Ha e le hore ho loketsi ho thusa Lekhotla la Molaotheo, molao oa Lekhotla la molaotheo o tsoanetsi ho lamolela motho—

(a)

ho rula taba ka Molaotheo Khotleng ea Molaotheo;

(b) ho ipiletsa ka ho toba ho khotla la Molaotheo ho tsoa ho lekhotla

(c)

efe le efe;

(d) ho hlaha joale ka motsoalle oa lekhotla. 168 Lekhotla la Holimo

(1) Lekhotla le ka Holimo-limo ke lekhotla le phahameng la tlaleho ebile le na le—

(a)

Moahloli e Moholo le Motlatsi oa Moahloli;

(b) ha ho na baahloli ba bang ba babeli ba Lekhotla le Phahameng; le

(c)

baahloli leha e le bafe ba e ketsehileng ba beiloeng tlase ha serapa sa (2).

(2) Ha e ba litsebeletso tsa moahloli e mong li hlokeha Lekhotleng le ka Holimolimo ka nako e lekanyelitsoeng, Moahloli e Moholo a ka khetha moahloli oa Lekhotla le Phahameng, kapa moahloli oa pele e le moahloli oa Lekhotla le Phahameng ka nako eo.

(3) Baahloli kapa baahloli ba pele ba khethetsoeng ho sebetsa tlase ha serapa sa

(2) ba ka na ba tsoela pele ho lula e le baahloli ba Lekhotla la Holimo-limo kamoraho ha hore ba khethoe, ka morero oa ho sebetsana le linyeoe tsohle tse qalileng ka peleha bona ha li ntse li etsa joalo. 169 Matla a Lekhotla la holimo-limo

(1) Taolo ea Lekhotla la holimo-limo ke ea bofelo Zimbabwe, kantleha e ba ke litaba tse li leng tlase ha taolo ea Lekhotla la holimo-limo.

(2) Ho latela tumelloano ea (1), Molao oa Palamente o ka ba le matla a okelitsoeng holimo ha matla a Lekhotla le ka Holimo-limo.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o ka nea ka tsamaiso ea matla a Lekhotla la holimolimo o ka nea morero le matla a ho etsa melao ea lekhotla.

(4) Melao ea lekhotla e ka nea mongoli oa Lekhotla la holimo-limo kapa mang ho lateloa molao oa Lekhotla o sebetsang ka litaba tsa sechaba—

(a)

2013

ho etsa litaelo tse hanyetsanoang, kantleha litaelo tse ferekanyang tokoloho ea motho leha e le mang;

(b) ho etsa qeto ea litaba tsa pele kapa tsa ho kena-kenana, ho kenyelletsa le mekhoa ea lihlahiso, empa e seng litaba tse amang tokoloho ea motho leha e le ofe; empa melao e tlameha ho fa motho leha e le ofe ea amehang ke taelo ea mongoli kapa qeto ea hore o na le tokelo ea ho e hla ke moahloli oa Lekhotla le ka Holimo-limo, ea ka e tiisang, ea e fetolang kapa ea le bea ka thuko kapa ea neang ka taelo leha e le efe kapa qeto eo ae nahanang e loketse. Lekhotla la holimo la. 170 Lekhotla la Holimo Lekhotla la Holimo ke lekhotla la holimo la tlaleho ebile le na le—

(a)

Moahloli e Moholo, Motlatsi oa Mookameli le Motlatsi oa Moahloli oa Lekhotla la holimo; le

(b) baahloli ba bang ba Lekhotla la holimo ba ka khetho ea nako le nako. 171 Matla a Lekhotla la holimo

(1) Lekhotla la holimo—

(a)

le na le matla a ho laola litaba tsohle tsa sechaba le tsa tlolo ea molao ho Zimbabwe eohle;

(b) le na le matla a ho laola likhotla tsa bo mastrata le likhotla tse li leng tlase ha le ho hlahloba liqeto tsa tsona;

(c)

le ka ntse ea nea phetha litaba tse tsamaelanang le Molaotheo ka ntleha tse li tsoanetseng ho phetho ea ho lateloa molao oa Lekhotla la molaotheo;

(d) e na e ka nea tsamaiso ea lingongoreho joale ka kemiso ea Molao oa Palamente.

(2) Molao oa Palamente o ka nea mekhano ea ho sebelisoa ha matla ke Lekhotla la holimo ebile ka lebaka le o ka ba le matla oa ho laola ka ho bopa melao ea Lekhotla.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o ka etsa hore Lekhotla la holimo e arohane ka likarohano tse khethehileng, empa karohano e joalo e tlameha ho ba le matla a ho sebelisa le khotla e kholo ea Lekhotla la holimo ka litaba tse seaneng.

(4) Melao ea khotla e ka nea mongoli oa khotla e matla ho motlalehi oa matla a Lekhotla la holimo litabeng tsa sechaba—

(a)

ho etsa litaelo linyeoeng tse sa hanyetsanoang, ntle le litaelo tse amanang le boemo kapa hlokomelo ea bana;

(b) ho etsa qeto ea litaba tsa pele kapa tsa ho kena-kenana, ho kenyelletsa le mekhoa ea lihlahiso, empa e seng litaba tse amang tokoloho ea motho leha e le ofe; empa melao e tlameha ho fa motho leha e le ofe ea amehang ke taelo ea mongolisi kapa qeto ea hore o na le tokelo ea hore e hlahlojoe ke moahloli oa Lekhotla la holimo, ea ka e tiisang, ae fetola kapa ae bea ka thõko kapa a nea ka taelo efe kapa efe eo a nahanang ka eona e loketse. 172 Lekhotla la tsa Basebeletsi

(1) Lekhotla Ea Basebeletsi ke lekhotla ea tlaleho ebile e na le—

(a)

moahloli o moholo; le.

(b) Baahloli ba bang ba khotla ea Basebeletsi ba ka khethoa ea nako le nako. [Karoloana e fetoletsoe ka karolo 3 ea Molao 10 oa 2017]

(2) ekhotla ea Basebeletsi e na le matla a joalo holimo ha litaba tsa mosebetsi le mosebeletsi tse ka neoang ho eona ka Molao oa Palamente.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o ka nea ka tsebeliso ea bolaoli ke Lekhotla la Basebeletsi ebile ka morero ona o ka nea ka matla a ho etsa melao ea lekhotla. 173 Lekhotla le laolang ka Mesebetsi

(1) Lekhotla ea Tsamaiso ea Matla ke khotla ekholo e akaretsang—

(a)

moahloli o moholo; le

(b) Baahloli ba bang ba khotla ea Tsamaiso ba ka khethoa ka nako le nako. [Karoloana e fetoletsoe ka karolo 4 ea Molao 10 oa 2017]

(2) Khotla ea Tsamaiso e na le matla a joalo holimo ha litaba tsa tsamaiso joalo kaha ho ka ba ho neiloe ke Molao oa Palamente.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o ka neoa mekhano ea ho sebelisa matla ho lateloa tse neiloeng ke Lekhotla ea Tsamaiso ea Matla. 174 Makhotla le a mangoe

(1) Molao oa Palamente o ka ba le kemiso,ha ho bopa ka mong le ho nea mekhano ea tsebeliso ea matla a—

(a)

Likhotla tsa mastrata, ho ahlola liqoso tsa sechaba le tsa tlolo ea molao;

(b) Likhotla tsa molao oa setso enang le matla o sebetsang ho lateloa molao oa setso;

(c)

Likhotla tse ling tse laoloang ke Lekhotla la holimo; le

(d) Likhotla tsa ho lokisisa, ho kenela litabeng ka mong le ho thusa e meng mefuta ea mekhoa ho lokisisa mong ho na le tsekisano.

(2) Tumelelanong le karolo 188(4) ea Molaotheo, meputso, meputso le lingoe tse ka neoang baahloli ba Khotla ea Basebetsi le Khotla ea Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsi ba leng mosebetsing kapa ba sebetsang ba emetsi beng ba litulo kamong ka letsatsi la ho qala ha ho sebetsa ha Molao o ka se fokotsoe. [Efetotsoe ka karolo 5 ea Molao 10 oa 2017]

175 Matla a likhotla ho litaba tsa Molaotheo

(1) Moo lekhotla e tse tsoanetseng ka ho selateloe ha molao leha o ka ba ofe, e ka ba o tsoang ho Mookameli kapa Palamente, melao ha e na matla kantle ha e le tumelloano le Lekhotla la molaotheo.

(2) Lekhotla le etsang hore taolo ea molao e sa sebetsanoeng le molao-motheo e boletsoeng ho serapeng (1) e ka nea ka taelo ea nakoana kapa thuso e itseng ea nakoana moketeng, kapa e ka emisa nyeoe, e ntse e emetse qeto ea Lekhotla la Motheo oa Molao ka ho nepahala ha molao kapa boitsoaro bo amehang.

(3) Motho leha e le ofe ea nang le thahasello e lekaneng a ka ipiletsa, kapa a kenya kopo ka ho toba ho Lekhotla la Motheo oa Molao ho tiisa kapa ho fapana le taelo mabapi le molao-motheo oa lekhotla ho latela tumelloano ea (1).

(4) Ha eba taba ea molaotheo e hlaha ka litsamaiso tse ling pele ha khotla, motho a okametseng lekhotla a ka etsa joalo, ebile ha e ba ho kopa motho e mong le e mong ho litsamaiso tse, o lokela ho fetisetsa taba eo ho Lekhotla la Molaotheo ntle le hore ha a nahana hore kopo ea hae e bohlokoa.

(5) Molao oa Palamente kapa melao oa lekhotla o ka tlameha ea Lekhotla la Motheo oa Molao oa taelo mabapi le ho se sebetse ha molao-motheo o entsoeng ka tatellano (1) ke lekhotla le leng ntle le Lekhotla la Molao e seng oa Molaotheo.

(6) Ha ho phetho ea taba tsa molaotheo li leng ho matla a eona lekhotla le ka—

(a)

phatlalatsa hore molao kapa boitsoaro leha e le bofe bo sa lumellaneng le Molaotheo ha bo sebetse ho ea ka ho se lumellane ha teng le molao;

(b) ho etsa taelo leha e le efe e nepahetseng le e lekanang, ho akaretsa e fokotsang matla a tumelloano e kile ea ba teng ho eso ho ruloi mo ho le pepeneneng ka ho sa sebetsa ha tse ha sebaka se e tseng ho nea sebaka sa molaoli a na le tsebo hore a lokisise mo ho senyehileng. 176 Matla a Lekhotla la Molaotheo, Lekhotla la Holimo-limo le Lekhotla la Holimo Lekhotla la Molaotheo, Lekhotla la Holimo-limo le Lekhotla la Holimo le na le matla a sa feleng a ho tsireletsa le ho laola ho sebetsa ha oona ka mong le ho matlafatsa molao o tloaelehileng kapa molao oa tloaelo, ho hlokomeloa tsamaiso, nahanisiso ea toka kapa molao ka tsoanelo le tse neiloeng ho Molaotheo o na. KAROLO 2 Ho Hiroa ha Balaoli ba Molao Le nako e Tsoanetsing ea ho Sebetsa 177 Litsoanelo tsa mosebetsi oa moahloli oa Lekhotla la Molaotheo

(1) Motho o tsoanetsi ho khetho ea ho ba moahloli oa Lekhotla la Molaotheo ha eba e le moahi oa Zimbabwe, a ka bang le mengoaha e mashome a mane e bile a nang le tsebo ka botlalo ka molao oa molaotheo, ebile, holimo ha mong a be le litsoanelo tse latelang—

(a)

o kile a ba moahloli oa lekhotla le nang le matla a se nang moeli litabeng tsa sechaba kapa tsa tlolo ea molao naheng e nang le molao o tloaelehileng oa Roman-Dutch kapa oa Senyesemane, le hore Senyesemane ke puo e amohelehang ka molao; kapa

(b) ha mengoaha leshome le metso emebeli, leha a ne a sebetsa ka mehla eohle kapa che, o ne a tsoanetsi ho sebetsa litaba tsa molao—

(i) Zimbabwe; kapa

(ii) naheng molao o tloaelehileng e le Roman-Dutch kapa Senyesemane le Senyesemane e le puo e amohelehileng ho le molaong; ebile le joale o tsoanetsi ho sebetsa.

(2) Hore motho a khethoe ho ba moahloli oa Lekhotla la Molaotheo o tsoanetsi ho ba motho a philileng hantle hape a tsoanetsing mosebetsi oa boahloli.

178 Litsoanelo tsa mosebeletsi oa moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo-limo

(1) Motho o tsoanela ho khetheloa mosebetsi oa ho ba moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo-limo ha e ba ke moahi oa Zimbabwe a nang le mengoaha e ka bang e mashome a manne, holimo ha mong, hape(a)

o kile a ba moahloli oa lekhotla le nang le matla a se nang moeli litabeng tsa sechaba kapa tsa tlolo ea molao naheng e nang le molao oa RomanDutch kapa oa Senyesemane, le hore Senyesemane ke puo e amohelehang ka molao; kapa

(b) ha mengoaha e leshome, leha a ne a sebetsa ka mehla eohle kapa che, o ne a tsoanetsi ho sebetsa litaba tsa molao—

(i) Zimbabwe; kapa

(ii) naheng molao o tloaelehileng e le Roma-Dutch kapa Senyesemane le Senyesemane e le puo e amohelehileng ho le molaong; ebile le joale o tsoanetsi ho sebetsa.

(2) Hore motho a khethoe ho ba moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo-limo o tsoanetsi ho ba motho a philileng hantlehape a tsoanetsing mosebetsi oa boahluli. 179 Tse hlokahalang hore motho a be Moahloli oa Khotla ea Holimo, Khotla ea Mesebetsi ea tse ka etsoang ka Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsi.

(1) Motho o tsoanela ho khetheloa mosebetsi oa ho ba moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo, Lekhotla la Basebeletsi kappa Lekhotla la Tsamaiso ea Matla ha e ba o na le mengoaha e ka—

(a)

o kile a ba moahloli oa lekhotla le nang le matla a se nang moeli litabeng tsa sechaba kapa tsa tlolo ea molao naheng e nang le molao oa RomanDutch kapa oa Senyesemane, le hore Senyesemane ke puo e amohelehang ka molao; kapa

(b) ha mengoaha e supileng, leha a ne a sebetsa ka mehla eohle kapa che, o ne a tsoanetsi ho sebetsa litaba tsa molao—

(i) Zimbabwe; kapa

(ii) naheng molao o tloaelehileng e le Roma-Dutch kapa Senyesemane le Senyesemane e le puo e amohelehileng ho le molaong;

(iii) ha e ba ke moahi oa Zimbabwe, naheng mo molao o tloaelehileng ke oa Senyesemane kapa le Senyesemane ke puo e amohelehileng mo hole molaong; ebile le joale o tsoanetsi ho sebetsa.

(2) Hore motho a khethoe ho ba moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo, Lekhotla la Basebeletsi le Lekhotla la Tsamaiso ea Matla o tsoanetsi ho ba motho a phElileng hantle hape a tsoanetsing mosebetsi oa boahloli. 180 Ho khetho ea ha baahloli

(1) Moahloli o Moholo, Motlatsi oa Moohloli o Moholo, le Mookameli oa Baahloli ba Khotla ea Holimo le bohle baahloli ba bang ba khetheloa mosebetsi ke Mookameli ho lateloa molao oa karolo ee.

(2) Moahloli o Moholo, Motlatsi oa Moahloli o Moholo kapa Mookameli oa Baahluli ba Khotla ea Holimo ba tla khetheloa mosebetsi ke Mookameli ho lateloa puisano le tumeleloano le Mokomeshene oa Tsamaiso ea Khotla ea Holimo.

(3) Ha e ba ho khethoa ha Moahloli e Moholo, Mothusi oa Moahloli o Moholo kapa Mookameli oa Baahloli ba Khotla ea Holimo a lumellane le khuthatso e tsoang ho mo Komeshene oa Tsamaiso ea Baahloli ba Khotla ea Holimo ho lateloa tse neiloeng ho karolo (2), Mookameli o tla etsa hore Senate e tsebisoe ka nako e lumelang: Ha e ba, ho thibela lipelaelo, ho boleloa hore tse tlamiloeng ke Mookameli ho khethoa ha baahloli mosebetsi ho tla ba ha ba bofelo.

(4) Nako le nako ha ho hlokahala ho khetha Moahloli ntle ha Moahloli e Moholo, Motlatsi oa Moahloli oa Moholo kappa Mookameli oa Baahloli ba Khotla e Kholo, Tsamaiso ea mo Khomeshene o tsoanetsi ho—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

phatlalatsa boemo; le ho mema Mookameli le sechaba ho khetha mabitso; le ho ba le merero le sechaba tsa batho ba ka tsoanelang mosebetsi oa teng; le ho hlopha mabitso a batho ba bararo ba khethiloeng joale ka batho ba tsoanetseng mosebetsi, le ho nea Mookameli mabitso; moo ho tla reng, ho lateloa karoloana (5), Mookameli o tsoanetsi ho khetha e mong oa ba khethiloeng hore ba sebetse mosebetsi oa teng.

(5) Ha Mookameli a bona hore a hona ho mabitso a neiloeng a tsoanetseng mosebetsi oa teng, ho lateloa karoloana (4)(e) o tla tsoanela ho re a laole hore Mokomeshene oa Tsamaiso ea Boahloli hore ba ngole mabitso a batho ba bararo ba tsoanetseng mosebetsi, moo Mookameli a tla khethang e mong oa ba bararo ba khethiloeng hore a sebetse mosebetse oa teng.

(6) Mookameli o tsoanetse ho tsebisa ho ngoe le ho ngoe ho khetha a ho etsang a latela molao oa karolo ee tse tla phatlalatsoang ho Gazette.

(7) Mesebetsi ea Moahloli e Moholo oa Khotla ea Tsamaiso ea Basebetsi le baahloli ba baholo ba Tsamaiso ea Khotla ea tsa Basebetsi li tsoanetse ho tsoaroa ke e mongo a baahloli kapa Moahloli e mong kapa E e mong a tla oketsang palo kapa a sebetsa a emetsi moahloli, ho ea ka hore ho e me joang, le hore ba khethoa ke Moahloli, o Moholo kamoraho ha puisano le tumelloano le Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea tsa Basebetsi. [Efetotsoe ka karoloana 6 ea Molao 10 oa 2017]

181 Ho hiroa ha Batlatsi ba baahloli

(1) Ha e ba ofisi ea Moahloli o Moholo e se na motho kapa mong oa ofisi a paleloa ke mosebetsi oa ofisi, Motlatsi oa Moahloli o tsoanetsi ho sebetsa sepakeng sa hae, empa ha e ba liofisi ka bobeli li sena motho kapa beng ba tsona ba paleloa ke mosebetsi, Moahloli o moholo oa Lekhotla la Molaotheo a nang le bolulo bo boholo bo latelang o tsoanetsi ho sebetsa joale ka Moahloli o Moholo.

(2) Ha e ba ofisi ea—

(a)

Mookameli oa Baahloli ba Lekhotla la Holimo;

(b) moahloli o moholo oa Khotla ea Basebeletsi; kapa

(c)

moahloli o moholo oa Khotla ea Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsi;

ha seo motho a sebetsang ho eona kapa mong oa ofisi a paleloa ke mosebetsi oa ofisi eong, moahloli o moholo a nang le bolulo bo latelang o tsoanetsi ho sebetsa joale ka Mookameli oa Baahloli; [Efetotsoe ke karoloana 7 ea Molao 10 oa 2017]

(3) Ha e ba mesebetsi ea e mong moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo, Lekhotla la Basebeletsi, le Lekhotla la Tsamaiso ea Mosebetsi ba ea e hlokeha ha sebaka se se khutsoane Mookameli, a sebetsa ka taelo ea Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Lekhotla la Molao, a ka khetha moahloli a kileng a sebetsa ho seo moahloli hore a sebetse ha likhoeli tse li safeteng tse leshome le metso e emebeli, nako e ka oketsoang ha mengoaha e leshome le metso e emebeli.

(4) Batho ba khethiloeng ho sebetsa ka tlase ha serapa sa (3) ba ka tsoelapele ho lula e le baahloli kamoraho ha hore ba khethoe, ka morero oa ho sebetsana le linyeoe tse qaletsoeng pele ho tsona ha li ntse li sebetsa joalo. 182 Ho khetho ea ha bo-mastrata le mangoe maloko a lisebeletsi tsa molao Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetsi ho lokisetsa ho khetho ea ha bo-mastrata le a mang maloko a balaoli ba molao ba se leng baahloli, empa—

(a)

bo-mastrata ba tsoanetsi ho khethoa ke Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Molao;

(b) basebeletsi ba ofisi tsa boahloli ntle le bo-mastrata kapa baahloli ba tsoanetsi ho khethoa kamoraho ha tumelloano ea Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Molao;

(c)

tsamaiso ea mesebetsi ee e tsoanetsi ho etsoa pepeneneng ho sena tsabo ho ema le lehlakore le e tseng kapa khethullo.

183 Ba sebetsang ho tsamaiso ea Molao a ba tsoanele ho khetheloa ho sebetsa ho khotla ea Tse etsang tsa Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsi Ntle a e ba ho neiloe ho Molaotheo oo, motho ha a tsoanele ho khetheloa ho sebetsa ho makhotla a fetang e ngoe. 184 Ho hiroa ha basebeletsi ba Molao ho tsoanetsi ho bontsa boemo ba sechaba Ho khetho ea ha basebeletsi ba lekhotla la toko ho tsoanetsi ho supa ho fapana ha batho le siemo sa tekano ho bong Zimbabwe. 185 Boikano

(1) Pele Moahloli o Moholo kapa Motlatsi oa Moahloli o Moholo a qala mosebetsi, o tsoanetsi hore, pele ha Mookameli kapa motho a neiloeng matla ke Mookameli, ho bolela boikano ba hae ka tsela ho neiloeng ka eona ho Lenaneo la Boraro.

(2) Pele moahloli, e seng Moahloli o Moholo kapa Motlatsi oa Moahloli o Moholo, a qala ho sebetsa, o tsoanetsi hore, pele ha Moahloli o Moholo kapa moahloli a latelang ka boholo mosebetsing a le teng, a supe boikano mosebetsing oa boahloli ka kano ea ho nkoa ke maloka a lekhotla.

(3) Melao ea Palamente mo ma mastrata a mang le mangoe maloko a lekhotla la molao, e seng baahloli, ba khetho eang ba tsoanetsi ho nea tsela ea ho e kana e tsoanetseng ho e tsoa ke maloko a molao. 186 Nako e neiloeng ea ho sebetsa ha baahloli

(1) Baahloli ba Lekhotla la Molaotheo ba khetheloa ho sebetsa ha mengoaha e leshome le metso e mehlano e sa oketsoeng, empa—

(a)

2013

ba tsoanetsi ho ema mosebetsi pele ba eso ho fihla mengoaha e mashome a supileng; le

(b) kamoraho ha ho phetho ea ha nako ea bona ea ho sebetsa, ba ka khethoa ho ba baahloli ba Lekhotla la Holimo-limo kapa Lekhotla la Holimo, ka thato ea bona, ha eba ba santse ba tsoanetsi ho khethoa joalo.

(2) Baahloli ba Lekhotla le ka Holimo-limo, Lekhotla la holimo le Baahloli ba bang ba na le boikarabelo ho tloha letsatsing la ho ba le boikarabelo ho fihlela ba fihla mengoaha e mashome a supileng, ha ba bile ba tsoanetsi ho ema mosebetsi.

(3) Motho a ka khethoa hore e be Moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo-limo, Lekhotla la Holimo le tse lingoe likhotla ha nako e etseng, empa ha e ba motho a khethiloe,leha nako ea hae ea ho sebetsa e e so e fihli.

(4) Leha moahloli a seile ho mosebetsi kapa a fihlile mengoaha e mashome a supileng kapa, ha e ba ke Moahloli oa Khotla ea Molaotheo kapa moahloli ea boleloang ho karolo (3), o fihlile qetellong ea nako ea hae ea ho sebetsa, kapa a ka etelapele a lula setulong sa ho ba moahloli ka morero hore a phethe mesebetsi e ne e qaliloe pele a eso a sebetseha joale ka moahloli.

(5) Moahloli a ka e tokolla mosebetsing oa hae ka nako leha e le efe ka lengolo le ngoletsoeng Mookameli le ea ho eena ka Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Molao.

(6) Ofisi ea moahloli ha e tsoanele ho felisoa ka nako a sa le setulong. 187 Ho tlosoa ha baahloli setulong

(1) Moahloli a ka tlosoa mosebetsing feela—

(a)

ha a paleloa mesebetsi ea ofisi ea hae, ho bakeloa ke ho fokola meleng kapa khopolong;

(b) ho paleloa ho sebetsa haholo ka tsoanelo; kapa

(c)

boitsoaro bo fotsahetseng bo bongata; kapa

le hore moahloli a ke ke a tlosoa mosebetsing ntleha ho latela karolo ena.

(2) Ha e ba Mookameli a nahana hore potso ea ho tlosa Moahloli o Moholo mosebetsing ho tsoanetsi ho batlisisoa, Mookameli o tsoanetsi ho hira lekhotla la baahluli ho hlolisisa taba.

(3) Ha e ba Komeshene ea Mosebeletsi oa Boahloli e lemosa Mookameli hore potso ka ho tlosa moahloli leha e le ofe, ho akaretsa Moahloli e Moholo, e tsoanetsi ho batlisisoa, Mookameli o tsoanetsi ho hira lekhotla la baahluli ho hlolisisa taba ena.

(4) Lekhotla le khethiloeng tlase ha karolo ena le tsoanetsi ho ba le maloko a seong tlase ha mararo a hiriloeng ke Mookameli, eo—

(a)

a ka bang motho ea—

(i) a ile a sebetsa a le moahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo-limo, kapa Lekhotla la HolimoZimbabwe; kapa

(ii) ke moahloli kapa o kile a ba moahloli oa lekhotla le senang mokhano oa ho sebetsa litaba tsa sechaba kapa tsa tlolo ea molao naheng eo molao oa eona e leng oa Roman-Dutch kapa Senyesemane, le Senyesemane e le puo e amohelehang molaong;

(b) a ka ba motho a khethoang, sehlopha sa batho ba bararo kapa ba fetang ba mengoaha supileng kapa e fetang ba khethiloeng ke mokhatlo, ba

leng tlase ha Molao oa Palamente, o emelang basebeletsi ba molao oa Zimbabwe.

(5) Mokhatlo ho buioang ka eona ho karolo (4)(b) o tsoanetsi ho hlopha mananeo a builoeng ka oona ho karoloana eong ha Mookameli a bile a e batla.

(6) Mookameli o tsoanetsi ho khetha e mong oa likala tsa lekhotla le khethiloeng tlase ha karolo ena ho ba molula-setulo oa lekhotla lena.

(7) Lekhotla le khethiloeng tlase ha karoloana (2) kapa (3) le tsoanetsi ho hlolisisa ka moahloli a tsoanetsing ho tlosoa mosebetsing, ebile ha a entse joalo, o tla rula litaba tsa teng ho Mookameli a bolele hore moahloli o tsoanetse ho tlosoa ofising kapa che.

(8) Mookameli o tsoanetse ho sebetsa ho lateloa tumelloano ea sehlopha sa lekhotla tlase ha karoloana (7).

(9) Lekhotla le khethiloeng tlase ha karolo ena le na le litokelo le matla a tsoanang joale ka Komeshene ea Molao oa Komeshene ea Patlisiso tlase ha Molao oa LiKomeshene tsa Lipatlisiso [Khaolo 10:07], kapa molao ofe kapa ofe o nkeloang sebaka ho Molao oo.

(10) Ha e ba potso ea ho tlosa moahloli e le moemeli e fetiselitsoe lekhotleng tlase ha karolo ena, moahloli o emisoa ho fihlela Mookameli, ka tumello ea sehlopha sa lekhotla, a tsosolosa ho emisoa kapa ho tlosa moahloli.

(11) Molao oa Palamente o ka matlafatsa Komeshene ea Baahloli kapa lekhotla le khethiloeng tlase ha karolo ena hore leha e le ofe Moahloli a tlise lengolo la hore moahloli a hlahlobioe ke lingaka tse likhethetsoeng mosebetsi oo, hore ho be le nnete ka bophelo ba hae ebile le khopolong. 188 Likemiso tsa mosebetsi le boemo ba mosebetsi oa maloko a baahloli

(1) Baahloli ba na le tokelo ea ho fumana meputso. Meputso le melemo e meng e behiloeng nako le nako ke Komeshene ea Tsebetso ea Likhotla ka tumello ea Mookameli a neiloeng kamoraho ha puisano le Minista le ikarabelang ka toka le ka khothatso ea Minista le ikarabelang litabeng tsa lichelete.

(2) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetsi ho nea ka maemo a ho sebetsa ha maloko a boahloli ho eena ntleha baahloli ebile o tsoanetsi ho ba le nnete hore ho phahamisoa ha bona mosebetsing, ho ea sebetsa ho honge le ho lelekoa, le e mingoe mekhato ea temoso, e etsahala—

(a)

ka tumelloano ea Komeshene ea Mosebetsi oa Boahloli; le

(b) ka tsela e sebetsang e sena lehlakore ebile e le pepeneneng ho sena poifo kapa ho khethulla.

(3) Meputso, le tse lingoe e mingoe Meputso ea maloko a Baahloli e tsoa ho Chelete efumanehang

(4) Meputso, le tse lingoe le melemo e meng ea maloka a likhotla ha li fokotsehe ha li ntse li tsoara kapa li sebetsa ofising e amehang.

KAROLO 3 Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea ba Molao 189 Kemiso le Pupo ea Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Balaoli ba Molao

(1) Ho na le Komeshene ea Tšebeletso ea Mesebetsi ea Baahloli le—

(a)

Moahloli o Moholo;

(b) Motlatsi oa Mookameli oa Moahloli;

(c)

Mookameli oa Maahloli oa Lekhotla la Holimo;

(d) moahloli a le mong ea khethiloeng ke baahloli ba Lekhotla la Molaotheo, Lekhotla la Holimo-limo, Lekhotla la Holimo, Lekhotla la Basebeletsi le Lekhotla la Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsi;

(e)

Moahloli Akakaretsang;

(f)

Molaoli o Moholo;

(g) Molula-Setulo oa Komiti ea Mesebetsi ea Hurumente;

(h) ba e meli ba molao ba bararo ba sebetsang ba mengoaa e qalang ho e supileng ba le mosebetsing ba khethiloeng ke mokhatlo, e leng tlase ha Molao oa Palamente, o emelang basebeletsi ba Zimbabwe;

(i)

moprofesa a le mong kapa lekichara a phahameng ea khethiloeng ke mokhatlo o emeloang ke matichere a mangata likolong tsa Zimbabwe kapa, ha ho se na mokhatlo oo, a khethiloeng ke Mookameli;

(j)

motho a le mong eo ka mengoaha e supileng a e leng a sebetsa Zimbabwe e le moemeli oa libuka kapa moqolotsi oa libuka, ebile o khethiloe ke mokhatlo, o etsoa tlase ha Molao oa Palamente, o emelang batho ba joalo; le

(k) motho a le mong bonyane phihlelo ea lilemo tse supileng tsamaisong ea mehloli ea batho, e behiloeng ke Mookameli.

(2) Moahloli o Moholo kapa, ha a le seo, Motlatsi oa Moahloli o Moholo o okamela mekopano ea Tsamaiso ea Komeshene ea Baahloli, ebileha ba e seo ha ba le mokopanong leha e le sefe maloka a bona li khethile e mong oa palo ea bona ho ba mokopanong.

(3) Maloko a Tsamaiso ea Komeshene ea Baahloli ea Likhotla tse boletsoeng likarolo (d), (h), (i), (j) le (k) ea karoloana (1) li khethiloe ka nako e le engoe e sa tsosolosoang mengoaha e tsilela. 190 Mesebetsi ea Komeshene ea Balaoli

(1) Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Boahloli e ka eletsa Hurumente leha ka taba efe e tsamaelanang le molao kappa bolaoli ba molao, ebile le Hurumente e tsonetsi ho amohela keletso eong.

(2) Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Boahloli e tsoanetse ho phahamisa le ho khothatsa boikemelo le boikarabelo ba boahloli le Batlatsi, ho sebetsa le tsamaiso ea mosebetsi oa tsa molao o leng pepeneneng lefatseng la Zimbabwe, ebile e nang le matla ohle a hlokahalang pakeng sa morero oo.

(3) Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Boahloli, ka tumelloano ea Minista le ikarabelang ka toka, e ka etsa melao ea tsamaiso pakengsa morero ofe kapa ofe o boletsoeng karolong ena.

(4) Molao oa Palamente o nea Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Boahloli tsamaiso e tsamaelanang le mosebetsi, taeo le tsela ee batho ba sebetsang ho Lekhotla la Molaotheo ee ba tsoanetseng ho sebetsa ka eona, Lekhotla la Holimo-limo, Lekhotla la Holimo, Lekhotla la Basebeletsi, Lekhotla la Tsamaiso ea Mesebetsle le tse lingoe likhotla. 191 Ho tsoanela le hoba pepeneneng ha tsamaiso ea Khomeshene ea Tsamaisoea Baahloli Khomeshene ea Tsamaiso Baahlolie tsoanetse ho sebetsa ka tsela e lokileng, e le pepeneneng. KAROLO 4 Tse Lingoe 192 Molao o tla laoloang Molao o tla laoloang ke likhotla tsa Zimbabwe ke molao o ne o sebetsa ho tsoa ka letsatsi o qalileng ho sebetsa ka lona, ka ho fetoloa ka tsela ea teng ka ho tsamaea ha nako. 193 Mekhano oa Matla a likhotla Fela likhotla tse latelang ke tsona tse ka sebelisang kapa tsa neoa matla a ho laola litaba tsa tlolo ea molao—

(a)

Lekhotla la Molaotheo, Lekhotla la Holimo-limo, Lekhotla le Holimo le likhotla tsa bo mastrata;

(b) lekhotla kapa lekhotla le sebetsang litaba tsa tlaseha taolo ea taeo, ho be ho fihle mo ho bonahalang hore mokhano oa taolo o tsoanetse hore matla a taeo a sebelisoe ho ba e tsoanetseng. KHAOLO 9 Likemiso molaotheo oa Bophelo le Bophelo ba Mekhoa 194 Molaotheo ea e laolang tsamaiso ea sechaba

(1) Puso ea sechaba mahlakoreng ohle a hurumente, ho akaretsa mekhatlo ea Hurumente, le tse lingoe likala tse li leng tlase ha Hurumente ka mong le tse emelang sechaba, e tsaoanetsi ho laoloa ke likemiso letlotlo ea puso ea nnete joale ka ha ho neiloe ho Molaotheo. Mo ho lateloa likemiso tse latelang—

(a)

Batlatsi bo botiileng bo tsoanetseng ho khethoa leho bolokoa;

(b) ho sebetsa thata leha sebelisa hantle tsohle lisebelisoa;

(c)

boeteleli ba sechaba bo tsoanetsei ho tsoelapele;

(d) mosebetse o tsoanetse ho etsoa hosena lenyatso, kaholekana hosena ho emela le hlakore;

(e)

batho batsoanetse ho feoa tse ba li hlokang ka nako etsoanetseng, sechaba le sona setsoanetse ho khothatsoa hore ke tsoanelo hore sekene le pupong ea molao;

(f)

boeteleli ba sechaba bo tsoanetse ho araba lipotso tse tsoang Palamenteng le ho sechaba;

(g) meaho ea sechaba le tsohle tse thusang mapakeng ohle litsoanetse ho sebetsa ka kutloisisano;

(h) bonnete bo tsoanetsei ho tsoeletsoa ka ho nea sechaba litaba tse totileng hape tsa nnete kanako etsoanetseng;

(i)

tsamaiso entle ea tse li fumanehangletsa tsebetso ka ho fumaneha ha mesebetse hore li tsoeletsoe leho khothatsa batho;

(j)

boeteleli ba sechaba bo tsoanetsei ho akaretsa leho emela mefuta eohle ea batho ba Zimbabwe;

(k) ho hiroa, ho rutela mesebetse le ho tsoeletsa mosebetse ho tsoanetsei ho latela thuto ea motho, Batlatsi bahahe, ka holekaneng ha banna le basali ho kopantsa batho bana le bohole bo e tseng: le lefatse ho tsoanetse ho hlokomela hore hoka etsoa joang ho kopantsa letsa molao hore ho tsoeletsoe litakatso tse bohlokoa le metheo.

(2) Ho hiroa mahlakoreng ohle a laoloang ke Hurumente, ho akaretsale mekhatlo e laoloang ke hurumente le tse lingoe likala. 195 Mekhatlo ea khoebo e laoloang ke Mmuso

(1) Likampani le lingoe tsa ho sebelisana ka tsa bhisimisi li laoloang le tsa lefatse/hurumente litsoanetse, holimo ha ho latela melao letotiso e leng ho karolo 194(1), hore li etse mesebetse ea tsona hore li boloke lehlakore la tsa li bhisimusi leho ikokobetsa melaong e latelang tsela tsa Mmuso

(2) Likampani le tse lingoe tsa ho sebelisana li neiloeng ho karoloana (1) li tsoanetse ho qala ho sebetsa hantle ka tsela eleng pepeneneng le tsela e tiileng. 196 Boikarabelo ba basebeletsi ba sechaba le melao-motheo ea boetapele

(1) Matla a neoang motho eo a sebetsang sechaba ke motho a tsepiloeng, e bile o tsoanetse ho sebetsa ka tsela—

(a)

e tsoanetseng le takatso le litetello tsa Molaotheo ona;

(b) e tsoletsang hlompho bathong le boikemelo ba ho thusa batho eseng hoba busa le;

(c)

ea ho holisa tsepo ofising e neiloeng motho a sebetsang ka nnete.

(2) Basebeletsi ba tsa sechaba lileng litulong tse li kholo litsoanetse ho latela le ho hlompha litaelo tsa boeteleli tse latelang—

(a)

ho ba le leihlo la bohlale la ho se tsoare lehlakore likhopolong;

(b) ho tsepeha ho etseng mesebetseng ea sechaba;

(c)

hoba le maikarabelo kamenahano leka liketso; le

(d) ho hlompha leho enela mosebetseng oa batho. 197 Boemo ba lihlooho tsa lihlopha tse laoloang ke Mmuso Molao oa Palamente o ka fokotsa nako ea boeteleli kapa ho lula setulong ha bohle ba baholo ba le tlase ha hurumente le tse lingoe tse thusang lefatseng. 198 Taelo ea ho tiisa khaolo 9 Molao oa Palamente o tlameha ho nea ka litlhoko tsa ho li tiisa tseneiloeng ho Karolo ena, ho akaretsa—

(a)

tse hlokang hore basebeletsi ba sechaba ba tsebisoe ka mehla ka thepa ea bona;

(b) ho thekha melao ea boitsoaro e lokelang ho hlokomeloa ke balaoli ba sechaba;

(c)

ho nea tsamaiso e ntle ea mekhatlo e lokelang ho hlokomeloa ke mebuso e laoloang ke Mmuso oa hurumente le mekhatlo e meng ea khoebo e nang le eona kapa e laoloang ke Mmuso ka ho feletseng;

(d) ho nea taelo pakeng sa khalemelo ea batho ba lateleng le litlhoko tsa Khaolo ena kapa melao leha e le efe ea boitsoaro kapa tsela e lumellanoeng e boletsoeng ho temana (b). KHAOLO 10 TseBeLeTso ea sechaBa 199 Tsebeletso ea Hurumente

(1) Hona le Lekhotla la Basebeletsi le lengoe fela le bonang ka tsamaiso ea hurumente ea Zimbabwe.

(2) Lekhotla la Tsireletso le akaretsa batho ba hiriloeng ke Hurumente ntle ha—

(a)

maloko a tsireletso ea sechaba le e mingoe e ka bopioang;

(b) baahloli, bo mastrata le batho bohle ba okamelang likhotla tsa holimo amaholo tse beiloeng ke Molao oa Palamente;

(c)

maloko ea Komeshene e khethiloeng e emisitsoeng ho lateloa kemiso ea Molaotheo;

(d) basebeletsi ba Palamente; le

(e)

ba bang ba ngoliloeng, ke Molaotheo kapa Molao oa Palamente, ho sa bopi karolo ea Tsamaiso ea Basebeletsi.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho semolla ka ho nea kemiso ea pupo, tsamaiso, boitsoaro le molao oa karolo 203, tsela ea ho sebetsa ha maloko a Basebeletsi ba Hurumente. 200 Boitsoaro ba maloka a Basebeletsi ba Sechaba

(1) Basebeletsi ba Hurumente ba tsoanetse ho etsoara ka tsela e rutsoeng ke Molaotheo le se le molao.

(2) Basebeletsi ba Hurumente ha ba lumeleloi hore ba etse mekhoa e sa tsamaelaneng le molao.

(3) Basebeletsi ba Hurumente a ba tsoanele ho—

(a)

emela lehlakure ho tsa politiki;

(b) tsoeletsa thato ea lihlopa tsa lipolitiki;

(c)

bontsa hore baemetsi sehlopha se fe sa politiki;

(d) thibela maikutlo a motho mang kapa mang.

(4) Basebeletsi ba Sechaba a ba tsoanele ho ba le malulo ho mekhatlo ea tsa politiki.

(5) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho emisa tsela e bona hore basebeletsi ba hurumente ha ba emi le le hlakore ho tsa Mmuso oa lefatse.

201 Likala li ikarabelang pakeng sa Tsebeletso ea Sechaba. Mookameli oa lefatse o tsoanetsi ho khetha Minista ea tla laolang Basebeletsi. 202 Ho bopeoa ha Komeshene ea Basebeletsi

(1) Ho na le Khomeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Basebeletsi e akaretsang—

(a)

Molula setulo le Motlatsi oa hae; le

(b) batho ba babeli ho ea ho ba bahlano ba ba khethiloeng ke Mookameli.

(2) Ba bopang Khomeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Hurumente ba tsoanetse ho khetho ea ka tsebo le ka nako e basebeletsi ba sebelitseng ka eona. 203 Mesebetsi ea Lekhotla la Basebeletsi ba Hurumente

(1) Khomeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Hurumente e na le mesebetsi e latelang—

(a) ho khetha batho ba na le tsebo e tsoanetseng mosebetsi;

(b) ho latela tse neoang ke karolo 65(5) ho tiisa molao o tsamaelanang le ba bereki ba hurumente, ho balisa meputso le tse lingoe tse thusang basebeletsi ba hurumente;

(c) ho busa leho a hlola ba sebeletsi ba hurumente;

(d) ho hlolisisa lingongoreho tsa basebeletsi le ho batla tsela ea ho oa ba thusa;

(e) hore ba be le nnete hore basebeletsi bohle basebetsa mosebetsi oa bona ka tsela e tsoanetseng;

(f) ho netefatsa hore basebeletsi ba Hurumente basebetsa ka tsoanelo;

(g) ho lemosa Mookameli le Minista ka litaba tse tsamaelanang le Bosebetsi ba Hurumente;

(h) ho khuthatsa maemo le litsamaiso mabapi le ea molao oa lefatse; le

(i) ho tsamaea tse ba li neiloeng ho tsoa ho molaotheo oa sechaba kapa Palamente.

(2) Khomeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Hurumente, ho lumelloane le Minista oa Basebeletsi ho phetha melao ea karoloana (1).

(3) Khomeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Hurumente, e tsoanetse ho laola ka tsela ea tsoanetse ho sebetsa e latela molao o ngoliloeng o ka neoang ke Minista.

(4) Ho lokisa ka meputso le tse lingoe tse thusang Basebeletsi ba Hurumente Komeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Hurumente, ba tsoanetsi ho sebetsa ba latela taolo ea Mookameli le Minista a lebaneng le mosebetsi oona le moraho ha puisano le Minista oa Basebeletsi ba Hurumente. Lekhotla lena le tsoanetse ho fumana tokolo ho tsoa ho Mookameli oa naha e tla laolang Minista oa tsa lichelete. 204 Liemeli le ba Batlatsi ba ka sehloong ba Zimbabwe Batlatsi ho mangoe mafatse ba khetho ea ke Mookameli e bile ba laoloa ke ena. 205 Bangoli ba baholo

(1) Bangoli ba khetho ea Mookameli moraho ha li puisano Mookameli oa a lumelelana le Komeshene e Basebeletsi ba Hurumente.

(2) Sebaka sa ho sebetsa ha Mongoli o Moholo ke mengoaha e mehlano, sebaka se se oketsoa fela ka bokhone ba Mookameli.

KHAOLO 11 Tsa Tsireletso KAROLO 1 Tsa Tsireletso Tse Tloaelehileng 206 Polokeho ea Sechaba

(1) Lekhotla lena le tsoanetse ho bona hore sechaba sa Zimbabwe se lula ka khotso, kutloano hape sene malokelo a lekanang kapa a sa arohanyeng batho.

(2) Lekhotla le letsoanetse ho tsireletsoa ho lateloa pupo ea molao oa lefatse;

(3) Lekhotla le letsoanetse ho hlokomeloa ka hlompo e kholo mabapi le a—

(a)

malokelo le tokoloho ea ho se hlalohanye joale ka ha ebeiloe popong ea Mmuso le molao oa lefatse; le

(b) puso ea molao. 207 Mesebetsi ea ba tsireletso ea lefatse

(1) Mesebetsi ea ba tsireletsang lefatse la Zimbabwe e akaretsa—

(a)

masole a ho tsireletsa lefatse;

(b) mapolesa;

(c)

bahloli;

(d) Litironko le ba Tsamaiso eaTotiso;

(e)

le tse lingoe tsa tsireletso tse libopiloeng ke molao oa Palamente.

(2) Ho tsireletsoa ha lefatse ho tsoanetsi ho laoloa ke Molaotheo, Mookameli, le Kabinete ho lateloa tse e bile bahloloa ke Palamente.

(3) Ba tsireletso ea lefatse ho hlokomela moseano oa batho bohle ba Zimbabwe. 208 Boitsoaro ba maloka a tsa litsebeletso tsa tsireletso

(1) Maloko a tsoanetse ho latela tsa Molaotheo oa lefatse;

(2) A se tsoanelo hore masole kapa e mongoe oa maloko ba re basebetsa hoMasole aba tsoanela ho—

(a) ba be le lehlakore ba le e melang;

(b) ba fumanehe ba tsoeletsa litakatso tsa le hlakore le leng ho tsa politiki;

(c) ba thibele litakatso tsa lehlakore le leng le bong molaong ho tsa politiki; le hore

(d) ba loantse litokelo le tokoloho tsa mang kapa mang.

(3) Maloko a tsireletso ha tsoanela ho berekela kapa ho tsoara litulo ho tsa politiki.

(4) Maloko a sebetsang ho tsireletso ea lefatse ha tsoanele ho sebetsa ho hurumente ea sechaba ntleha hon ale tseleng tsa potlaho. 209 Lekhotla la Tsireletso ea Sechaba Honale kansili ea tsireletso ea lefatse e bopuoang ke Mookameli ena a le moluli setulo le balateli ba Mookameli le minista, lebabang ba tsireletso ea lefatse ba khethiloeng kemolao oa Palamente.

(1) Mesebetse ea Khansili ea Tsireletso ea Lefatse ke—

(a)

ho tsoeletsa molao oa tsireletso ea Zimbabwe;

(b) ho tsebisa le ho lemosa Mookameli ka litaba tse li tsamaelana le tsireletso ea tsa lefatse; le

(c)

ho phetha tse lingoe likaba li beiloe ke molao oa Palamente.

(2) Baeteleli ba tsireletso ea lefatse ba tsoanetse ho tsebisa ba Khansili ea Tsireletso ea Lefatse ka tse li etsahalang lefatseng mabapi le tsireletso ea lefatse ka mokho oa khansili ba be le litlaleho ka boemo ba tsireletso ea Zimbabwe . 210 Mekhoa e ikemetseng ea litletlebo Ho tsoanetse hoba le Molao oa Palamente o tiileng hape o lokolohileng oa ho amohela lingongoreho le ho batlisisa ka tsona tse tsoang sechabeng ka boitsoaro ba maloko a tsireletso ea lefatse le litsela tsa ho lilokisa ntle ha phapano kapa ka kutloano. KAROLO 2 Masole a Tsireletso 211 Masole a Tsireletso

(1) Masole a Tsireletso ea lefatse la Zimbabwe a akaretsa masole, masole a moeng le e mingoe mesebetsi e ka emisoang tlase ha Molao oa Palamente;

(2) Masole a Tsireletso ke ona fela masole a leng molaong Zimbabwe;

(3) Masole a Tsireletso ea Zimbabwe ba tsoanetse ho hlompha litokelo le litokoloho tsa batho bohle ba sena lehlakore ba le e metsing, ba supe boitsoaro ba lefatse, ba rate lefatse la bona, ba be le botsoiriri le boikokobetso hare ha sechaba joale ka ha ho neiloe ke Molaotheo.

(4) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho lula a bolokehile le masole a ntoa a na le boitsoaro bo bontle.

(5) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho nea pupo, kemiso, tsamaiso, melao, boitsoaro le ho holisa kapa ho tlosa setulong ha baetapele le ba bang ho ea ka Molao o tsoang ho karolo 218 mabapi le tsamaiso ea tsireletso. 212 Mosebetse oa masole Mosebetsi oa masole ke oa ho tsireletsa sechaba ne lefatse la Zimbabwe, batho ba eona, tsireletso ea sechaba le hlompho ea sona, le ho phahamisa Molaotheo. 213 Tsela ea ho sebetsa ha masole

(1) Ho lateloa Molaotheo, Mookameli ke ena fela,joale ka Molaoli o Moholo oa Tsamaiso ea Masole, a na le matla aho—

(a)

laola mo ho tsoanetseng ho sebetsa masole; le

(b) ho khetha aka romelang masole.

(2) Ka taolo ea Mookameli, Masole a Tsireletso a ka romeloa ho sebetsa Zimbabwe—

(a)

hore a tsireletse lefatse;

(b) ho tsehetsa Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa ha hona le meferefere; kapa

(c)

ho tsehetsa Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa, sechaba ka nako hona le likotsi kapa tsenyahalo.

(3) Ka taolo ea Mookameli, Masole a Tsireletso a ka rumeloa ho e sebetsa ntle ha Zimbabwe—

(a)

ho boloka khotso ho lateloa taolo ea United Nations Organisation kapa likhotla tsa mafatse ohle mo Zimbabwe e leng leloko;

(b) ho tsireletsa likhaolo tsa mangoe mafatse;

(c)

ho tlatsa tumellaono ea lefatse lohle ka bophara; kapa

(d) ho tsireletsa litakatso tsa Zimbabwe.

(4) Ha e ba hona le palo ea tse peli ho tsa tharo (2/3) ea maloko a Palamente ho Kopano ea Seneta le Ntlu ea Boemeli, Palamente e sa lumeleng hore Masole a Tsireletso a sebetsang ntleha lefatse la Zimbabwe a tle lefatseng la bona.

(5) Ha Palamente e lumetsi hore Masole a tsireletso a le ntle ha lefatse a tle lefatseng la bona, Mookameli o tsoanetse ho etsa tse tsoanetseng, ho bona hore Masole a tsireletso a tlisitsoe mahae a bona, e bile u e tsa joalo hore ho tsireletsoe sechaba sa Zimbabwe. 214 Boikarabelo ba lipolotiki pakeng sa ho romeloa ha masole Ha Masole a Tsireletso a romeloa—

(a)

Zimbabwe ho thusa hore ho be le khotso; kapa

(b) Ntle ha Zimbabwe. Mookameli o tsoanetse ho tsebisa Palamente, kapela le ka botlalo, ka mabaka athumo ea bona—

(i) Mo ba rometsoeng teng Zimbabwe, poleke ee ba rometsoeng ho eona;

(ii) mo ba rometsoeng ntle ha lefatse, ho tsoanetse lefatse le ba romeloang ho lona ba tsebahale. 215 Minista a bonang ka Masole a Tsireletso Mookameli o tsoanetsi ho khetha o Moholo a tla bonang ka Masole a Tsireletso. 216 Taolo ea Masole a Tsireletso

(1) Molao oa Palamente o ka emisa hore—

(a)

Masole a Tsireletso, a tsoanetse ho ba tlase ha taolo ea molao oa Molaoli emong; kapa

(b) kala engoe lengoe ea Masole a Tsireletso, kapa tsepeli le hofita likopane, li tsoanetsi hoba tlase ha taolo ea Molaoli e mong fela.

(2) Molaoli o mong le e mong oa Masole a Tsireletso, le Molaoli e mong le e mong oa Kala ea Masole a Tsireletso, o khetho ea ke Mookameli moraho ha puisano le Minista a bonang ka tsa Masole a Tsireletso.

(3) Balaoli ba Masole a Tsireletso le ba Balaoli ba tsamaiso ea Masole a Tsireletso, le lingoe likala tsa tsireletso ba khethoa ho sebetsa ha mengoaha e mehlano fela, motho u lumeletsoe ho sebetsa ofiseng e le ngoe ha libaka tsepeli.

(4) Motho a ile a sebetsa joale ka Molaoli oa kala ea Masole a Tsireletso, a ka hiroa joale ka Molaoli oa Masole a Tsireletso, empa motho a kile a sebetsa joale ka Molaoli oa Masole a Tsireletso a ka se ke a khetheloa ho ba Molaoli oa tsamaiso ea Masole a Tsireletso kapa a ba molaoli oa le e le efe kala e sebetsang ka tsa tsireletso.

(5) Molaoli ohle oa Masole a Tsireletso, o tsoanetse ho sebetsa a latela molao o ngoliloeng ke Minista a lebaneng le Masole a Tsireletso a sebetsang a le tlase ha Mookameli. 217 Ho theho ea le ho theho ea ha Komeshene ea Tsebeletso ea Tsireletso

(1) Ho nale le Khomeshene ea Masole a Tsireletso e akaretsang motsoare oa setulo a tsoanetseng ho ba Molula setulo oa Khomeshene ea Basebeletsi ba Hurumente, ba ka bang babeli kapa tsilela ba khethoang ke Mookameli.

(2) Maloko a Tsamaiso ea Komeshene ea Masole a Tsireletso a tsoanetsi ho khethoa ka tsebo le Batlatsi, ho tsoaroang ha litaba tsa ntoa, kapa ho bonahala joale ka batho ba tsoanetsing ho hiroa, le hore—

(a)

karolo e ngoe ho tsepeli ea batho bao ba tsoanetse hoba batho ba sa kang ba bereka ba mosebetsi oa ho ba Masole a Tsireletso;

(b) O mongoe oa bona o tsoanetse ho ba motho a ele a ba le setulo se phahameng kaleng ea tsireletso ha sebaka sa mengoaha e mehlano. 218 Mosebetse ea Komeshene ea Tsebeletso ea Masole

(1) Komeshene ea Masole a Tsireletso e na le mesebetsi e latelang—

(a)

ho hira batho ba rutehileng hape ba na le Batlatsi ba tsoanelang ho sebetsa ho likala tsa Masole a Tsireletso;

(b) ho lokisa leho laola li tsela tsa ho sebetsa, ho akaretsa le meputso, le tse lingoe tse thusang ba basebeletsi ba tsireletso;

(c)

ho netefatsa maitsoaro le tsamaiso ea mosebetsi oa Masole a Tsireletso;

(d) ho ba le nnete hore maloko a Masole a Tsireletso a sebetsa ka ho latela karolo 208;

(e)

ho bona hore ho na le khotso le tsoarano hare ha Masole le sechaba;

(f)

ho lemosa Mookameli le Minista ka taba leha e le e tsamaelanang le Masole;

(g) ho sebetsa leha o le ofe mosebetsi e laotsoeng ho Komeshene, ke Molaotheo ona kapa Molao oa Palamente.

(2) Tsamaiso ea Komeshene ea Masole a Tsireletso, ho tsoa ho Minista oa Masole a Tsireletso, a ka a tsoanetse ho sebetsa ka tsela ea maliba ka mo le laetsoe ka hona ke Minista oa Masole, a ka laola leha ka se fe ka le baka la karoloana (1).

(3) Ho raroloa taba ea meputso le tse lingoe tse lithusang Masole a tsireletso, Tsebetso ea Komeshene ea tsireletso e tsoanetse ho phethahatsa ka taolo ea Mookameli ho tsoa ho maikarabelo a Minista a bonang ka tsa chelete hape ka taolo ea minista a bonang ka tsa masole.

KAROLO 3 Tsebeletso ea Mapolesa 219 Tsebeletso ea mapolesa le mesebetse ea bona

(1) Hona le kala ea Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa e sebetsang ho—

(a)

batlisisa, le ho thibela bo senye;

(b) boloka lefatse la Zimbabwe;

(c)

tsireletsa le ho boloka bophelo le leruo la batho;

(d) tiisa moloao le boiketlo;

(e)

boloka Molaotheo oo le ho tiisa molao ho sena poifo le ho khetha;

(2) Likala tsa maplesa e tsoanetse ho sebetsa ka bongoe—

(a)

le e ka ba efe kala ea bahloli e sebetsang mo ho le molaong;

(b) leha e ka ba mang a khethiloeng ho lateloa molao hobane a hlole,kappa a sireletse mefuta ea melato e seaneng; le hore

(c)

likala tsa hae le tsa mangoe mafatse tsa ho loantsa bosenye.

(3) Likala tsa Mapolesa li tsoanetse ho se be le lehlakore ho tsa politiki, li e tse tsa lefatse, litsepehe, lihlompe mosebetse oa eona le baholo ba sechaba mabapi joale ka ha ho neiloe ke Molaotheo ona.

(4) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho nea pupo, maemo a puso,tsamaiso, hlompo, le khuliso le ho ruloa maloko bolulo mosebetsing, ho lateloa tse neiloeng ho karolo 223, tsela ea ho sebetsa ha Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa. 220 Minista e lebetseng ka Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa Moo kameli oa lefatse o tsoanetse ho khetha Minista a lebeletseng Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa. 221 Komeshene ea Molao oa Mapolesa

(1) Likala tsa mapolesa li tlase ha taolo ea Moetapele oa Mapolesa a hiriloeng ke Mookameli moraho ha ho buisana le Minista a sebetsang tsa Mapolesa;

(2) Moetapele o khethoa neoa sebaka sa ho sebetsa se fihlang mengoaha e mehlano hape a ka neoa sebaka se le sengoe fela sa ho boela setulong sena.

(3) Motho a ileng a lula setulo sena a ka se ke a khethoa hore a etelele leha e kaba sefe sehlopha sa tsireletso.

(4) Moetapele-o Moholo o tsoanetse ho etsa mosebetse oa hae ho lateloa molao o ngoliloeng moraho ha tumelloano le Minista a lebeletseng Mapolesa ho lateloa taolo ea Mookameli. 222 Kemiso lepupo ea Komeshene ea Mapolesa

(1) Hona le Komeshene ea Maphnolisa e na le Molula setulo, a tsoanetseng ho ba molula setulo oa Komeshene ea Basebetse ba Hurumente ea Mmuso le babang bababeli ho ea ho ba supileng bakhethiloeng ke Mookameli.

(2) Maloko a Tsamiso ea Mapolesa a tsoanetse ho khetho ea ka tsebo ea molao le thutho ea bona,kapa ho tsoanela mosebetsi, le

(a)

2013

sekamo sa bona ke batho basa e tseng mosebetsi ebile a bako sebetsa joale ka maloko a Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa;

(b) o mongoe fela oa bona o tsoanetsei hoba motho a ile a ba smolula setulo se se holo ho Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa. 223 Mesebetse oa Komeshene ea Mapolesa

(1) Komesheneea Mapolesa e na le mesebetse elatelang—

(a)

ho hira batho ba e thutileng, ba na lebokhone ba ho ba ba lulibalitulo tsa holimo ho Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa;

(b) ho lokisa le ho tsamaisa ka molao siemo sa mosebetsi, ho akaretsang meputso/meputso le tselingoe tse thusang likala tsa mapolesa;

(c)

ho netefatsa hore maitsoaro, le tsoaro ea mapolesa e be enepileng;

(d) ho tiisa hore maloko a Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa a hlokomele tse neiloeng ho karolo 208;

(e)

ho tsoeletsa tsoarano le kutloano ha Mapolesa le sechaba;

(f)

ho lemosa Mookameli le Minista ka tsohle tse akaretsang Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa;

(g) ho etsa mosebetse leha o kaba ofe ba o roesoang Komeshene ea Mapolesa ke Molaotheo o na Molao oa Palamente.

(2) Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Mapolesa, ka tumelloano le Minista oa Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa, e ka bea Molao o hlalositsoeng ho karoloana (1).

(3) Ho lokiseng ka meputso/meputso, maloko a Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Mapolesa, Komeshene ea Komeshene ea Mesebetsi ea Mapolesa, e tsoanetse ho sebetsa e latela tumelloano le Minista oa tsa Chelete eneloeng ka tiisoeaa minista a lebetseng katsa chelate moraho ha tumelloano le Minista a lebeletseng ka tsa Mapolesa. KAROLO 4 Tsebeletso ea Bohlale/Hlaselo 224 Kemiso ea tsamaiso ea Bahloli

(1) Engoe le ngoe kala ea bahloli ba Mmuso, ntleha bohloli ba kala ea Masole a Tsireletso kapa kala ea Tsamaiso ea mapolesa, e tsoanetse ho bopioa ho lateloa molao oa Mookameli kapa taelo ea Kabinete,

(2) Likala tsa basebeletsi ba Bohloli leha ele efe a litsoanele ho khethulla, litsoanetse ho supa siemo sa naha, lisupe ho rata lefatse la bona, lisupe bohlale hape li be le boikokobetso joale ka kemiso ea Molaotheo. 225 Minista ea laoang tsebeletso ea bohlale le hlaselo Mookameli o tsoanetse ho khetha Minista a tla sebetsang leha ho le efe kala ea bohloli. 226 Taelo le taolo ea tsamaiso ea bohloli ba naha

(1) Kala ea tsamaiso ea bahloli e tsoanetse ho sebetsa tlase ha bolaoli bo boholo ba tsireletso ea Molaoli o Moholo oa Tsamaiso ea Bahloli ea tsoanetsing ho khethoa ke Mookameli ha sebaka se fihlang mengoaha e mihlano e ka bosetsoang ha ngoe.

(2) Molaoli o Moholo oa Tsamaiso ea Bahloli o tsoanetse ho etsa mosebetse oa hae alatela litaelo tsa Minista e a lebeletseng tsa mosebetse oa bohloli ba lefatse tlase ha taolo ea Mookameli.

(3) Motho a ileng a sebetsa ale Molaoli o Moholo oa Tsamaiso ea Bohloli a ka seke a khethoa hoba moetapele oa kala efe kapa efe ea mosebetsi oa tsireletso. KAROLO 5 Tsa Litoronko Le Tokiso 227 Tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokiso

(1) Hona le kala ea Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso e sebetsang ho—

(a)

tsireletsa sechaba ho basenye ka ho hlaela leho rutisa bontle ho ba senyang, bohle ba tsoanang le bona ba tsoanelang ho hlaeloa hape leho lokolloa ha bona leho kopantsoa ha bona le sechaba.

(b) ho tsamaisoa ha ho sebetsa ha litoronko le tsa khalemo.

(2) Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso a litsoanele hoba le lehlakore, hoba le sechaba sechabeng, ho rata naha, setsebe leho ekemela ho balaoli ba sechaba joalo ka ha ho neiloe ho Molaotheo.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o tsoanetse ho nea mokhatlo, kemiso, puso, molao, hlompho le ho phahamisa kapa ho folosa basebeletsi bo lulong, ho lateloa karolo 231, tsela ea ho sebetsa ha maloko a Tsamaiso ea Litoronko le Tokiso. 228 Minista a lebeletsing ka mosebetsi oa Litoronko le Tokiso Mookameli o tsoanetei ho khetha Minista a tla sebetsang ho Kala ea Tsamaiso ea Litoronko le Tokisiso. 229 MoKomeshene ea akaretsang oa tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokiso

(1) Kala ea Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso e tlase ha taolo ea Molaoli o Moholo oa Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso a khethoang ke Minista a lebeletsing ka Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso

(2) Moetapele o Moholo oa kala ea Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso o khethoa a neoa sebaka sa ho sebetsa ha mengoaha e mehlano e ka bosetsoang ha ngoe fela.

(3) Motho a ileng a ba Moetapele o Moholo oa Kala ea Litoronko le Tsamaiso ea Tokisiso o tsoanetsei ho e tsa mosebetse a laoloa ke taelo etsoa ho Minista ea lebeletseng ka tsa litoronko lekhalemelo a latela taelo ea Mookameli. 230 Ho hlomale ho bupioa ha Komeshene ea tebeletso ea li toronko le tokiso

(1) Honale Komeshene ea Kemiso le Pupo ea ea Komeshene ea Tsamaiso ea Litoronko le Tokisiso e bopeoang ke Molulaosetulo oa Komeshene ea Basebetsei ba Hurumente kapa Mmuso le ba babeli ka bonyenyane kapa botsilela kabongata bakhethiloeng ke Mookameli.

(2) Ba khethoang ba tsoanetse hoba le tsebo le bohlale, le tsebo ba ho boloka molao le boiketlo, ho etela kapa thuto ea bona mosebebetseng le tsoanelo ea hore ba khethoe le—

(a)

2013

ba banyenyane ba bona ba be batho ba se ea kang baba leloko la tsireletso ea litoronko le tokiso

(b) e mongoe fela oa bona o tsoanetse hore a be motho a e le a tsoara setulo se seholo tsebeletsong ea litoronko lekhalemong ha sebaka selekanang lilemo tse hlano. 231 Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea tsebeletso ea litoronko le tokiso

(1) Komeshene ea toronkong le khalemo ena le mesebetse elatelang—

(a)

ho sebelisa kapa ho hira batho bae thutileng bana le tsebo hore batsoare litulo tse phahameng mosebetseng oa tsa litoronko lekhalemo;

(b) ho nea leho emisa tsela tsa ho bereka tse akaretsang meputso kapa patella le tselingoe tse tsoanang letsa ho boka tsa mesebetsi ea litoronkong le tokiso;

(c)

ho hlokomela bophelo ba basebetse ba kala ea litoronko le tokiso, le tsamaiso ea Mmuso oa bona le poloko ea maemo a bona aho sebetsa;

(d) ho netefatsa hore bohle ba sebetsang tlase ha kala ea li toronko ba hlokomele tse li neiloeng ho karolo 208;

(e)

ho khuthatsa khotso le kutloano hare ha basebetse ba kala ea litoronko le sechaba;

(f)

ho tsebesa Mookameli le Minista ka tsohle tse etsahalang ho likala tsohle tsa litoronko Le tokiso;

(g) ho etsa ofe kapa ofe mosebetsi ba oneiloeng kapa oneiloeng Komeshene ea ba litoronkong ke Molaotheo kapa Molao oa Palamente.

(2) Komeshene ea litoronkong le Litokiso ka tumelo ea Minista ea tsa litoronko e na le matla a ho bopa melao ea ketso tse seaneng.

(3) Ho lokisa Meputso (meholo) letsohle tse li loketse basebetse kapa ba litoronko, Komeshene ena e tsoanetse ho sebetsa e latela tumelo ea Mookameli eneiloeng ka taelo ea Minista a lebeletseng ka tsa lichelete moraho ha lipuisano le minista a lebeletseng ka tsebeletso ea litoronko lekhalemo. KHAOLO 12 Litsamaiso Tse Ikhethileng Li Tsehetsa Temokrasi KAROLO 1 Tse Li Akaretsang 232 Komeshene li ekemetseng LiKomeshene tse ekemetseng—

(a)

Komeshene ea tsa likhetho;

(b) Komeshene ea tsa malokelo a batho;

(c)

Komesheneea tsa le tsehali le bonna;

(d) Komesheneea tsa litaba; le

(e)

Komesheneea tsa tokoloho le ho tsoarelana.

233 Sepheo sa LiKomeshene li ekemetseng Komeshene tse ekemetseng lina letetello tse latelang holimo ha tseo li neiloeng—

(a)

ho tiisa le ho tsoeletsa litokelo tsa batho le boipuso;

(b) ho tsireletsa tokoloho le takatso tsa sechaba;

(c)

ho tsireletsa molaotheo;

(d) ho holisa bonnete le ho sebetsa hantle tulong tsa hurumente;

(e)

ho boloka le ho khutlisetsa mananeho lemaemo a lefatse le litulo tsohle tsa hurumente; le

(f)

ho hlokomela hore bosenye kapa bobolu le meferefere eohle bo ea thibeloa.

234 Basebetse baKomeshene bae kemetseng LiKomeshene li ekemetseng linale matla aho etsa lisebeletsi ho lateloa molao ho laola tsela ea bona eaho sebetsa. 235 Boipuso ba liKomeshene

(1) LiKomeshene li ekemetseng—

(a)

li ea e kemela ebile ha li epehe tlase ha tataiso kapa tlase ha taolo ea mang kapa mang;

(b) litsoanetse ho sebetsa lilatela molaotheo oa lefatse;

(c)

litsoanetse ho etsa mesebetse ea tsona li sena letsoalo, khetho kapa hatheletso le mo litsoanetse ho araba Palamenteng ka ho sebetsa thata mosebetseng oa tsona;

(2) Lefatse letulo tsohle tsa lona le likala tsa hurumente le mananehong ohle ho latela le tsoanetseng ho thusa liKomeshene li ikemetseng leho tsireletsa boipuso ba tsona, ho setsoare lehlakore, bohlokoa leho sebetsa ha tsona—

(3) Ha hona motho a tsoanetseng ho ikemela mosebetseng kapa tsela Komeshene li ikemetseng lisebetsang ka eona. 236 Maloko a liKomeshene li ikemetseng e seng tsa lipolitiki

(1) Maloko a Komeshene tse ekemetseng ha tsoanele ho etseng mosebetseng ea tsona—

(a)

ho etsa ka tsela e bontsang lehlakure ho tsa lipolitiki;

(b) hotsoeletsa li takatso tsa lehlakore ho tsa lipolitiki;

(c)

hohateletsa li takatso tsa tsa le tlakore la tsa lipolitiki leho etsa kapa;le

(d) ho ferekanya litokelo le litokoloho tsa motho e ka ba mang.

(2) Batho ba leng maloko a tsa lipolitiki kapa e mingoe mekopano ka nako bakhethoa ho ba maloko a Komeshenetse ekemetseng ba tsoanetse ho siea ho ba leloko tsa lipolitiki ba sa senye nako hare ha matsatsi a mashome a mararo.

(3) Ha leloko la Komeshenee ikemetseng—

(a)

a ba leloko la tsa lipolitiki kapa mengoe mekopano kapa;

(b) a ne a le leloko la tsa lipolitiki ka nako a khethoa ho kena ho Komeshene, a e so hoba leloka la tsa lipolitiki hare ha nako e beiloeng.

237 Ho khethoa le ho tlosoa mosebetseng ha maloko aKomeshene tse e kemetsing

(1) Pakeng la ho khethoa batho balokelang ho kena Komeshene e ikemetseng ke komiti ea melao e beiloeng le tsoanelo itsoanela—

(a)

ho hlahisa ka setulo se se neoang;

(b) ho koka kapa ho hlopha sechaba hore se khethe batho ba ka lulang setulong sena;

(c)

ho etsa hlopho ea hlahlobo tsa batho ba lakatsang ho kena kapa ho lula setulo sena;

(d) ho ngola palo ea batho ba lebeletsoeng ho khetho ea;

(e)

ho fihlisa pampiri e nale mabitso ho Mookameli.

(2) Motho a leng leloko la Komeshene e ikemetseng a ka tlosoa setulong fela ha motho ona—

(a)

a sena tsebo ea ho etsa mosebetse oa hae setulong, ka mabaka a bohole meleng kapa khopolong;

(b) a sebetsa tse sa khotsofatseng;

(c)

a bile lemolato ka mabaka a bo senye kapa ;

(d) a ne a sa ko tsoanela ho khethoa hore a kene ho Komeshene ena.

(3) Tsela ea holelekoa ha ba hlahlobi setulong hotsoana leholelekoa ha leloka la Komeshene e ikemetseng. KAROLO 2 Komeshene eaKhetho eaZimbabwe 238 Kemiso le Pupo ea Komeshene tsa Likhetho Zimbabwe

(1) Ho na le Komeshene e bitsoang ka hore ke Komeshene ea tsa Likhetho Zimbabwe ebopeoang ke—

(a)

molula setulo a khethiloeng ke Mookameli moraho ha lipuisano le Komeshene ea tsa molao likhotleng le ba komiti ea bamelao e beiloeng le litsoanelo le;

(b) maloko a fihlang a robeli a khethiloeng ke Mookameli a tsoang mabitsong a sa fiteng leshome le metso e mebeli a neiloeng Mookameli a tsoa ho komiti ea molao le litsoanelo e beiloeng;

(2) Moluli setulo oa Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa liKhetho o tsoanetse hoba moahloli kapa motho akile aba moahloli, kapa motho ae thutileng aloketseng hore aka khetho ea hoba moahloli.

(3) Ha eba likhetho tsa moluli setulo oa Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa li Khetho lisa lumelelani le Komeshene ea lekhotla la molao, Mookameli o tsoanetse ho tsebisa komiti ea melao le li tsoanelo ebe eloeng hore e tsebe kapela;

(4) Maloko aKomeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa liKhetho atsoanetse hoba baahi ba Zimbabwe, bakhetho ea ka Batlatsi, tsebo lehosebetsa kathatha katsela ea ho etsa ha bona likaleng tsa lefats letse ekemetseng.

(5) Maloko a Komeshene ea tsa likhetho a khetheloa setulong sena ha nako e lekanang lilemo tse tsiletsing hape ba ka khetho ea ha sebaka sesengoe setulong

setsoanang, kapa asebetsa Komesheneng moraho ha hoba leloko moraho ha selemo sesengoe fela kapa hofeta hofihlela lilemo tse leshome le metso e meli. 239 Mesebetse ea Komeshene ea liKhetho ea Zimbabwe Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa liKhetho enale mesebetse ena—

(a)

Ho lokisetsa tsamaeso leho etella—

(i) likhetho ofising ea Mookameli le Palamente;

(ii) li khetho tsa li profense le li toropong tse holo le likala tse etellang li khansili tsa mekhatlo ea sechaba;

(iii) likhetho tsa maloko a khansili ea marena e hlahisoang timaneng 285 le tsa pupo ea molao;

(iv) le hohlokomela hore likhetho le tsa pupo ea molao li tsamaesoa hantle, ka tokoloho, ka ho lekana ho sena ho tseetsana leka thato ea molao.

(b) ho laola li khetho tsa Mookameli oa Seneta le tsa Sebuoi sa Palamente leho tsoara mokopano oa hore li khetho tsena li etsoe hantle mabapi le molao;

(c)

ho ngolisa ba loketseng ho khetha;

(d) ho ngola boka e na le mabitso a batho ba loketseng ho khethoa;

(e)

ho kopa poloko, lesetsoaro sa li boka tse linale mabitso abatho baloketseng ho khetho ea;

(f)

ho nea li khaolo tsa mekhatlo ea litulo tsa ho etsa lihlopha ka tsela e tsoanetseng;

(g) ho etsa ho ngola leho tsoeletsa lipampiri tsa ho sebelisa ho khetha, netefatsa le horeka lisebelisoa tsa li khetho le ho supa tulo tsa ho khethela;

(h) ho tsamaisa leho etella lithuto tsa li khetho;

(i)

ho nea matla ho ba hlokomelo ho tsa likhetho letsa lipupo tsa melao;

(j)

ho nea khuthatso ho lisebeletsi tsohle tsa lifatse le mekhatlo ea litulo, ho etsetsa hore hobe leho sebetsa thata ,ho nale tokoloho ka ho lekana ka nako ea tsa lekhetho le tsa lipupo tsa melao; le

(k) ho amohela le ho hlokomela lingongoreho tsa sechaba. 240 Ho tlosoa setulong ha leloko la Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa li Khetho Batho ba latelang, ba khethiloeng ho karolo 320(2) a ba tsoanela ho khethoa hore ba be lisebeletsi tsa Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa li Khetho—

(a)

lisebeletsi tsa sechaba, kantleha baahloli;

(b) lisebeletsi tsa likhansele tsa liprofense le litoropotse holo le tsa mekhatlo; le

(c)

maloko le lisebeletsi tsa hurumente letse laoloangke hurumente.

241 Komeshene ea Zimbabwe e tla tsebisa ka tsa likhetho le referendum Holimo ha litsoanelo tsa karolo 323, Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea tsa liKhetho e tsoanetsi ho sebelisana le Minista a tsoanetseng ho tsamaisa melaetsa ho Palamente ka tsamaiso ea tsa likhetho le ka tsa melao.

KAROLO 3 Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea Li TokeLo Tsa Batho 242 Kemiso le pupo ea Komeshene ea litokelo tsa batho ea Zimbabwe

(1) Ho na le Komeshene etla tsibeang ka hore ke Komeshene ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwee bopeoang ke—

(a)

moluli setulo ea a khethiloeng ke Mookameli moraho ha li puisano le Komeshene ea Tsebeletso ea Molao liKomiti ea melao e beiloeng le litsoanelo; le

(b) maloko a fehlang robeli a khethiloeng ke Mookameli a tsoang mabitso a safiting leshome le peli a neiloeng Mookameli a tsoang ho Komiti ea molao e beiloeng le litsoanelo.

(2) Moluli setulo oa Komeshene ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe o tsoanetse hoba motho atletseng ka thuto hape letsebo setulong sena hanako eka fehlang li lemo tsesupa ale mosebetseng olebelelang ka melao mona Zimbabwe.

(3) Ha ho khethoa moluli setulo ho Komeshene ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe, Mookameli o tsoanetse ho tsebesa le Komiti ea melao e beiloeng hore e tsebe kapila.

(4) Maloko a Komeshene ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe, a tsoanetse ho khethoa ho lateloa bokhone le tsebo lekutloisiso letsebo ea mesebetse ho tsoeletso ea malokelo a batho. 243 Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea Litokelo Tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe

(1) Ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe ena le mesebetse e latelang—

(a)

ho holisa hlokomelo le hlompho ea malokelo a batho le tukelomafapeng otle asechaba;

(b) ho holisa tsireletso tsoeletso le tiiso ea malokelo a batho le litukelo;

(c)

ho laola le ho batlisisa leho hlokomela hore malokelo a batho a ea lateloa le li tokelo tsa bona;

(d) ho amohela le ho hlokomela lingongoreho li tsoang bathong le hore mo hlala oa tsona o lateloe ho be teng ho lokisoa hantle;

(e)

ho tsireletsa sechaba hateletsong ho tsoa ho ba e telelile ba busi ba litulo tsa hurumente leba lula setulo;

(f)

ho batlisisa ka mesebetse e na le k hatellelo e kaba etsoang ba tsoareng ba litulo le baeteleli ka litokelo a bona hot soeletsa tharollo ea litokelo;

(g) ho batla tsela e bonolo ea ho bahisana e akaretsang ho nea matla mapolesa hore mosenye o tsoarang ka ho ferekanya malukelo le litukelo tsa batho;

(h) ho nea molaoli o moholo oa Komeshene hore a batlisise ka bosenye ba ho ferekanya malokelo abatho le li tokelo tsa bona lehotsebisa Komeshene ea malokelo abatho ka tse lifumanehileng patlisisong ena;

(i)

ho nea Palamente mano aneiloeng hore malokelo abatho atsoeletsoe;

(j)

ho etsa patlisiso litabeng tsa litokelole takatso letokolohoea sechaba;le

(k) ha e tella kapa ho etella ea hlolisisang;

(i) litoronko tsa ba emetseng tseko, batsabi, balintoa letselingoe tse tsoanag le;

(ii) tulo tsa ho boloka bafrerekaneng lebaholofetseng lihopolo, hore babe leneti ea setsoaro lepolokeho lehore ba nee kopoho minista ea lebeletseng taoloo ea tulo tseo.

(2) Molaoli oMoholo oa mapolesa o tsoanetsei ho etsa joale ka a neoake Komeshene ea litokelo abatho holateloa karoloana (1)(h). 244 Tlaleho/Lirepoto tsa Komeshene ea Litokelo Tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe le ea eona

(1) Komeshene ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe e batla hore motho e kaba mang tulong ea hurumente kapa a hiriloeng ke lefatse kapa joang hore a—

(a)

tsebise Komeshene ka mabaka a beiloeng ho tsoeletsa litokelo tsa batho le litokelo li neiloeng thalathalong ea malokelo le ;

(b) ho nea Komeshene tsebo e batlehang ho lokisa leho nea likala tse mehato e mingoe mafatse ka litokelo tsa batho le kutloano le litumeleloano tse akaretsang le Zimbabwe.

(2) Holimo ha tsebiso tse tsoanelang ho litsamaiso ho ea ka karolo 323 Komeshene ea Litokelo tsa Botho ea Zimbabwe, hosebelisa Minista atsoanetsing ho ka tsamaesa li tsebiso tsa eona Palamente e nea taba tseseaneng tsa malokelo abatho le litokelotsetsoanetseng ho neoa peleha Palamente holateloa monahano oa Komeshene. KAROLO 4 Komeshene ea Tsa Bong ea ZimBaBwe 245 Ho theho ea le ho hlophisoa ha Komeshene ea tsa Tekana ea banna le basali Zimbabwe

(1) ho na le Komeshene e tsebeho eang ka Komeshene ea tsa Bong ea Zimbabwe e bopeoa ke—

(a)

moluli setulo ea khethiloeng ke Mookameli moraho ha li puisano le ba komiti ea melao e beiloeng le litsoanelo; le

(b) maloko a fihlang a robeli hore;

(i) ba bosupa ba bona ba khethiloe ke Mookameli batsoa mabitsong ba neiloeng ke ba komiti ba melao e beiloeng le litsoanelo ba safeteng ba leshome le metso emebeli le;

(ii) e mong fela a le neiloeng a tsoang ho khansili ea marena a khethiloeng ke Mookameli.

(2) Maloko a Komeshene ea litsehali le bonna ea Zimbabwe a tsoanetse ho khethoa ho lateloa thuto le tsebo le kutloisiso tabeng ea tsa botsehali le bonna, setso, khumo, letsa lipolitiki li tsoanetse hore li neoe sebaka lekemelo e lekaneng ho Komeshene ea tsona. 246 Mosebetsi oa Komeshene ea tekano ea Zimbabwe Komeshene ea Tekano ea banna le basali Zimbabwe le bona ena le mesebetse e latelang—

(a)

ho hlokomela likhang tsa ka tekano ea basali le banna le ho bona hore tsena li ea lateloa ka tsela e supiloeng molaong oa Palamente;

(b) ho batlisisa ka hatheletso tse kaba teng ho tekano ea banna le basali;

(c)

2013

ho amohela leho tiisa li ngongoreho litsoang sechabeng leho tsoaea phoso ho bao ba ferekanyang batho;

(d) ho batlisisa ka li khang tse li tsamaelanang le tekano ea banna le basali le tokoloho ea sechaba leho laola mesbetsing tse bakelang hatello le khethullo ho basali le banna;

(e)

ho tsebisa sechaba ka tulo tse e kemetseng ka mabaka a tsoanetseng ho latelloa hore ho be le hlompho hare ha basali le banna;

(f)

ho netefatsa tsamaeso e tsoeletsang litaba ka tsa tekano ea basali le banna;

(g) honetefatsa hore ba tsoeletsang hatellole khethullo ea banna le basali batlisoe pele ha molao;

(h) ho netefatsa puisano mong hona le hoferekana le khatheletso ea basali le banna; le

(i)

ho etsa tsohle tse tsoanetseng ho tsoeletsa tekano ea basali le banna.

247 Litlaleho tsa Komeshene ea Zimbabwe ea Tekano Holimo ha li tsebiso e tsoanelang ho li latela ho lateloa karolo 323, Komeshene ea tsa botsehali le bonna ea Zimbabwe, ho sebelisoa minista ea totoleng, eka tsamaesa li tsebiso tsa eona Palamentee e nea taba tse sieaneng tsa botsehali le bonna tse tsoanetseng ho neoa pele ha Palamente ho lateloa monahano oa Komeshene. KAROLO 5 Komeshene ea ho phatlalatsa Li TaBa Zimbabwe 248 Ho hlopha le ho bopa Komeshene ea ho phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe

(1) Hona le Komeshene ea ho Phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe e bopeoang ke—

(a)

moluli setulo a khethiloe ke Mookameli, moraho ha puisano le ba komiti ea melao le litsoanelo le;

(b) maloko a fihlang a robeli a khethiloeng ke Mookameli a tsoang mabitsong a sa feteng a leshome le metso emebeli a neiloeng ke ba tsa komiti ea ba tsa melao e beiloeng.

(2) Maloko a Komeshene ea ba tsa litaba a tsoanetsi ho khethoa ho lateloa thuto le tsebo le ho e neela ha bona tsamaisong ea mosebetse ea bona le kutloisiso ho tsa malokelo a batho le liketso tse ntle ho tsa litaba. 249 Mesebetse ea Komeshene ea ho Phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe

(1) Komeshene ea ho Phatlalatsa li Taba ea Zimbabwe e na le mesebetse e latelang—

(a)

ho emeletsa, ho holisa le ho tsoeletsa tokoloho ea ho hlahisa litaba;

(b) ho netefatsa ketso tsentle le hlompho ho hlahiseng litaba;

(c)

ho netefatsa, ho hlahisoa ha litaba ka tsela e thakhatsang sechabeng lehore batho bohle bafumane litaba ka ho lekana le hore ba lokolohe ho nea menahano ea bona e akaretsang sechaba sohle sa Zimbabwe;

(d) ho khuthaletsa hore hobe le litsela tse sieaneng tsa ho bolela le basebeletse ba litabeng le hore mong li sa fumaneheng teng li bo; teng

(e)

ho amohela le ho lokisisa lingongoreho tse litsoang sechabeng ka tsoanelo le ho tsoaea phoso kaba batlisisi ba litaba le bangoe ba teng lisebeletsi tsa litaba le ho hlahisa ba fumanehang batlotse molao ofe molao kapa ekaba efe tsela ba eneiloeng ea ho etsa mosebetsi oa bona;

(f)

ho bona hore bohle batho ba Zimbabwe bafumana litaba ka tsoanelo;

(g) ho khuthatsa ho sebelisoa leho tsoeletsoa ha lipuo tsohle tse li boteng lefatseng;

(h) ho khuthatsa kamohelo ea tsela tsa joale tsa ho sebelisa mechini ho hlahiseng litaba le tlarullo ea tsona;

(i)

ho holisa bapisano e lekaneng lefetoho hlahisong ea litaba;

(j)

ho etsa patlisiso ka litaba litsamaelanang letokoloho ea litaba tsa li koranta le ka ho bua le ka mokhoa o joalo ba khone ho tsoeletsa fetoho tse sieaneng tsa molao.

(2) Molao oa Palamente o ka nea matla ho Komeshene ea litaba hore—

(a)

e etse patlisiso ka—

(i) ketso le litsela tse supang bokotse ho tokelo ho hlahisoa ea litaba le;

(ii) boitsoaro ba tsa litaba le;

(b) ho nea tumelo hore bahlahisi ba litaba ba na le liketso tse li tsoanelang tlase ha letsoho la molao ba sekisoe ka ho tlola molao.

(3) Molao oa Palamente o ka nea hape melao ea ho hlahisoa ha litaba. 250 Litlaleho tsa Komeshene ea ho phatlalatsa litaba ea Zimbabwe Holimo ha li tsebiso e tsoanelang ho li tsamaisa ho lateloa karolo 323, Komeshene ea litaba tsa Zimbabwe, ho sebelisoa Minista atotileng ikatsamaesa litsebeso tsa eona Palamente inea taba tse seaneng tsa hlahiso ea litaba tse tsoanetseng ho tsebisoa Palamente ho lateloa monahano oa Komeshene. KAROLO 6 Komeshene ea Sechaba ea Khotso Leho Tsoarelana 251 Ho hlopha le ho bopa Komeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana Mmuso

(1) Ha nako e lekana lilemo tse leshome ho tsoa letsatsi lapele ho nale Komeshene tse tsebeang hore ke Komeshene ea khotso leho tsoarelana e bopeoa ke—

(a)

Moluli setulo ea khethiloeng ke Mookameli moraho ha puisano le Komeshene ka tsa melao lekhotleng le ba komiti e aba molao ebeiloeng le li tsoanelo le;

(b) Maloko afetlang robeli akhethiloeng ke Mookameli atsong mabitsong asafihleng leshome le metso emeli aneiloeng Mookameli atsoang ho komiti ea melaole litsoanelo.

(2) Moluli setulo oa Komeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana o tsoanetse ho ba motho eo a e thutileng ho ea nako e kafihlang lilemo tse supileng hore asebetse a le motsamaise oa tsa melao mona lefatseng la Zimbabwe.

(3) Ha e ba likhetho tsa moluli setulo oa Komeshene ea tsa khotso leho tsoarelana ea lefatse a sena tsoanelo ka tumelo ea Komeshene ea molao oa Lekhotla, Mookameli o tla tsebisa komiti ea molao le li tsoanelo ka potlako.

(4) Maloko a tsa Komeshene ea khotso leho tsoarelana ea Zimbabwe a tsoanetse ho khethoa ho lateloa Bathusi le tsebo le kutloisiso e tiileng tsireletso ea likhakabana, hokopanya baele ba khopisana leho kupa hape khotso. 252 Mesebetse ea Komeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana Komeshene ea tsa khotso letsa ho tsoarelana ea lefatse e na le mesebetse e latelang—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

(i)

(j)

ho bona hore hona le ho tsoarelana moraho ha lihlopisano, ho folisana le tokoloho; ho tsoeletsa leho tsamaisa mananeo a loketseng ho holisa pholiso, tsoarano leboiketlo ba ho amohela holokisetsa tselastsa ho fetsa likhang; ho tlisa kutloano leho tsoarelana kaho khothatsa batho hore babue nnete katsefetelengle ho hlahisa tukiso tsa tse hlokang holokisoa hore hone lethabo; ho tsoeletsa litsela le litulo tse li tla sebelisoang hore hobe le ho buisana hare ha tsa lipolitiki, lichaba le lingoe lihlopha tse ikemetseng ho thibela li ntoa le likhang tse li ka hlahang ka nako e latelang; ho tsoeletsa tsela tse seaneng ea hore bohle ba ileng ba kena bohlokong bona le tsutsumelitsoeng ka tsa lipolitiki; ho amohela leho etsa tse tsoanetseng ka lingongoreho li tsoang bathong; ho lokisa tsela tse tla pakisang ho tlokomela tulo tsenale batho bakhopisanang leho etsa ka hohle ho qeta likhopisano; ho etsa ka hohle ho thibela lintoa le ho ho thaletsa khotso; ho tharolla liphapaneo hare ha sechaba kapa lihlophenga ho mang kapa mang; le ho nea matla molaong honetefatsa hore thuso engoe le engoe efeoa bao ba lemetseng.

253 Litlaleho tsa KomesheneKomeshene ea tsa khotso le ho tsoarelana Mmuso Ho e tsoa holimo ha molao oneioang ke karolo 323 oa ho romela libapatso tsa mesebetse ea Komeshene ea tsa khotso letsa tsoarelano ea tsa lefatse. KHAOLO 13 Mekhatlo e Loang Le Bobolu Le tlolo ea Molao KAROLO 1 Komeshene Etlosang Taba Tsa Bobolu Le tlolo ea Molao ea Zimbabwe 254 Pupo le kemiso ea Komeshene e elebetseng ka tsa bobolu le tlolo ea molao

(1) Ho na le Komeshene etla tsebeang ka hore ke Komeshene e lebeletseng katsa boferefere ea Zimbabwe e bupioang ke—

(a)

moluli setulo a khethiloeng ke Mookameli moraho ha lipuisano le ba komiti ea melao eneiloeng ne litotiso le;

(b) mangoe maloko arobong akhetho eang ke Mookameli atsoang mabotsong a asafeteng lesumi lepeli aleng mabitso aneiloeng Mookameli atsoa hoba komiti ea melao eneiloeng lelitotiso

(2) Maloko a komiti elebeletseng meferefere a khetho ea ho lebeletsoe bohlale letsebo ea bona le nako banaleng eona ka tsamaeso ea mosebetse kapa ka ho sekoa ha melato kapa hobatlisisa kaketso empi kapa ho tsoaneloa hokhethelo setulong le—

(a)

hore motho ona o tsoanetse hoba a ithutile hape a tsoanela ho sebetsa mona Zimbabwe a le kaleng ea tsa molao kapa a be motho o na letsebo e tletseng ea mosebetse hana ko e kaba lilemo tse supang;

(b) hore motho ona o tsoanetsei hoba ae thutileng ahona ho sebetsa ale beletse tsa li chelate tsa sechaba kapa a hlolisisa tsela lechelete letselingoe lisebelisoang ka eona mona Zimbabwe, abe motho hape eo abileng letsebo ena sebaka sa lielemo tse supa le;

(c)

hore motho ona o tsoanetsei hoba le lilemo tse lesumi tsa tsebo ea ho batlisisa katsa bo senye kapa molao.

255 Mesebetsi ea Komeshene ea Zimbabwe loanang le ho satsepheha

(1) Komeshene ea Zimbabwe elebeletseng ka tsa boferefere enale mosebetse ona—

(a)

ho batlisisa le ho tsoeletsa phatlalatsa tsohle litaba tsa boferefere hare hase chaba lesepiring;

(b) ho thibela boferefere, bopatsoa, hosebelisoa hampe, hohateletsa bangoe basebetsang tlase ha hao lelingoe tsela tsempi hare ha se chaba lesepiring mesebetseng;

(c)

ho tsoeletsa hotsepeha, boeketlo, holichelete lehosebetsa phatlalatsa mesebetseng ea sechaba lesepering;

(d) ho amohela leho hlokomela lingongoreho litsoang bathong le ho etsa tsela e tsoanetseng hore hobe letotiso ka litsoanelo;

(e)

ho nea matla ho moholo oa mapolesa a ho batlisisa liketso tsa boferefere le honea li tsebiso ho Komeshene ka patlisiso ea bona;

(f)

ho fitisetsa litaba pele ha kala e lebeletseng ka tsekiso le kahlolo ho bao ba fumanehang ba le molato;

(g) ho kupa thuso ho maloko a tsireletso ea mapolesa le lingoe likala tsa patlisiso tsa lefatse; le

(h) ho nea taelo kapa matla ho hurumente le bangoe batho ka mabaka a ka tsoang ho tsoeletsa bathusi lemaikarabelo leho thibela tsela tse seaneng holoha mesebetseng ea sechaba le eleng sephereng.

(2) O moholo oa mapolesa o tsoanetse ho etsa joale ka ha a laeloa ke Komeshene e lebeletseng ka boferefere tlase ha karolo (1).

(3) Hurumente e tsoanetse ho bona, a sebelisa likala tsa ba molao le ka lingoe litsela, hore Komeshene e lebeletseng ka boferefere Zimbabwe e na le matla a ho laola hore mosenye a tsoaroe a sekisoe hape ho neoe katlolo molatong oa hae ho lateloa matla le melao e neiloeng ke Komeshene. 256 Kopo ea akaretsang Khaolo 12 ho Komeshene ea Zimbabwe loanang le hosatsepheha Karolo 234, 235, 236, 237 li ea sebetsa ho Komeshene e lebeletseng ka tsa boferefere Zimbabwe le maloko a eona ka hore e bonahala joale ka Komeshene e e kemetseng e qaliloeng ke khaolo 12.
